# Milestone 9.8: Model roles: one role table, models discovered from the CLIs

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (the user's standing choice) to implement this brief task by task, TDD. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

> Written 2026-10-07 against `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-model-roles-design.md` ("MR §n" below), which the user approved and which binds this milestone. Where MR disagrees with an earlier spec or brief on a matter it covers (how a model is chosen for any role, the Settings screen's model sections, the orchestrator's task routes), MR wins. Evidence is the code on `main` at `67e210d8` (protocol 18); every `file:line` below is on that commit and relative to `crates/` unless the path says otherwise. This milestone is built after PR #49 ("fix: Codex workers commit on Linux via permission profiles", branch `fix/codex-linux-git`) merges: it touches `decider/{argv,call,mod}.rs`, `scout/spec.rs`, `run/orch/launch.rs`, `run/role_launch.rs`, `headless/argv.rs` and `lifecycle/codex_version.rs`, and adds `headless::codex_sandbox::recorded_version()`, which task M9.8.6 uses. Task M9.8.1 re-checks every name below on the merged `main` first.

**Goal.** Every model anthrex launches is read from one role table (`[models]`, with per-repository overrides); the `C-b S` screen shows that table and a picker that lists what the user's installed Claude Code and Codex report, with their own names, descriptions and effort levels; a config written for anthrex ≤ 0.1.2 keeps choosing the same models.

**Architecture.** The role types live in `proto::models` (they cross the wire in the settings document); `config::models` parses, migrates and resolves them. A run freezes the resolved table (`RunLimits.models`) at its start, with each model's effort list from the catalog; every launcher reads it, and escalation steps along it. The daemon's new `models` module probes each CLI on `spawn_blocking` under a 10 s deadline, caches per CLI version, and answers `ClientMsg::ListModels`. The TUI's models screen and picker are pure state plus rendering.

**Tech stack.** Rust 2024 workspace; `serde`/`rmp-serde` wire types; `toml` for config; `ratatui`/`crossterm` for the TUI; `fake-agent` for every test.

**Spec.** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-model-roles-design.md`. Read it whole before task M9.8.2.

The user's design rules, which bind every decision here and are never reinterpreted:

- **Models are chosen per role** (MR D1): one table of rows; nothing else picks a model. The orchestrator sizes tasks and never picks models (D4).
- **anthrex never switches to a model the user did not choose** (D2): escalation raises effort, then switches to the row's "if it struggles" model, then stays.
- **The reviewer is never the exact model of the work's author** (D3); same runtime is allowed.
- **The picker lists what the installed CLIs report** (D8); no prompt is ever sent by a discovery probe.
- **Old configuration keeps working** (D9): migrated on load, rewritten only when the user saves.
- **Tests never reach a real agent.** Every fixture pins `ANTHREX_CLAUDE_BIN`, `ANTHREX_CODEX_BIN` and `ANTHREX_DECIDER_BIN` to `fake-agent` or a nonexistent path; no test changes program selection.
- **No new kill or signal code.** The probes reuse `lifecycle/codex_version.rs`'s `ProbeChild` (moved, unchanged) and `process::signal_group`; nothing in this milestone writes a `kill`, `signal_group` or `SIGKILL` call of its own.

## Goal

One role table decides every model: `orchestrator`, `planner`, `implementer.small|medium|hub`, `test_writer`, `reviewer`, `research`, `brainstorm` (two models) and `helpers` (with six per-kind overrides), global in `config.toml` and overridable per repository in `<data_dir>/repos/<basename>-<hash8>/models.toml`. Each row is a model, an effort and an optional "if it struggles" model. `C-b S` shows the table on one screen; `⏎` opens a picker fed by the installed CLIs' own model lists (Claude Code's `initialize`, Codex's `model/list`), cached per CLI version, with a minimal built-in list when neither works. Escalation climbs the model's discovered effort list, then switches to the fallback. The orchestrator only sizes tasks. Old keys are migrated on load (a golden test written first proves the same models are chosen) and removed when the user saves.

## Scope

In:

- `proto::models` (the role and catalog types), `proto::Effort` as a string, `ListModels`/`Models`, the settings document's role table and the repository scope's requests; protocol 19 (MR §3, §4.3).
- `config::models`: parse, render, resolve, the repository file, the built-in table, the migration of every old key (MR §3.3, §3.4, §6).
- `fake-agent`: both discovery handshakes from fixtures (MR §8).
- `daemon::models`: the two probes, the version probe, the cache, the `Cached` and `Builtin` fallbacks, `ListModels` (MR §4.1, §4.2).
- Every launcher reading the frozen table: workers by size, racer, test writer, reviewer (D3), research and scouts, planners, orchestrator, brainstorm, the document reviewer, helpers by kind; launch-time effort validation (MR §3.1, §4.4).
- Escalation along the effort list, then the fallback (MR §3.5).
- The orchestrator's and plan files' routes ignored and noted; contract rules 4, 21, 22 and the sub-planner's rule 7; `get_context`'s `roles` (MR §3.6).
- The TUI's models table, scopes, helper expansion, warnings and picker; the goal form and task edit form on the picker (MR §5).
- The save path: `[models]` written, old keys removed, `config.toml.bak`; `models.toml` for a repository (MR §5.1, §6.2).
- Removal of strength, the shipped catalog, the route lists, the roster pickers and the route refit (MR §6.3).
- End-to-end tests, CHANGELOG, README.

Out:

| Out | Owner |
|-----|-------|
| Runtimes other than Claude and Codex; per-run overrides beyond the goal form's orchestrator choice; per-task model choice by the orchestrator | Never (MR §2) |
| A `anthrex models` CLI subcommand | Not this milestone: `cli/src/main.rs` is at 596 lines and MR names none (decision 25) |
| Migrating a repository's applied route proposals (`tuning.toml [routes.<class>]`) | Never: the route refit is removed (MR §6.3); such a file loads and the table is ignored (decision 30) |
| Running the manual check (§8's real discovery) | The user, after the branch is pushed (Manual check) |

## Design decisions

Numbered and final. If one proves wrong or impossible, stop work on it, record the evidence under "Implementation notes", and continue with the tasks that do not depend on it. A decision marked **(R)** is a ruling on something MR leaves open or says inconsistently; each is also listed under "Spec and brief defects".

### Structure

1. **Where the code lives.**
   - `proto/src/models.rs` (new, re-exported at the crate root): `ModelRef`, `RoleChoice`, `BrainstormChoice`, `HelperKind`, `Role`, `ModelTable`, `ModelCatalog`, `CatalogModel`, `CatalogSource`. Tests in `proto/src/models_tests.rs`. **(R)** MR §3.2 shows `RoleChoice` without a crate; it lives in `proto` because the settings document carries the table over the wire, and `config::models` re-exports it.
   - `proto/src/effort.rs` (new): `Effort`, moved out of `run.rs` (591 lines) and re-exported from `run.rs`'s old path by `pub use crate::effort::Effort;`.
   - `config/src/models/` (new): `mod.rs` (re-exports, built-ins, `resolve`), `parse.rs` (read a `[models]` table, render one), `repo.rs` (the repository file, the only file here with I/O), `migrate.rs` (the old keys), `tests.rs`, `migrate_tests.rs`.
   - `config/src/settings/write_models.rs` (new): the `[models]` writer and the old-key remover; `write_models_tests.rs`.
   - `daemon/src/probe_child.rs` (new): `ProbeChild`, moved out of `lifecycle/codex_version.rs` without one changed line in its body or `Drop`, made `pub(crate)`.
   - `daemon/src/models/` (new): `mod.rs` (`ModelService`, `DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT`, `ProbeError`), `lines.rs` (a nonblocking line reader under a deadline), `version.rs`, `claude_probe.rs`, `codex_probe.rs`, `cache.rs`, `builtin.rs`, `tests.rs`, `tests_service.rs`.
   - `daemon/src/run/model_roles.rs` (new, pure): `RunModels`, `ModelEfforts`, the reviewer and racer rules; `run/model_roles_tests.rs`.
   - `daemon/src/run/role_step.rs` (new, pure): escalation; `run/role_step_tests.rs`.
   - `daemon/src/run/model_roles_golden.rs` (new, `#[cfg(test)]`) and `run/model_roles_golden.json` (checked in).
   - `daemon/src/run/driver/build_models.rs` (new, I/O): reads the repository file and the catalogs for a start, off the engine lock.
   - `fake-agent/src/discovery.rs` (new) and `fake-agent/fixtures/{claude-initialize.json,codex-model-list.json}`; `fake-agent/tests/discovery.rs`.
   - `tui/src/app/models_table.rs`, `app/model_picker.rs`, `app/models_keys.rs` (new, pure state and keys); `tui/src/ui/models_table.rs`, `ui/model_picker.rs` (new, pure rendering); tests beside each.
   - End-to-end: `cli/tests/models_discovery.rs`, `cli/tests/models_migration.rs`, `cli/tests/run_e2e_model_roles.rs`, `cli/tests/support/models.rs`.

   **Pure** (no `std::fs`, `std::process`, `std::net`, `std::thread`, `tokio`, `SystemTime`): `proto/src/models.rs`, `config/src/models/{mod,parse,migrate}.rs`, `daemon/src/run/{model_roles,role_step}.rs`, every new `tui` file. **I/O**: `config/src/models/repo.rs`, `daemon/src/models/**` but `builtin.rs`, `daemon/src/run/driver/build_models.rs`.
2. **Protocol 19** (header). New enum variants are appended **last**: `ClientMsg::ListModels`, `DaemonMsg::Models`, `SettingsRequest::{RepoModels, PutRepoModels}`, `SettingsReply::{RepoModels, RepoSaved}`. New struct fields are `#[serde(default)]` and left out while empty: `OrchestratorChoice.effort`, `SettingsDoc.roles`, `RunLimits.models`, `ModelCatalog.problem`. Every new message, variant and field gets a JSON and a MessagePack round-trip test, and every client changes in the same task (AGENTS.md rule 4): the TUI handles `Models`, the CLI client's exhaustive matches gain the arm, `anthrex hook` and `anthrex mcp` need nothing (they never see these messages; M9.8.4 checks).
3. **Words.** A **row** is one entry of the role table. A **role** names a row (`Role`), a helper kind included. The **global table** is `config::Orchestrator.roles`: the migrated old keys overlaid by the explicit `[models]` rows, built-ins *not* included. The **resolved table** of a run is `RunModels`: every role resolved (repository → global → built-in), frozen at start. The **catalog** is one runtime's discovered model list.

### Types and resolution (MR §3)

4. **`ModelRef`** prints and parses as `<runtime>:<id>`, with `<runtime>:default` for `id: None` (the CLI's default). `runtime` is `claude` or `codex`; `id` is 1 to 100 characters (`MODEL_ID_MAX_CHARS`, the MCP schema's old model bound), with no whitespace, no control character and no `safe_text` format character. It serialises as that string, so it is a map key in JSON and MessagePack.
5. **`Effort` is a string** (MR §3.2). `proto::Effort` becomes `#[serde(transparent)] struct Effort(Cow<'static, str>)` with consts `DEFAULT` (`""`: the model's default, no flag passed), `LOW`, `MEDIUM`, `HIGH`, `Effort::new(name)`, `as_str`, `is_default`. A protocol-18 value (`"low"`, `"medium"`, `"high"`) decodes unchanged, in JSON and MessagePack (rmp-serde writes a unit variant as its name, `encode.rs:675-679` of rmp-serde 1.3.1). It is `Ord` by rank (`DEFAULT` < `low` < `medium` < `high` < any other, others by name) so the comparisons left until M9.8.8 behave as today. `RoleChoice.effort` stays `Option<String>` as MR writes it; `None` becomes `Effort::DEFAULT` at launch. An effort name is 1 to 16 characters of `[a-z0-9_-]` (`EFFORT_MAX_CHARS = 16`), checked wherever one is read (config, the repository file, the settings document, the goal form's choice).
6. **`--effort` and `model_reasoning_effort`** are passed only for a non-default effort: `headless::argv::effort` and `decider::argv::effort` return `Option<&str>`, and every caller skips the flag on `None`. The value still goes through `toml_string` for Codex.
7. **Resolution** is `config::models::resolve(role, repo, global) -> RoleChoice` exactly as MR §3.4: repository row, else global row, else built-in; for `Role::Helper(kind)`: repository kind, global kind, repository `helpers`, global `helpers`, built-in. A row is overridden whole (model, effort and fallback together). `resolve_brainstorm(repo, global)` takes the repository's, else the global, else the built-in pair.
8. **Built-ins** are MR §6.1's table with one correction **(R)**: `research` is `claude:claude-haiku-4-5` at `low`, not `claude:claude-sonnet-5` at `medium`, because today's run scouts resolve to Haiku at `low` (`[orchestrator.scouts]` defaults `strength = fast`, `effort = low`, `config/src/orchestrator/adapt.rs:66-71`, through `scout::spec::run_scout_route`), and MR §6.1 says to correct its table where the golden test differs. `helpers` has no effort (MR's table); today passes `--effort low` to Haiku, which MR §8's golden test does not compare (decision 11).
9. **A run freezes `RunModels`** (`RunLimits.models: Option<RunModels>`) at its start, built by the driver (I/O, `driver/build_models.rs`) from the live global table, the repository file (read on `spawn_blocking`) and the catalogs in memory, and validated per MR §4.4 there (decision 21). Edits, rung 2, reviewers and racers of a running run use the run's copy, whatever the config says later (as 9.5's frozen lists did). A run recorded before this milestone (`models: None`) gets one at restore from the live table and the repository file, with the run-log line `models: this run predates the role table; using the current one` **(R)**; its tasks keep the routes they already have.
10. **Which row a task uses**: `RunModels::task_role(task)`: `TaskKind::Research` → `research`; `TaskKind::Review` → `reviewer`; a hub task → `implementer.hub`; size `S` → `implementer.small`; anything else (`M`, and an `L` rung 3 raised) → `implementer.medium`. A task's route is its row's model at its row's effort, unless the **user** set one with the task edit form (`EditSource::User`, MR §5.2), which is kept as given.

### Migration (MR §6)

11. **The golden test compares the model only** (MR §8: "asserts the same model for every role"), as `<runtime>:<id>` strings, over the matrix of M9.8.1. Effort differences are intended where MR's rows are coarser than today's (the reviewer's effort followed the review level; the helpers' `low`); each old key's effort mapping is pinned by M9.8.3's unit tests instead.
12. **The reviewer's golden cell is the medium review** **(R)**: today the reviewer's model depends on the review level (`roster::pick_reviewer`: `Small` → `fast`, `Medium` → the author's strength, `Frontier` → `frontier`); MR D3 has one reviewer row. The golden cell is an `M` task's reviewer (level `Medium`, the common case). A hub task's reviewer changes (today Opus by the same-runtime step when Codex has no frontier model; now the reviewer row, `codex:default`), which MR accepts by having one row.
13. **The racer is not in the golden test** **(R)**: MR §3.1 changes it on purpose (today the peer runtime's model at the task's strength, `validate_patterns::peer_route`; now the implementer row's fallback, else the same model).
14. **Migration is a pure port of today's resolution**, run once at load on the old keys, writing a row only where (a) one of the old keys that feeds it is present in the file, or (b) its model differs from the built-in's. It lives in `config/src/models/migrate.rs` with private copies of the roster helpers it needs (`first_at`, `lowest_at_or_above`, `strongest_of`, `route_within`, the reviewer and writer choices), because those leave the daemon in M9.8.13 and old configs must keep migrating. The golden test (run with the real daemon code in M9.8.1, against `config::models::resolve` from M9.8.3) is what proves the port. Feeds: `orchestrator` ← `[orchestrator.agent]`, `routes.orchestrator`; `planner` ← `planners.{runtime,strength,effort}`, `routes.planner`; `implementer.*` ← `routes.s|m|hub`, `default_runtime`; `reviewer` ← `routes.review`; `research` ← `scouts.{runtime,strength,effort}`, `routes.scout`; `helpers` ← `deciders.{strength,effort}`, `routes.decider` (a `mode` of `codex` reaches it through (b): `mode` stays a live key and never feeds a row by its presence alone, decision 17); `brainstorm` ← `routes.brainstorm`; `test_writer` ← none (derived from the medium row, written only by (b)).
15. **`test_writer` and `reviewer` are derived** **(R)** from the migrated `implementer.medium` as today's code derives them for an `M` task (the writer: the peer runtime's first roster model at the medium route's strength, else the medium route; the reviewer: the `review` list's first candidate on the other runtime at or above the author's strength, else `pick_reviewer` at `Medium`). MR §6.2 does not list this, but MR §1 ("keeps choosing the same models") and the golden test require it: with `default_runtime = "codex"` today's writer and reviewer are Claude Sonnet, not the built-in `codex:default`.
16. **`default_runtime` with no model of that runtime at a class's strength** **(R)**: today a plan's task of that class fails validation (`validate.rs:383-394`, "the roster has no codex model at frontier strength"). Migration keeps the row's built-in and adds the note `config: orchestrator.default_runtime = "codex" has no codex model at frontier strength in the roster; implementer.hub keeps claude:claude-opus-5-5`. The golden cell is `unresolved`, and M9.8.3's comparison expects the built-in there.
17. **`[orchestrator.deciders] mode`** **(R)** stays a live key: `off` turns the helpers off as today; `claude` or `codex` feeds the `helpers` row's runtime in migration (today's `DeciderContext::new` takes the runtime from it, `decider/mod.rs:273-278`). Saving never removes `mode`.
18. **Notes.** Each old key present in the file gives one note, logged once with `tracing::warn!` at daemon start beside the config problems (`lifecycle.rs:254-256`), and kept in `Orchestrator.roles_notes`. The text is exact: `config: <key> is replaced by <row>; it is migrated until you save in C-b S`, where `<key>` is `[orchestrator.routes.<name>]` or `[[orchestrator.models]]` for a table and the dotted path for a scalar (`orchestrator.agent.model`), and `<row>` is `[models.<row>]` (`[models.implementer.small]` …), or `the models your CLIs report (C-b S)` for `[[orchestrator.models]]` and `orchestrator.builtin_models`.
19. **Precedence** (MR §6.2): built-ins < migrated rows < explicit `[models]` rows < the repository file's rows. `Orchestrator.roles` holds the migrated rows overlaid by `[models]` (`ModelTable::overlaid`).

### Discovery (MR §4)

20. **Probes run lazily** **(R)**: never at daemon start. The first `ListModels`, and the first run start with no catalog in memory, start a refresh in the background (`ModelService::refresh_in_background`); a start never waits for it (MR §7: "a probe that fails or times out never blocks a launch"). At daemon start the catalogs on disk are loaded into memory on `spawn_blocking` (no process is spawned).
21. **Effort validation at launch** (MR §4.4) happens when a run freezes its table, against the `Live` and `Cached` catalogs only **(R)**: a `Builtin` list is a guess and validates nothing. A row's effort the catalog says its model does not offer becomes the model's `default_effort` (or `DEFAULT` when it has none) with one run-log line, exactly `effort 'xhigh' not offered by gpt-6-luna; using medium` (`using the model's default` when it has none). A model missing from the catalog launches as configured (MR §4.4).
22. **One deadline per runtime**: `DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT = 10 s` covers that runtime's version probe and its model probe together; the two runtimes run on two `spawn_blocking` closures at once. Each closure reserves `REAP_RESERVE = 100 ms` of its deadline for `ProbeChild`'s kill-and-reap, as `codex_version::probe` does (`read_deadline = deadline - 100 ms`), because `ProbeChild::drop` only reaps while `Instant::now() < deadline`.
23. **Version.** Codex's comes from `headless::codex_sandbox::recorded_version()` (PR #49) when the startup probe has recorded it, else from a `codex --version` run through the same bounded reader; Claude's from `claude --version`. Both parse with `launch::codex::parse_version` (the last whitespace token of the form `x.y.z`; `2.1.290 (Claude Code)` gives `2.1.290`). A version that does not parse is `unknown`, which never matches a cache.
24. **The catalog** carries one field MR §4.2 does not list **(R)**: `problem: Option<String>` (`codex not found`, `claude did not answer within 10 s`, `claude's reply had no models`), which the picker shows. A runtime whose CLI is missing is `Builtin` with `problem: Some("<runtime> not found")`.
25. **No CLI subcommand** (Scope); the CLI client only gains the exhaustive arm for `DaemonMsg::Models`. The end-to-end discovery test speaks the protocol directly (`cli/tests/support/models.rs`).
26. **Raw replies are kept** for the manual check: each live probe writes the reply lines it read (at most 256 KiB) to `<data_dir>/models/<runtime>-<version>.raw.jsonl` beside the cache, so the fixtures can be compared with the real CLIs' replies (MR §4.1's last bullet).

### Launchers and escalation (MR §3.1, §3.5)

27. **The reviewer** (D3): the reviewer row's model, unless it is exactly the author's (same runtime and same id, `default` equal only to `default`); then the row's fallback at the fallback's default effort; with no fallback (or a fallback equal to the author too), the row's model and one run-log line: `reviewer: <model> reviews work by the same model; set an "if it struggles" model for the reviewer in C-b S`. The document reviewer's author is the orchestrator. The review level still decides whether a small task is reviewed (`review_small`); it no longer chooses the model or effort.
28. **The racer** (MR §3.1): the second lane takes the task's row's fallback at its default effort when set, else the task's own route. **The test writer** takes the `test_writer` row. Both keep today's overlap rule for a lane on another runtime (FW-1, FW-5: `route_pick_step::open_roster`'s `Mover::Transient` skip): when the row's runtime differs from the task's and an unfinished task on that runtime overlaps the task's `owns`, the lane takes the task's own route instead. The predicate is extracted from `open_roster` into `run/model_roles.rs::runtime_open(run, i, runtime, mover)` before `open_roster` is deleted.
29. **Escalation** (MR §3.5) is `role_step::escalate(models, role, current) -> Option<Route>`: on the row's model, raise the effort one step along the model's discovered list (`DEFAULT` counts as the model's `default_effort`, else as below the first); at the top, or with no list, switch to the fallback at `DEFAULT`; on the fallback, raise along the fallback's list **(R: MR does not say what happens on the fallback)**; then `None`, and the ladder's later rungs (bounce cap, human block) apply as today. A route that failed in this task for an environment reason (ruling RL-1, `failed_routes`) is skipped: its step is the next one. `roster::escalate`, the peer-runtime step and the strength steps are removed.
30. **The route refit** goes: no route proposal is made, `Tuned.routes` and `ClassRoutes` are removed, a `tuning.toml` with `[routes.<class>]` loads with that table ignored, and `[orchestrator.tuning] escalate_above_percent` stays a known key whose value is read and ignored (one problem: `orchestrator.tuning.escalate_above_percent: no longer used; models come from the role table (using nothing)`).

### The orchestrator's routes (MR §3.6)

31. **Ignored, not refused.** An `add_task`, `split_task` or `amend_task` from the orchestrator or a sub-planner (`EditSource::{Orchestrator, Planner}`), and every task of a plan file (`run start --plan`), has its `route` cleared before resolution; when any task in the batch or plan named a runtime, model, strength or effort, the run log gets one line, exactly `route model ignored: models come from the role table`. A user's `amend_task` (`EditSource::User`: `anthrex run edit` and the task edit form) keeps its route (decision 10).
32. **The MCP schema** keeps `route` on `plan_task` and `plan_edit` so an older orchestrator's call is not rejected, as an open object of at most the four old keys with the description `Ignored: models come from the role table.`; `tools_bounds`' `Rule::Route` keeps checking it is such an object, values unchecked. `mcp/src/fixtures/orch_tools.json` is regenerated.
33. **Rule 9 stays** **(R)** (no two tasks on different runtimes with overlapping `owns`, MR silent): `planned_runtime` becomes the task's resolved route's runtime, which is its row's. The refusal names the cause: `overlaps task t1's owns (src/a.rs) and the two tasks run on different runtimes (codex, claude): the role table runs size S on codex and size M on claude; give them the same size or separate owns (rule 9)`. Contract rule 22 says so.
34. **`get_context`** loses `roster` and `limits.routes` and gains `roles`: `{"small": "codex:gpt-6-sol", "medium": "claude:claude-sonnet-5", "hub": "claude:claude-opus-5-5", "installed": {"claude": true, "codex": true}}`.

### TUI (MR §5)

35. **The Settings screen** keeps its frame and the `limits` section; its sections become `models` and `limits` (`tab` switches). The `claude`, `codex` and `orchestrator` sections, `ModelRow`, `CustomModel` and the shipped rows are deleted.
36. **Scopes.** `everywhere` edits `SettingsDoc.roles`; `this repo (<basename>)` edits the repository table of `App::goal_project()`'s project, fetched with `SettingsRequest::RepoModels` when the scope is first entered and saved with `PutRepoModels` **(R: MR names no wire message for it)**. With no project, the scope line shows `this repo (none)` dimmed and `‹`/`›` does not move to it.
37. **Catalogs** reach the TUI only as `DaemonMsg::Models` into `App.catalogs: Catalogs` (pure); opening the screen sends `ListModels { runtime: None, refresh: false }` when `App.catalogs` is empty, and `r` in the picker sends `refresh: true` (MR §5.3).
38. **Cleaning.** Every string from a catalog (`label`, `description`, an effort name) is drawn through `safe_text::one_line` and cut to its column; a model id typed under `custom…` is checked by `ModelRef::parse` before it is accepted.
39. **The goal form**: `GoalField::Runtime` and `GoalField::Model` become one `GoalField::Model` whose `⏎` opens the picker with a first entry `role table (<orchestrator row's label>)` (no choice), and a `GoalField::Effort` after it, cycling the chosen model's efforts and `default`; `OrchestratorChoice.effort` carries it. **The task edit form**: `EditField::{Runtime, Model, Strength}` become one `EditField::Model` (the picker, first entry `role table`), and `EditField::Effort` cycles the chosen model's catalog efforts.

### Save (MR §5.1, §6.2)

40. **`w` in `everywhere`** sends `SettingsRequest::Put` with `SettingsDoc.roles`; the daemon's save writes every row as `[models.<row>]` tables, removes every old key decision 18 lists (whole `[orchestrator.routes.*]` and `[[orchestrator.models]]` blocks; the scalar lines), and, when it removed at least one, first copies the file to `config.toml.bak` (replacing an older `.bak`) **(R: MR says "keeps the previous file"; once per save that removes old keys)**. The writer refuses a `models` key it cannot edit (an inline table, a dotted key) with `UNSUPPORTED_FORM`, and proves its output by reading it back: the new text's `Orchestrator.roles` equals the document's table and it has no `roles_notes`. The status line says `saved · new runs use these models · runs in progress keep theirs`.
41. **`w` in `this repo`** sends `PutRepoModels`; the daemon writes `<repo_dir>/models.toml` (`profile::repo_dir(data_dir, project)`, the profile's own directory) atomically (temp file and rename) on `spawn_blocking` within `SETTINGS_WRITE_TIMEOUT`; an empty table removes the file.
42. **Live.** A save swaps `roles` into `LiveSettings` (`config::settings::apply_owned` copies it); helpers read the live table per call (decision 9 freezes only runs).

## Interfaces

### `proto` (`proto/src/models.rs`, new)

```rust
//! Milestone 9.8 (MR §3, §4.2): the role table and the discovered model catalogs.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serialize, Serializer};
use crate::types::Runtime;

/// `ModelRef`'s `id`, at most this many characters (the MCP schema's old model bound).
pub const MODEL_ID_MAX_CHARS: usize = 100;
/// An effort name, at most this many characters of `[a-z0-9_-]`.
pub const EFFORT_MAX_CHARS: usize = 16;

/// A model as a row names it: `claude:<id>`, `codex:<id>`, or `<runtime>:default`
/// (`id: None`, whatever that CLI is configured to use).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub struct ModelRef {
    pub runtime: Runtime,
    pub id: Option<String>,
}

impl ModelRef {
    pub fn default_of(runtime: Runtime) -> ModelRef { ModelRef { runtime, id: None } }
    /// `<runtime>:<id>` or `<runtime>:default`.
    pub fn label(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}:{}", self.runtime.label(), self.id.as_deref().unwrap_or("default"))
    }
    /// The model as a route spells it: the id, or `""` for the CLI's default.
    pub fn route_model(&self) -> &str { self.id.as_deref().unwrap_or("") }
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Result<ModelRef, String> {
        let (runtime, id) = text
            .split_once(':')
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("{text:?} is not <runtime>:<model>"))?;
        let runtime = match runtime {
            "claude" => Runtime::Claude,
            "codex" => Runtime::Codex,
            other => return Err(format!("{other:?} is not claude or codex")),
        };
        if id == "default" {
            return Ok(ModelRef { runtime, id: None });
        }
        let bad = |c: char| c.is_whitespace() || c.is_control() || crate::safe_text::is_hidden_format(c);
        if id.is_empty() || id.chars().count() > MODEL_ID_MAX_CHARS || id.chars().any(bad) {
            return Err(format!("{id:?} is not a model name (1 to {MODEL_ID_MAX_CHARS} visible characters, no spaces)"));
        }
        Ok(ModelRef { runtime, id: Some(id.to_string()) })
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Display for ModelRef { /* writes self.label() */ }
impl Serialize for ModelRef { /* serializer.serialize_str(&self.label()) */ }
impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for ModelRef { /* String, then ModelRef::parse, mapping Err to D::Error::custom */ }

/// Whether `name` is an effort name (decision 5).
pub fn valid_effort(name: &str) -> bool {
    (1..=EFFORT_MAX_CHARS).contains(&name.len())
        && name.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_lowercase() || b.is_ascii_digit() || b == b'_' || b == b'-')
}

/// One row (MR §3.2).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RoleChoice {
    pub model: ModelRef,
    /// `None`: the model's default effort.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub effort: Option<String>,
    /// "If it struggles" (D2).
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub fallback: Option<ModelRef>,
}

/// The brainstorm row: two models and one effort.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BrainstormChoice {
    pub first: ModelRef,
    pub second: ModelRef,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub effort: Option<String>,
}

/// The six one-shot calls (MR D5), in `DeciderKind::ALL`'s order but `run_name` first.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, PartialOrd, Ord, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum HelperKind { RunName, Triage, SizeCheck, CheckSummary, BlockedReason, CiSummary }

impl HelperKind {
    pub const ALL: [HelperKind; 6] = [
        HelperKind::RunName, HelperKind::Triage, HelperKind::SizeCheck,
        HelperKind::CheckSummary, HelperKind::BlockedReason, HelperKind::CiSummary,
    ];
    /// `run_name`, `triage`, … (the TOML table and the `DeciderKind::label`).
    pub fn key(self) -> &'static str { /* one arm per kind */ }
    /// What the table shows: `run name`, `triage`, `size check`, `check summary`,
    /// `blocked reason`, `ci summary`.
    pub fn label(self) -> &'static str { /* one arm per kind */ }
}

/// A row's name. `Helper(kind)` is a helpers override.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, PartialOrd, Ord)]
pub enum Role {
    Orchestrator, Planner, ImplementerSmall, ImplementerMedium, ImplementerHub,
    TestWriter, Reviewer, Research, Helpers, Helper(HelperKind),
}

impl Role {
    /// The table's rows in MR §5.1's order (brainstorm is its own field).
    pub const ROWS: [Role; 9] = [
        Role::Orchestrator, Role::Planner, Role::ImplementerSmall, Role::ImplementerMedium,
        Role::ImplementerHub, Role::TestWriter, Role::Reviewer, Role::Research, Role::Helpers,
    ];
    /// `ROWS`, then every helper kind.
    pub fn all() -> impl Iterator<Item = Role> {
        Role::ROWS.into_iter().chain(HelperKind::ALL.into_iter().map(Role::Helper))
    }
    /// `orchestrator`, `implementer.small`, `test_writer`, `helpers.run_name`, …
    pub fn key(self) -> String {
        match self {
            Role::Orchestrator => "orchestrator".into(),
            Role::Planner => "planner".into(),
            Role::ImplementerSmall => "implementer.small".into(),
            Role::ImplementerMedium => "implementer.medium".into(),
            Role::ImplementerHub => "implementer.hub".into(),
            Role::TestWriter => "test_writer".into(),
            Role::Reviewer => "reviewer".into(),
            Role::Research => "research".into(),
            Role::Helpers => "helpers".into(),
            Role::Helper(kind) => format!("helpers.{}", kind.key()),
        }
    }
    pub fn parse_key(key: &str) -> Option<Role> { Role::all().find(|r| r.key() == key) }
    /// MR §5.1's names: `orchestrator`, `planner`, `implementer · small` (`· medium`,
    /// `· hub`), `test writer`, `reviewer`, `research`, `helpers`, and a kind's
    /// `HelperKind::label` (`run name`, …); the table adds `▸` and the indent.
    pub fn label(self) -> String { /* one arm per variant, exactly these texts */ }
}

impl Serialize for Role { /* serialize_str(&self.key()) */ }
impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for Role { /* String, then parse_key, else D::Error::custom */ }

/// One `[models]` table: only the rows it sets.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ModelTable {
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    pub rows: BTreeMap<Role, RoleChoice>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub brainstorm: Option<BrainstormChoice>,
}

impl ModelTable {
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool { self.rows.is_empty() && self.brainstorm.is_none() }
    /// `self` with every row `top` sets replaced by `top`'s (decision 19).
    pub fn overlaid(&self, top: &ModelTable) -> ModelTable {
        let mut out = self.clone();
        out.rows.extend(top.rows.iter().map(|(r, c)| (*r, c.clone())));
        if top.brainstorm.is_some() { out.brainstorm = top.brainstorm.clone(); }
        out
    }
}

/// Where a catalog came from (MR §4.2).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum CatalogSource { Live, Cached, Builtin }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CatalogModel {
    /// The id a route passes (`--model`), `default` for Claude's default entry.
    pub id: String,
    pub label: String,
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub efforts: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub default_effort: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub is_default: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ModelCatalog {
    pub runtime: Runtime,
    /// `2.1.290`, `0.160.1`, or `unknown`.
    pub cli_version: String,
    /// Seconds since the epoch.
    pub fetched_at: u64,
    pub source: CatalogSource,
    pub models: Vec<CatalogModel>,
    /// Decision 24: why the catalog is not live (`codex not found`, …).
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub problem: Option<String>,
}

impl ModelCatalog {
    /// The model `m` names, if the catalog lists it (`id: None` is the entry with
    /// `is_default`, else none).
    pub fn find(&self, m: &ModelRef) -> Option<&CatalogModel> {
        if m.runtime != self.runtime {
            return None;
        }
        match &m.id {
            Some(id) => self.models.iter().find(|c| &c.id == id),
            None => self.models.iter().find(|c| c.is_default),
        }
    }
}
```

`proto/src/effort.rs` (new; `run.rs` drops its `Effort` and `impl Effort` and re-exports this one):

```rust
use std::borrow::Cow;
use std::cmp::Ordering;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// How much reasoning a route asks for: the model's own effort name (`low` … `xhigh`,
/// `max`), or [`Effort::DEFAULT`] (empty) for the model's default, which passes no
/// flag. A string since milestone 9.8 (MR §3.2); a protocol-18 value decodes as is.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
#[serde(transparent)]
pub struct Effort(Cow<'static, str>);

impl Effort {
    pub const DEFAULT: Effort = Effort(Cow::Borrowed(""));
    pub const LOW: Effort = Effort(Cow::Borrowed("low"));
    pub const MEDIUM: Effort = Effort(Cow::Borrowed("medium"));
    pub const HIGH: Effort = Effort(Cow::Borrowed("high"));

    /// `name` as given; callers check it with `models::valid_effort` first.
    pub fn new(name: impl Into<String>) -> Effort { Effort(Cow::Owned(name.into())) }
    /// `Some(name)` → that effort; `None` → [`Effort::DEFAULT`].
    pub fn of(name: Option<&str>) -> Effort { name.map_or(Effort::DEFAULT, Effort::new) }
    pub fn as_str(&self) -> &str { &self.0 }
    pub fn is_default(&self) -> bool { self.0.is_empty() }
    /// The one step milestone 8a's ladder knew (`low` → `medium` → `high`); removed with
    /// its last caller in M9.8.8.
    pub fn raised(&self) -> Option<Effort> {
        match self.as_str() { "low" => Some(Effort::MEDIUM), "medium" => Some(Effort::HIGH), _ => None }
    }
    fn rank(&self) -> (u8, &str) {
        match self.as_str() {
            "" => (0, ""), "low" => (1, ""), "medium" => (2, ""), "high" => (3, ""),
            other => (4, other),
        }
    }
}

impl Ord for Effort { fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Ordering { self.rank().cmp(&other.rank()) } }
impl PartialOrd for Effort { fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<Ordering> { Some(self.cmp(other)) } }
impl std::fmt::Display for Effort { /* writes as_str(), or `default` when empty */ }
```

```rust
    RepoSaved { project: PathBuf, table: ModelTable },
```

`SettingsDoc.models: Vec<ModelEntry>` and `SettingsDoc.orchestrator` stay until M9.8.12 removes them (still protocol 19); `ModelEntry` goes in M9.8.14.

### `config` (`config/src/models/`)

```rust
// mod.rs
pub use proto::{BrainstormChoice, HelperKind, ModelRef, ModelTable, Role, RoleChoice};
/// The repository file's name, in the repository's data directory (MR §3.3).
pub const REPO_FILE: &str = "models.toml";
pub fn builtin_choice(role: Role) -> RoleChoice;          // MR §6.1, decision 8
pub fn builtin_brainstorm() -> BrainstormChoice;          // opus + codex:default, high
pub fn resolve(role: Role, repo: Option<&ModelTable>, global: &ModelTable) -> RoleChoice; // decision 7
pub fn resolve_brainstorm(repo: Option<&ModelTable>, global: &ModelTable) -> BrainstormChoice;
pub use parse::{parse_text, render};                      // a file's text ↔ a table
pub use repo::{load_repo, save_repo};
pub use migrate::migrate;
pub(crate) use parse::read_table;

// parse.rs
/// `[models]` (`value`, the top-level key's value) into a table; a bad row or key is a
/// `Problem` keyed `models.<row>.<key>` and is skipped; the rest is kept.
pub(crate) fn read_table(value: &toml::Value, problems: &mut Vec<Problem>) -> ModelTable;
/// A whole file's text (the repository file), read as `[models]`.
pub fn parse_text(text: &str) -> Result<(ModelTable, Vec<Problem>), String>;
/// `table` as `[models.<row>]` tables, rows in `Role::all()` order, then brainstorm.
pub fn render(table: &ModelTable) -> String;

// repo.rs (I/O)
/// `None` and no warning for a missing file; `None` and one warning for an unreadable
/// or unparseable one (MR §7); the table and one warning per bad row otherwise.
pub fn load_repo(path: &Path) -> (Option<ModelTable>, Vec<String>);
/// Writes `table` to `path` atomically (temp and rename); an empty table removes it.
pub fn save_repo(path: &Path, table: &ModelTable) -> Result<(), String>;

// migrate.rs
/// Decisions 14–18: the rows the old keys give and one note per old key present.
/// `raw` is the file's `[orchestrator]` table, `None` without one.
pub fn migrate(o: &Orchestrator, raw: Option<&toml::Table>) -> (ModelTable, Vec<String>);
```

`config::Orchestrator` gains, last: `pub roles: ModelTable` and `pub roles_notes: Vec<String>` (both `Default`). `config::parse` fills them after `orchestrator::read`: `let explicit = table.get("models").map(|v| models::read_table(v, &mut problems)).unwrap_or_default(); let (migrated, notes) = models::migrate(&config.orchestrator, table.get("orchestrator").and_then(|v| v.as_table())); config.orchestrator.roles = migrated.overlaid(&explicit); config.orchestrator.roles_notes = notes;`, and `report_unknown_keys` accepts `"models"` (its own unknown keys are `read_table`'s problems).

### `daemon`

```rust
// daemon/src/models/mod.rs
/// Decision 22: one runtime's version and model probes together.
pub const DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
/// Decision 22: kept from the deadline for `ProbeChild`'s kill-and-reap.
pub const REAP_RESERVE: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);
/// A reply line longer than this, or a reply past `REPLY_MAX_BYTES`, ends the probe.
pub const LINE_MAX_BYTES: usize = 1 << 20;
pub const REPLY_MAX_BYTES: usize = 4 << 20;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProbeError { Missing, Timeout, Cancelled, Failed(String) }

/// What a runtime's probes do; real ones are `CliProbes`, tests use their own.
pub trait Probes: Send + Sync + 'static {
    fn version(&self, runtime: Runtime, deadline: Instant) -> Result<String, ProbeError>;
    /// The models and the raw reply lines read.
    fn models(&self, runtime: Runtime, deadline: Instant) -> Result<(Vec<CatalogModel>, Vec<String>), ProbeError>;
}

pub struct CliProbes { pub claude_bin: String, pub codex_bin: String, pub cancel: CancellationToken }

pub struct ModelService { /* data_dir, probes: Arc<dyn Probes>, memory: Mutex<BTreeMap<Runtime, ModelCatalog>> (crate::lock), gates: [tokio::sync::Mutex<()>; 2] */ }

impl ModelService {
    pub fn new(data_dir: PathBuf, probes: Arc<dyn Probes>) -> Arc<ModelService>;
    /// Loads `<data_dir>/models/*.json` into memory on `spawn_blocking`; spawns nothing.
    pub async fn load_disk(&self);
    /// The catalogs in memory now, one per runtime that has one. Never probes.
    pub fn current(&self) -> Vec<ModelCatalog>;
    /// MR §4.2 for each runtime asked, the two at once, each single-flight.
    pub async fn list(self: &Arc<Self>, runtime: Option<Runtime>, refresh: bool) -> Vec<ModelCatalog>;
    /// Decision 20: `list(None, false)` on a spawned task when nothing is in memory.
    pub fn refresh_in_background(self: &Arc<Self>);
}

// daemon/src/run/model_roles.rs (pure)
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ModelEfforts {
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub efforts: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub default: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RunModels {
    /// Every role of `Role::all()`, resolved.
    pub rows: BTreeMap<Role, RoleChoice>,
    pub brainstorm: BrainstormChoice,
    /// The efforts the catalog reported for each row's model and fallback.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    pub efforts: BTreeMap<ModelRef, ModelEfforts>,
}

impl RunModels {
    pub fn resolve(global: &ModelTable, repo: Option<&ModelTable>) -> RunModels;
    /// Decision 21: fills `efforts` from the Live and Cached catalogs and replaces an
    /// effort the model does not offer; the run-log lines, one per replaced row.
    pub fn validate(&mut self, catalogs: &[ModelCatalog]) -> Vec<String>;
    pub fn choice(&self, role: Role) -> &RoleChoice;
    /// The row's model at its effort.
    pub fn route(&self, role: Role) -> Route;
    pub fn route_of(model: &ModelRef, effort: Option<&str>) -> Route;
    pub fn task_role(task: &Task) -> Role;                       // decision 10
    pub fn reviewer_route(&self, author: &Route) -> (Route, Option<String>); // decision 27
    pub fn racer_route(&self, role: Role, current: &Route) -> Route;          // decision 28
}

/// Decision 28: whether a lane on `runtime` may run beside task `i` (FW-1, FW-5): the
/// runtime is the task's own, or it is installed and no unfinished task on it overlaps
/// (`open_roster`'s `open`, `route_pick_step.rs:163-176`). `Mover` moves here from
/// `route_pick.rs:215` in M9.8.7a.
pub fn runtime_open(run: &Run, i: usize, runtime: Runtime, mover: Mover) -> bool;

// daemon/src/run/role_step.rs (pure)
/// Decision 29: the next route for `role` from `current`, skipping `failed`; `None`
/// when there is none.
pub fn escalate(models: &RunModels, role: Role, current: &Route, failed: &[Route]) -> Option<Route>;
```

`Route` keeps `strength` until M9.8.14; every route built from a row sets `strength: Strength::Standard` with the comment `// removed in M9.8.14; no rule reads it after M9.8.8`. `RunLimits` gains, last, `#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] pub models: Option<RunModels>`, and `RunLimits::models(&self) -> &RunModels` (panics with `the run's models are frozen at start and filled at restore` when `None`, which decision 9 makes unreachable). `BuildContext` gains `pub models: RunModels`. `RunService` gains `pub fn models(&self) -> Arc<ModelService>`.

A move-only commit's test count is recorded before and after it (they must be equal), per the review-failure patterns in memory.

## Tasks

Do them in this order. Each task's tests are written first and must fail before its change (AGENTS.md rule 6), except those marked **pinning** (they pin today's behaviour and pass on today's code by design). One commit per task; a move-only split is its own commit inside its task.

**Why this order** (the required shape, adjusted where the code argues): the TUI tasks (M9.8.10–11) and the save path (M9.8.12) come **before** the removals (M9.8.13–14), because today's Settings screen is built on `shipped.rs`, `ModelEntry` and the roster (`tui/src/app/settings_screen.rs:12-18`), so deleting them first would break the workspace. The launchers are two tasks (M9.8.7a tasks and their sessions, M9.8.7b the roles), and the removals two (M9.8.13 the pickers, M9.8.14 `Strength`), because each half alone is a reviewable change of 30+ files.

**Shared test rules.**

- Every test uses `fake-agent` only. Every fixture pins `ANTHREX_CLAUDE_BIN`, `ANTHREX_CODEX_BIN` and `ANTHREX_DECIDER_BIN` (`cli/tests/support/mod.rs::pin_agents`, `RunHarness`); daemon unit tests that spawn a CLI spawn a `/bin/sh` script written with `testexec::write_executable`. No test changes program selection, and no test signals a process it did not start: a probe test that checks a child is gone reads that child's pid from a file the child wrote, and only calls `libc::kill(pid, 0)`.
- Run tests with `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1` (CI has no git identity). During a task run only the touched tests; the full suite and the smoke run are the merge gate (Verification).
- **Timing.** Every new wall-clock bound names its derivation from the constants it waits on, per `docs/timing-budgets.md`'s standing rules, and gets a row there in the task that adds it. The bounds this brief adds: a probe past its deadline returns within `deadline + REAP_RESERVE + WALL_CLOCK_SLACK (2 s)`; a `Models` reply in an end-to-end test within `MODELS_WAIT = DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT (10 s) + HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT (5 s) + 5 s` (the two runtimes probe at once, so one `DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT`, not two); a run in an end-to-end test within the harness's existing `RUN_WAIT`. None may be a fixed sleep as the only synchronisation.
- Commands below use the package names: `anthrex-proto`, `anthrex-config`, `anthrex-daemon`, `anthrex-tui`, `anthrex-fake-agent`, `anthrex-mcp`, `anthrex` (the CLI and its `tests/`).

### M9.8.1 Golden test: what each role resolves to today

**Files.** Create `daemon/src/run/model_roles_golden.rs` and `daemon/src/run/model_roles_golden.json`; mount it in `daemon/src/run/mod.rs` as `#[cfg(test)] mod model_roles_golden;`. Update this brief's "Implementation notes" with the fact check below.

On `main` after PR #49 merged, record under "Implementation notes", each with the command and its output: `PROTO_VERSION` is 18 (else re-derive the header); every `file:line` and name in "Starting point" and "Interfaces" exists with that spelling and shape (each difference and the name used instead); `headless::codex_sandbox::recorded_version` exists with signature `fn() -> Option<(u64, u64, u64)>`; `wc -l` of every file in "File sizes". A name that moved is corrected in place in this brief.

**Tests first (pinning).** The test passes on today's code: it records today's resolution before anything changes (MR §8). Write it exactly:

```rust
//! Milestone 9.8 task 1 (MR §8): what each role resolves to with today's code, for a
//! matrix of old configs, pinned in `model_roles_golden.json` before anything changes.
//! Task M9.8.3 adds the comparison with `config::models::resolve`; task M9.8.13 deletes
//! `today` with the code it calls, and the JSON stays the record.

use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use proto::{DeciderMode, Route, Runtime};

use crate::run::model::Run;
use crate::run::model_tuning::FrozenList;
use crate::run::orch::test_support as orch_support;
use crate::run::orch::{launch, roles};
use crate::run::route_pick::{self, Installed};
use crate::run::test_support::{build_with, plan_with, show, task_toml};

/// A cell whose plan does not validate today (decision 16).
pub(crate) const UNRESOLVED: &str = "unresolved";

const GOLDEN: &str = include_str!("model_roles_golden.json");

const CODEX_ROWS: &str = r#"
[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6-luna"
strength = "fast"

[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6-sol"
strength = "standard"

[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6.1-sol"
strength = "frontier"
"#;

const ROUTES: &str = r#"
[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6-sol"
strength = "standard"

[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6.1-sol"
strength = "frontier"

[orchestrator.routes.s]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6-sol", effort = "low" }, { runtime = "claude", model = "claude-sonnet-5" }]

[orchestrator.routes.m]
candidates = [{ runtime = "claude", model = "claude-sonnet-5", effort = "medium" }, { runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6-sol" }]

[orchestrator.routes.hub]
candidates = [{ runtime = "claude", model = "claude-opus-5-5", effort = "high" }, { runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6.1-sol" }]

[orchestrator.routes.review]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6.1-sol", effort = "high" }, { runtime = "claude", model = "claude-opus-5-5" }]

[orchestrator.routes.scout]
candidates = [{ runtime = "claude", model = "claude-sonnet-5", effort = "medium" }]

[orchestrator.routes.decider]
candidates = [{ runtime = "claude", model = "claude-sonnet-5" }]

[orchestrator.routes.planner]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6.1-sol", effort = "high" }]

[orchestrator.routes.orchestrator]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6.1-sol", effort = "high" }, { runtime = "claude", model = "claude-opus-5-5" }]

[orchestrator.routes.brainstorm]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6-sol" }, { runtime = "claude", model = "claude-sonnet-5" }]
"#;

/// The matrix (MR §8): each name and its `config.toml`. Built at test time so the
/// roster rows are written once.
pub(crate) fn matrix() -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
    vec![
        ("default", String::new()),
        ("default_runtime_codex", "[orchestrator]\ndefault_runtime = \"codex\"\n".into()),
        ("routes", ROUTES.into()),
        ("agent", "[orchestrator.agent]\nruntime = \"claude\"\nmodel = \"claude-sonnet-5\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n".into()),
        ("planners", "[orchestrator.planners]\nruntime = \"codex\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n".into()),
        ("scouts", "[orchestrator.scouts]\nruntime = \"claude\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"high\"\n".into()),
        ("deciders", "[orchestrator.deciders]\nmode = \"codex\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n".into()),
        ("custom_roster", format!("[orchestrator]\ndefault_runtime = \"codex\"\n{CODEX_ROWS}")),
        ("no_builtin_models", format!("[orchestrator]\ndefault_runtime = \"codex\"\nbuiltin_models = false\n{CODEX_ROWS}")),
    ]
}

pub(crate) fn golden() -> BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>> {
    serde_json::from_str(GOLDEN).expect("model_roles_golden.json parses")
}

/// `<runtime>:<model>`, `default` for the CLI's own.
fn model(route: &Route) -> String {
    let id = if route.model.is_empty() { "default" } else { route.model.as_str() };
    format!("{}:{id}", route.runtime.label())
}

const PROFILE: &str = "goal = \"golden\"\n\n[profile]\ncheck = \"true\"\nhub = [\"hub/**\"]\n";

/// A one-task plan built with `cfg`: the task's route is today's whole resolution
/// (`resolve_task_lenient`, then `route_pick::pick_all`).
fn one_task(cfg: &config::Orchestrator, size: &str, owns: &str) -> Result<Run, String> {
    let extra = "test_mode = \"check\"\ntest_mode_reason = \"golden\"";
    let plan = plan_with(PROFILE, &[task_toml("t", size, owns, extra)]);
    build_with(&plan, cfg).map_err(|e| show(&e))
}

/// Today's helpers route: `DeciderContext::new`'s route, then `route_over` with every
/// runtime installed (its `decider` list's first, else the mode's route).
fn helpers_today(cfg: &config::Orchestrator) -> Route {
    let d = &cfg.deciders;
    let runtime = if d.mode == DeciderMode::Codex { Runtime::Codex } else { Runtime::Claude };
    let base = crate::decider::call::ladder_route(&cfg.models, runtime, d.strength, d.effort);
    let list = FrozenList::freeze(&cfg.tuning.routes.decider, &cfg.models);
    route_pick::role(&list, 0, base.effort, &Installed::new())
        .and_then(|pick| pick.route)
        .unwrap_or(base)
}

/// What each golden row resolves to with today's code.
pub(crate) fn today(cfg: &config::Orchestrator) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    for (key, size, owns) in [
        ("implementer.small", "S", "[\"a/**\"]"),
        ("implementer.medium", "M", "[\"b/**\"]"),
        ("implementer.hub", "M", "[\"hub/x.rs\"]"),
    ] {
        let cell = one_task(cfg, size, owns).map_or(UNRESOLVED.to_string(), |run| model(&run.tasks[0].route));
        out.insert(key.to_string(), cell);
    }
    let mut run = one_task(cfg, "M", "[\"b/**\"]").expect("the medium plan builds in every matrix config");
    let none = Installed::new();
    let agent = &cfg.agent.agent;
    let orchestrator = roles::lists::orchestrator(None, &run.limits.route_lists.orchestrator, agent.effort, &none, || {
        launch::resolve_orchestrator(None, agent, cfg.default_runtime, &run.roster)
    })
    .expect("the orchestrator resolves")
    .route;
    let mut record = orch_support::orchestrator();
    record.route = orchestrator.clone();
    run.orch.orchestrator = Some(record);
    let picks = roles::lists::brainstorm_picks(&run);
    let rows = [
        ("orchestrator", orchestrator),
        ("planner", launch::planner_route(&run).expect("an orchestrator is set")),
        ("test_writer", route_pick::writer_route(&run, 0)),
        ("reviewer", run.tasks[0].review_route.clone().expect("an M task is reviewed")),
        ("research", launch::scout_route_of(&run, "golden")),
        ("brainstorm.first", picks[0].route.clone()),
        ("brainstorm.second", picks[1].route.clone()),
        ("helpers", helpers_today(cfg)),
    ];
    for (key, route) in rows {
        out.insert(key.to_string(), model(&route));
    }
    out
}

#[test]
fn todays_resolution_matches_the_golden_file() {
    let golden = golden();
    let matrix = matrix();
    assert_eq!(golden.len(), matrix.len(), "one golden entry per matrix config");
    for (name, text) in &matrix {
        let (config, problems) = config::parse(text);
        assert!(problems.is_empty(), "{name}: {problems:?}");
        assert_eq!(today(&config.orchestrator), golden[*name], "{name}");
    }
}
```

```rust
use super::*;
use proto::Runtime;

fn choice(model: &str, effort: Option<&str>, fallback: Option<&str>) -> RoleChoice {
    RoleChoice {
        model: ModelRef::parse(model).unwrap(),
        effort: effort.map(str::to_string),
        fallback: fallback.map(|f| ModelRef::parse(f).unwrap()),
    }
}

fn table(rows: &[(Role, RoleChoice)]) -> ModelTable {
    ModelTable { rows: rows.iter().cloned().collect(), brainstorm: None }
}

#[test]
fn model_refs_parse_and_print() {
    assert_eq!(
        ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-opus-5-5").unwrap(),
        ModelRef { runtime: Runtime::Claude, id: Some("claude-opus-5-5".into()) }
    );
    assert_eq!(ModelRef::parse("codex:default").unwrap(), ModelRef::default_of(Runtime::Codex));
    assert_eq!(ModelRef::parse("codex:gpt-6-sol").unwrap().label(), "codex:gpt-6-sol");
    assert_eq!(ModelRef::default_of(Runtime::Claude).label(), "claude:default");
    let long = format!("codex:{}", "x".repeat(101));
    for bad in ["", "opus", "shell:x", "claude:", "claude:a b", "claude:a\u{202e}b", "claude:a\nb", long.as_str()] {
        assert!(ModelRef::parse(bad).is_err(), "{bad:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_models_table_reads_every_row_shape() {
    let (config, problems) = crate::parse(
        r#"
[models.orchestrator]
model = "claude:claude-opus-5-5"
effort = "high"

[models.implementer.medium]
model = "codex:gpt-6-sol"
effort = "medium"
fallback = "claude:claude-opus-5-5"

[models.helpers]
model = "claude:claude-haiku-4-5"

[models.helpers.run_name]
model = "claude:claude-sonnet-5"

[models.brainstorm]
first = "claude:claude-opus-5-5"
second = "codex:default"
effort = "high"
"#,
    );
    assert!(problems.is_empty(), "{problems:?}");
    let t = &config.orchestrator.roles;
    assert_eq!(t.rows[&Role::Orchestrator], choice("claude:claude-opus-5-5", Some("high"), None));
    assert_eq!(
        t.rows[&Role::ImplementerMedium],
        choice("codex:gpt-6-sol", Some("medium"), Some("claude:claude-opus-5-5"))
    );
    assert_eq!(t.rows[&Role::Helpers], choice("claude:claude-haiku-4-5", None, None));
    assert_eq!(t.rows[&Role::Helper(HelperKind::RunName)], choice("claude:claude-sonnet-5", None, None));
    assert_eq!(
        t.brainstorm,
        Some(BrainstormChoice {
            first: ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-opus-5-5").unwrap(),
            second: ModelRef::default_of(Runtime::Codex),
            effort: Some("high".into()),
        })
    );
    assert_eq!(t.rows.len(), 4);
}

#[test]
fn resolution_takes_the_repository_row_then_the_global_then_the_builtin() {
    let global = table(&[
        (Role::Reviewer, choice("claude:claude-opus-5-5", Some("high"), None)),
        (Role::Planner, choice("codex:gpt-6-sol", None, None)),
    ]);
    let repo = table(&[(Role::Reviewer, choice("codex:gpt-6.1-sol", None, None))]);
    // A row is overridden whole: the repository's row has no effort, and none is inherited.
    assert_eq!(resolve(Role::Reviewer, Some(&repo), &global), choice("codex:gpt-6.1-sol", None, None));
    assert_eq!(resolve(Role::Planner, Some(&repo), &global), choice("codex:gpt-6-sol", None, None));
    assert_eq!(resolve(Role::Research, Some(&repo), &global), builtin_choice(Role::Research));
}

#[test]
fn a_helper_kind_takes_kind_then_helpers_repository_first() {
    let triage = Role::Helper(HelperKind::Triage);
    let run_name = Role::Helper(HelperKind::RunName);
    let global = table(&[
        (Role::Helpers, choice("claude:claude-sonnet-5", None, None)),
        (triage, choice("codex:default", None, None)),
    ]);
    let repo_helpers = table(&[(Role::Helpers, choice("codex:gpt-6-luna", None, None))]);
    let repo_triage = table(&[(triage, choice("claude:claude-opus-5-5", None, None))]);
    assert_eq!(resolve(triage, Some(&repo_triage), &global).model.label(), "claude:claude-opus-5-5");
    // The global kind beats the repository's helpers (MR §3.4's order).
    assert_eq!(resolve(triage, Some(&repo_helpers), &global).model.label(), "codex:default");
    assert_eq!(resolve(run_name, Some(&repo_helpers), &global).model.label(), "codex:gpt-6-luna");
    assert_eq!(resolve(run_name, None, &global).model.label(), "claude:claude-sonnet-5");
    assert_eq!(resolve(run_name, None, &ModelTable::default()), builtin_choice(Role::Helpers));
}

#[test]
fn a_repository_file_loads_and_a_broken_one_is_ignored_with_a_warning() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join(REPO_FILE);
    assert_eq!(load_repo(&path), (None, vec![]));
    std::fs::write(&path, "[models.reviewer]\nmodel = \"codex:gpt-6.1-sol\"\n").unwrap();
    let (table, warnings) = load_repo(&path);
    assert!(warnings.is_empty(), "{warnings:?}");
    assert_eq!(table.unwrap().rows[&Role::Reviewer].model.label(), "codex:gpt-6.1-sol");
    std::fs::write(&path, "[models.reviewer\n").unwrap();
    let (table, warnings) = load_repo(&path);
    assert_eq!(table, None);
    assert_eq!(warnings.len(), 1, "{warnings:?}");
    assert!(warnings[0].starts_with(&format!("{}: not valid TOML", path.display())), "{warnings:?}");
}

#[test]
fn saving_an_empty_repository_table_removes_the_file() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join(REPO_FILE);
    let t = table(&[(Role::Planner, choice("codex:default", None, None))]);
    save_repo(&path, &t).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(load_repo(&path).0, Some(t));
    save_repo(&path, &ModelTable::default()).unwrap();
    assert!(!path.exists());
}
```

And in `proto/src/models_tests.rs`: `roles_print_and_parse_their_keys` (`Role::all()` round-trips through `key`/`parse_key`, `Role::ImplementerMedium.key() == "implementer.medium"`, `Role::Helper(HelperKind::CiSummary).key() == "helpers.ci_summary"`, and `parse_key("helpers.nope") == None`) and `a_model_table_is_a_json_map_keyed_by_role` (`serde_json::to_value` of a two-row table is `{"rows": {"implementer.small": {"model": "codex:gpt-6-sol"}, "reviewer": {"model": "claude:default", "effort": "high"}}}` and decodes back).

**Change.** Write the types (Interfaces), `builtin_choice`/`builtin_brainstorm` (decision 8), `resolve`/`resolve_brainstorm` (decision 7):

```rust
pub fn resolve(role: Role, repo: Option<&ModelTable>, global: &ModelTable) -> RoleChoice {
    let row = |t: Option<&ModelTable>, r: Role| t.and_then(|t| t.rows.get(&r)).cloned();
    let found = match role {
        Role::Helper(_) => row(repo, role)
            .or_else(|| row(Some(global), role))
            .or_else(|| row(repo, Role::Helpers))
            .or_else(|| row(Some(global), Role::Helpers)),
        _ => row(repo, role).or_else(|| row(Some(global), role)),
    };
    found.unwrap_or_else(|| builtin_choice(role))
}
```

`read_table` walks `models.<row>` (`orchestrator`, `planner`, `test_writer`, `reviewer`, `research`: a row table), `models.implementer.{small,medium,hub}`, `models.helpers` (a row table whose six kind keys are row tables), and `models.brainstorm` (`first`, `second`, `effort`); keys `model` (required), `effort` (`valid_effort`), `fallback`; problems sorted by key. `load_repo` reads on the calling thread (callers are on `spawn_blocking`) and names the path in each warning.

**Run.**
- `cargo test -p anthrex-config models::` and `cargo test -p anthrex-proto models_tests` → FAIL before (no `models`), PASS after.

**Acceptance.** The tests pass; `config::parse("")` gives an empty `roles`; the five AGENTS.md commands pass.

**Commit.** `feat(config): the role table, its resolution and the repository file`

```rust
// Mounted from `models/mod.rs` as `#[cfg(test)] mod migrate_tests;`.
use super::*;

fn parsed(text: &str) -> crate::Orchestrator {
    crate::parse(text).0.orchestrator
}

fn row(o: &crate::Orchestrator, role: Role) -> (String, Option<String>, Option<String>) {
    let c = &o.roles.rows[&role];
    (c.model.label(), c.effort.clone(), c.fallback.as_ref().map(ModelRef::label))
}

const ROSTER: &str = "[[orchestrator.models]]\nruntime = \"codex\"\nmodel = \"gpt-6-sol\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\n\n[[orchestrator.models]]\nruntime = \"codex\"\nmodel = \"gpt-6.1-sol\"\nstrength = \"frontier\"\n";

#[test]
fn a_default_config_migrates_to_nothing() {
    let o = parsed("");
    assert!(o.roles.is_empty(), "{:?}", o.roles);
    assert!(o.roles_notes.is_empty(), "{:?}", o.roles_notes);
}

#[test]
fn a_route_list_gives_the_rows_model_and_fallback() {
    let o = parsed(&format!(
        "{ROSTER}\n[orchestrator.routes.review]\ncandidates = [{{ runtime = \"codex\", model = \"gpt-6.1-sol\", effort = \"high\" }}, {{ runtime = \"claude\", model = \"claude-opus-5-5\" }}]\n"
    ));
    assert_eq!(row(&o, Role::Reviewer), ("codex:gpt-6.1-sol".into(), Some("high".into()), Some("claude:claude-opus-5-5".into())));
    assert!(o.roles_notes.contains(&"config: [orchestrator.routes.review] is replaced by [models.reviewer]; it is migrated until you save in C-b S".to_string()), "{:?}", o.roles_notes);
    assert!(o.roles_notes.contains(&"config: [[orchestrator.models]] is replaced by the models your CLIs report (C-b S); it is migrated until you save in C-b S".to_string()), "{:?}", o.roles_notes);
}

fn the_agent_table_migrates_to_the_orchestrator_row() {
    let o = parsed("[orchestrator.agent]\nruntime = \"claude\"\nmodel = \"claude-sonnet-5\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n");
    assert_eq!(row(&o, Role::Orchestrator), ("claude:claude-sonnet-5".into(), Some("medium".into()), None));

        "[orchestrator.planners]\nruntime = \"codex\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n\n[orchestrator.scouts]\nruntime = \"claude\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"high\"\n\n[orchestrator.deciders]\nmode = \"codex\"\nstrength = \"standard\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n",
```

Add to `daemon/src/run/model_roles_golden.rs`:

```rust
/// What the role table resolves each golden row to (task M9.8.3).
fn resolved(cfg: &config::Orchestrator) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    use config::models::{Role, resolve, resolve_brainstorm};
    let t = &cfg.roles;
    let mut out: BTreeMap<String, String> = [
        ("orchestrator", Role::Orchestrator), ("planner", Role::Planner),
        ("implementer.small", Role::ImplementerSmall), ("implementer.medium", Role::ImplementerMedium),
        ("implementer.hub", Role::ImplementerHub), ("test_writer", Role::TestWriter),
        ("reviewer", Role::Reviewer), ("research", Role::Research), ("helpers", Role::Helpers),
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .map(|(key, role)| (key.to_string(), resolve(role, None, t).model.label()))
    .collect();
    let b = resolve_brainstorm(None, t);
    out.insert("brainstorm.first".into(), b.first.label());
    out.insert("brainstorm.second".into(), b.second.label());
    out
}

#[test]
fn the_role_table_resolves_every_old_config_as_today() {
    let builtin = resolved(&config::Orchestrator::default());
    for (name, text) in &matrix() {
        let (config, _) = config::parse(text);
        let new = resolved(&config.orchestrator);
        for (row, model) in &golden()[*name] {
            let want = if model == UNRESOLVED { &builtin[row] } else { model };
            assert_eq!(&new[row], want, "{name}: {row}");
        }
    }
}
```

`cli/tests/models_migration.rs::daemon_start_logs_each_migrated_key`: `RunHarness::with_config("", "[orchestrator.routes.review]\ncandidates = [{ runtime = \"claude\", model = \"claude-opus-5-5\" }]\n", &[])`; a deadline loop (`DAEMON_START_WAIT`) on `h.log_tail()` until it contains `config: [orchestrator.routes.review] is replaced by [models.reviewer]; it is migrated until you save in C-b S`; then `h.log_tail().matches("[orchestrator.routes.review] is replaced").count() == 1`.

**Change.** `migrate.rs` per decisions 14–18: private `Old { runtime, model, strength, effort }` (`model_ref`, `choice(fallback)`), private ports of `roster::{first_at, lowest_at_or_above, strongest_of, peer}` and `scout::spec::route_within`, `listed(list, roster, effort)` (first candidate in the roster as the model, the next as the fallback), and one block per row in this order: orchestrator (list, else `launch::resolve_orchestrator`'s rule: `agent.runtime` else `default_runtime`; `agent.model` when non-empty, else that runtime's first `frontier` entry, else its strongest, else `default`), planner (list, else `route_within(planners.runtime` else the orchestrator's runtime`, strength, effort)`), the three implementers (list, else `first_at(default_runtime, S standard | M standard | hub frontier)` at `low | medium | high`, else the decision-16 note), test writer and reviewer from the medium route (decision 15), research (list, else `route_within(scouts.runtime` else `default_runtime, …)`), brainstorm (list: the first two on different runtimes, else the first two, one entry twice; else the strongest of Claude then Codex, one twice, none the orchestrator's twice; effort the first's), helpers (list, else `lowest_at_or_above(mode's runtime, strength)`, else that runtime's first entry, else `default`). A row is written by the decision-14 rule; notes by decision 18, in the file's key order. In this task `Effort` is still the three-value enum: `fn effort_name(e: Effort) -> String` matches on it; M9.8.4 replaces the match with `e.as_str().to_string()`.

```rust
use serde_json::json;
use crate::delivery_tests_wire::variant_names;
use crate::*;

fn both<T: serde::Serialize + serde::de::DeserializeOwned + PartialEq + std::fmt::Debug>(v: &T) {
    let back: T = serde_json::from_value(serde_json::to_value(v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(&back, v);
    let back: T = rmp_serde::from_slice(&rmp_serde::to_vec_named(v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(&back, v);
}

fn catalog() -> ModelCatalog {
    ModelCatalog {
        runtime: Runtime::Codex,
        cli_version: "0.160.1".into(),
        fetched_at: 1_780_000_000,
        source: CatalogSource::Live,
        models: vec![CatalogModel {
            id: "gpt-6-sol".into(),
            label: "gpt-6 sol".into(),
            description: "Balanced".into(),
            efforts: vec!["low".into(), "medium".into(), "high".into(), "max".into()],
            default_effort: Some("medium".into()),
            is_default: true,
        }],
        problem: None,
    }
}

#[test]
fn effort_is_a_string_and_a_protocol_18_value_decodes() {
    let old = json!({"runtime": "claude", "model": "claude-opus-5-5", "strength": "frontier", "effort": "high"});
    let route: Route = serde_json::from_value(old.clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(route.effort, Effort::HIGH);
    // MessagePack, as a protocol-18 peer wrote the enum: its variant name.
    let back: Route = rmp_serde::from_slice(&rmp_serde::to_vec_named(&old).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back, route);
    let xhigh: Effort = serde_json::from_value(json!("xhigh")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(xhigh.as_str(), "xhigh");
    assert!(Effort::DEFAULT.is_default() && !Effort::LOW.is_default());
    assert!(Effort::DEFAULT < Effort::LOW && Effort::LOW < Effort::MEDIUM && Effort::MEDIUM < Effort::HIGH && Effort::HIGH < xhigh);
    assert_eq!(Effort::new("low"), Effort::LOW);
}

#[test]
fn catalogs_round_trip_and_problem_is_left_out_while_none() {
    both(&catalog());
    assert!(serde_json::to_value(catalog()).unwrap().get("problem").is_none());
    let mut missing = catalog();
    missing.source = CatalogSource::Builtin;
    missing.problem = Some("codex not found".into());
    both(&missing);
}

#[test]
fn list_models_and_models_round_trip() {
    both(&ClientMsg::ListModels { runtime: Some(Runtime::Claude), refresh: true });
    both(&ClientMsg::ListModels { runtime: None, refresh: false });
    both(&DaemonMsg::Models { catalogs: vec![catalog()] });
}

#[test]
fn the_new_variants_are_appended_last() {
    assert_eq!(variant_names::<ClientMsg>().last().map(String::as_str), Some("ListModels"));
    assert_eq!(variant_names::<DaemonMsg>().last().map(String::as_str), Some("Models"));
    let requests = variant_names::<SettingsRequest>();
    assert_eq!(requests[requests.len() - 2..], ["RepoModels", "PutRepoModels"]);
    let replies = variant_names::<SettingsReply>();
    assert_eq!(replies[replies.len() - 2..], ["RepoModels", "RepoSaved"]);
}

#[test]
fn orchestrator_choice_effort_defaults_to_none() {
    let old: OrchestratorChoice = serde_json::from_value(json!({"runtime": "codex", "model": "gpt-6-sol"})).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(old.effort, None);

    let mut table = ModelTable::default();
```

### M9.8.5 fake-agent: both discovery handshakes

**Files.** Create `fake-agent/src/discovery.rs`, `fake-agent/fixtures/claude-initialize.json`, `fake-agent/fixtures/codex-model-list.json`, `fake-agent/tests/discovery.rs`. Modify `fake-agent/src/main.rs` (`mod discovery;`; at the top of `run`, before `headless::detect`: `if let Some(code) = discovery::codex_app_server(&args)? { return Ok(code); }`; `--version` prints `FAKE_AGENT_VERSION` when set, else today's line), `fake-agent/src/decider.rs::claude` (after `read_first_line`: `if let Some(id) = discovery::claude_initialize(first.as_deref()) { return discovery::answer_claude(&id, args).map(Start::Answered); }`).

**Behaviour.**
- **Claude** (`-p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --verbose`, no MCP server, not resumed, first line `{"type":"control_request","request_id":<id>,"request":{"subtype":"initialize"}}`): records the line with `roles::record_stdin("discovery-claude", line)`, writes `{"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":<id>,"response":<fixture>}}` where `<fixture>` is `claude-initialize.json` (`{"commands": [], "models": [...]}`), then reads stdin to EOF and exits 0. Never a `system`, `assistant` or `result` line: no turn runs.
- **Codex** (`app-server`): newline-delimited JSON-RPC without a `jsonrpc` member, as Codex's app-server speaks it. `{"id":N,"method":"initialize",…}` → `{"id":N,"result":{"userAgent":"fake-agent"}}`; the `initialized` notification → nothing; `{"id":N,"method":"model/list","params":{"includeHidden":false,"cursor":C}}` → page `C` of `codex-model-list.json` (an array of pages; `cursor: null` is page 0, `"p2"` page 1) as `{"id":N,"result":<page>}`; any other method → `{"id":N,"error":{"code":-32601,"message":"method not found"}}`; EOF → exit 0. Every line is recorded with `record_stdin("discovery-codex", …)`.
- `FAKE_AGENT_MODELS_DIR`, when set, replaces the built-in fixtures with that directory's files of the same names. `FAKE_AGENT_DISCOVERY` = `hang` (read stdin, answer nothing, never exit until killed), `garbage` (answer each request with `not json` then a valid reply), `empty` (Claude: `"models": []`; Codex: one empty page).

Fixtures (the shape MR §4.1 names, from the public SDK and app-server docs; the manual check compares them with the real replies, decision 26):

```json
  {"value": "claude-haiku-4-5", "displayName": "Haiku 4.5", "description": "Fastest for quick tasks", "supportsEffort": false, "supportedEffortLevels": []},
  {"value": "claude-sonnet-5", "displayName": "Sonnet 5", "description": "Best for everyday tasks", "supportsEffort": true, "supportedEffortLevels": ["low", "medium", "high", "max"]},

]}
```

**Tests first.** `fake-agent/tests/discovery.rs` (spawns `env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_fake-agent")` with piped stdio, `FAKE_AGENT_STDIN_FILE` in a temp dir, a 5 s read deadline per line through a reader thread and `recv_timeout`; each child is reaped by the test with `wait` after closing stdin, and a `hang` child is killed by the test through its own `Child::kill`, never by pid):

```rust
#[test]
fn claude_answers_initialize_with_the_fixture_models_and_runs_no_turn() {
    let out = talk(
        &["-p", "--input-format", "stream-json", "--output-format", "stream-json", "--verbose"],
        &[r#"{"type":"control_request","request_id":"anthrex-models-1","request":{"subtype":"initialize"}}"#],
        &[],
    );
    assert_eq!(out.len(), 1, "{out:?}");
    let reply: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&out[0]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(reply["type"], "control_response");
    assert_eq!(reply["response"]["request_id"], "anthrex-models-1");
    let models = reply["response"]["response"]["models"].as_array().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(models.iter().map(|m| m["value"].as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5-5"]);
}

#[test]
fn codex_app_server_pages_model_list() {
    let out = talk(
        &["app-server"],
        &[
            r#"{"id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"clientInfo":{"name":"anthrex","version":"0"}}}"#,
            r#"{"method":"initialized"}"#,
            r#"{"id":2,"method":"model/list","params":{"includeHidden":false,"cursor":null}}"#,
            r#"{"id":3,"method":"model/list","params":{"includeHidden":false,"cursor":"p2"}}"#,
        ],
        &[],
    );
    assert_eq!(out.len(), 3, "{out:?}");
    let page = |i: usize| serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&out[i]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(page(1)["id"], 2);
    assert_eq!(page(1)["result"]["nextCursor"], "p2");
    assert_eq!(page(2)["result"]["data"][0]["id"], "gpt-6.1-sol");
    assert!(page(2)["result"]["nextCursor"].is_null());
}

#[test]
fn a_version_override_is_printed() {
    let out = std::process::Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_fake-agent"))
        .arg("--version")
        .env("FAKE_AGENT_VERSION", "2.1.290 (Claude Code)")
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim(), "2.1.290 (Claude Code)");
}
```
