### M9.8.6 Discovery: probes, cache and ListModels

**Files.** First a **move-only commit**: `ProbeChild` (its struct, its `Drop`, nothing else) from `daemon/src/lifecycle/codex_version.rs` to `daemon/src/probe_child.rs` as `pub(crate)`, mounted in `daemon/src/lib.rs` as `mod probe_child;`; `codex_version.rs` imports it. Its fields become `pub(crate)` so `codex_version::probe` still builds them; no line of `Drop` changes (the review diffs the moved block). Record the daemon crate's test count before and after. Then create `daemon/src/models/{mod,lines,version,claude_probe,codex_probe,cache,builtin}.rs`, `models/tests.rs`, `models/tests_service.rs`; modify `daemon/src/lib.rs` (`pub mod models;`), `daemon/src/run/driver.rs` (or wherever `RunService` is defined: an `Arc<ModelService>` field and `pub fn models(&self)`; `RunService::for_manager` builds it with `CliProbes` from `manager.config().{claude_bin, codex_bin}`), `daemon/src/server.rs` and `server/requests.rs` (`ClientMsg::ListModels` → `requests::list_models(runs.models(), out_tx.clone(), runtime, refresh)`, a spawned task that awaits `list` and sends `DaemonMsg::Models`), `daemon/src/lifecycle.rs` (`runs.models().load_disk().await` before `serve`). Create `cli/tests/models_discovery.rs` and `cli/tests/support/models.rs`. Add the timing rows to `docs/timing-budgets.md`.

**Interfaces.** Consumes M9.8.4's types, M9.8.5's fake. Produces Interfaces "`daemon`"'s `models` module and `RunService::models`.

**The probes.**
- `lines.rs`: `LineReader::new(stdout: ChildStdout) -> io::Result<LineReader>` sets `O_NONBLOCK` exactly as `codex_version::probe` does (`fcntl`, `:156-160`); `next(&mut self, until: Instant, cancel: &CancellationToken) -> Result<Option<String>, ProbeError>` returns the next complete line (without `\n`), `Ok(None)` at EOF, `Timeout` at `until`, `Cancelled`, or `Failed` past `LINE_MAX_BYTES` or `REPLY_MAX_BYTES` total; it polls with a 5 ms sleep, as the version probe does. It keeps every line it returned (for decision 26).
- `version.rs`: `cli_version(program, deadline, cancel) -> Result<String, ProbeError>`: `program --version` with `stdin(null)`, `stderr(null)`, `process_group(0)`, owned by `ProbeChild { deadline, … }`; reads to EOF under `deadline - REAP_RESERVE`; `parse_version` (decision 23) gives `x.y.z`, else `unknown`. A spawn error of kind `NotFound` (or `PermissionDenied` on a directory) is `Missing`.
- `claude_probe.rs`: `probe(program, deadline, cancel, env_remove: &[&str]) -> Result<(Vec<CatalogModel>, Vec<String>), ProbeError>`: spawns `program -p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --verbose` with the session environment a headless Claude session gets (`headless::credential_scrub_for(Runtime::Claude, ClaudeAuth::Login)` removed, `headless::session_vars(Runtime::Claude, &[])` set), `process_group(0)`, stdin and stdout piped, stderr null, owned by `ProbeChild`; writes exactly one line, `{"type":"control_request","request_id":"anthrex-models-1","request":{"subtype":"initialize"}}` (never a user message); reads lines, skipping any that is not JSON or is not a `control_response` with that `request_id`; takes `response.response.models[]` (`value` → `id`, `displayName` → `label`, `description`, `supportedEffortLevels` → `efforts` when `supportsEffort` is true, else none; `default_effort: None`; `is_default` for `value == "default"`); then drops stdin and lets `ProbeChild` reap. An `error` subtype is `Failed("claude refused initialize: <message cut to 200 chars>")`; no models is `Failed("claude's reply had no models")`.
- `codex_probe.rs`: same spawn rules with `program app-server` and `credential_scrub_for(Runtime::Codex, ClaudeAuth::Login)`; sends `{"id":1,"method":"initialize","params":{"clientInfo":{"name":"anthrex","version":<crate version>}}}`, waits for id 1, sends `{"method":"initialized"}`, then `{"id":N,"method":"model/list","params":{"includeHidden":false,"cursor":C}}` with `N` from 2 and `C` from `null`, following `nextCursor` until it is null or absent, at most 20 pages; takes `data[]` (`id`, else `model`; `displayName`; `description`; `supportedReasoningEfforts[]` as strings or as objects' `reasoningEffort`; `defaultReasoningEffort`; `isDefault`). The requests are a few hundred bytes each, far under the pipe buffer, so the writes on this blocking thread cannot stall (the AGENTS.md PTY fact is about a PTY master, not a pipe this thread alone writes).
- Every string from a reply is kept as given; the TUI cleans it when drawing (decision 38). A model id that `ModelRef::parse` would refuse is dropped from the catalog.

**The service** (MR §4.2): `list(runtime, refresh)` runs each runtime asked on its own `tokio::spawn`, each behind that runtime's `tokio::sync::Mutex<()>` gate (single flight: a second caller waits for the first's result, then reads memory). One runtime's refresh, inside one `spawn_blocking` with `deadline = now + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT`: the version (decision 23); unless `refresh`, a cache file `<data_dir>/models/<runtime>-<version>.json` for that version is used as `Live` with its own `fetched_at`; otherwise the live probe, whose catalog (`Live`) is written to that file and its raw lines to `<runtime>-<version>.raw.jsonl` (decision 26); on `Missing`: the built-in list, `Builtin`, `problem: "<runtime> not found"`; on any other error: the newest `<runtime>-*.json` by `fetched_at` marked `Cached` with `problem` set (`claude did not answer within 10 s` for `Timeout`, the error's text otherwise), else the built-in list, `Builtin`, with that `problem` and one `tracing::warn!`. The memory map (`std::sync::Mutex`, taken with `crate::lock` only to clone or replace a catalog) is updated after the blocking work returns. `builtin.rs`: Claude `claude-haiku-4-5` (Haiku 4.5, no efforts), `claude-sonnet-5` (Sonnet 5) and `claude-opus-5-5` (Opus 5.5) with `low`, `medium`, `high`; Codex one `default` entry (`Codex default`, `is_default`, `low`, `medium`, `high`): only today's three effort names, since a built-in list validates nothing (decision 21).

**Tests first.** `daemon/src/models/tests.rs` (each probe program is a `/bin/sh` script written by `testexec::write_executable` into a temp dir; scripts answer with fixed lines):

```rust
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken;
use super::*;

const WALL_CLOCK_SLACK: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);

fn script(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str, body: &str) -> String {
    let path = dir.join(name);
    testexec::write_executable(&path, &format!("#!/bin/sh\n{body}\n"));
    path.to_str().unwrap().to_string()
}

const CLAUDE_REPLY: &str = r#"{"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":"anthrex-models-1","response":{"commands":[],"models":[{"value":"claude-opus-5-5","displayName":"Opus 5.5","description":"Most capable","supportsEffort":true,"supportedEffortLevels":["low","medium","high","max"]},{"value":"claude-haiku-4-5","displayName":"Haiku 4.5","description":"Fastest","supportsEffort":false}]}}}"#;

#[test]
fn claude_probe_reads_the_initialize_reply_and_sends_no_prompt() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let seen = dir.path().join("stdin");
    let program = script(dir.path(), "claude", &format!(
        "IFS= read -r line; printf '%s\\n' \"$line\" > '{}'\necho 'Warning: a banner that is not JSON'\necho '{CLAUDE_REPLY}'\ncat >> '{}'",
        seen.display(), seen.display()
    ));
    let deadline = Instant::now() + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT;
    let (models, raw) = claude_probe::probe(&program, deadline, &CancellationToken::new(), &[]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(models.iter().map(|m| m.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["claude-opus-5-5", "claude-haiku-4-5"]);
    assert_eq!(models[0].efforts, ["low", "medium", "high", "max"]);
    assert!(models[1].efforts.is_empty());
    assert_eq!(raw.len(), 2, "{raw:?}");
    let lines: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_to_string(&seen).unwrap().lines().map(str::to_string).collect();
    assert_eq!(lines, [r#"{"type":"control_request","request_id":"anthrex-models-1","request":{"subtype":"initialize"}}"#]);
}

#[test]
fn codex_probe_follows_the_cursor() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let page = |id: u32, model: &str, next: &str| format!(
        r#"{{"id":{id},"result":{{"data":[{{"id":"{model}","displayName":"{model}","description":"d","supportedReasoningEfforts":[{{"reasoningEffort":"low"}},"high"],"defaultReasoningEffort":"low","isDefault":false}}],"nextCursor":{next}}}}}"#
    );
    let program = script(dir.path(), "codex", &format!(
        "read -r a; echo '{{\"id\":1,\"result\":{{}}}}'\nread -r b\nread -r c; echo '{}'\nread -r d; echo '{}'\ncat > /dev/null",
        page(2, "gpt-6-sol", "\"p2\""), page(3, "gpt-6.1-sol", "null")
    ));
    let deadline = Instant::now() + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT;
    let (models, _) = codex_probe::probe(&program, deadline, &CancellationToken::new(), &[]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(models.iter().map(|m| m.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["gpt-6-sol", "gpt-6.1-sol"]);
    assert_eq!(models[0].efforts, ["low", "high"]);
    assert_eq!(models[0].default_effort.as_deref(), Some("low"));
}

#[test]
fn a_probe_past_its_deadline_is_killed_reaped_and_reported() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pid_file = dir.path().join("pid");
    let program = script(dir.path(), "claude", &format!("echo $$ > '{}'\nexec sleep 30", pid_file.display()));
    let started = Instant::now();
    let deadline = started + Duration::from_millis(500);
    let result = claude_probe::probe(&program, deadline, &CancellationToken::new(), &[]);
    assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err(), ProbeError::Timeout);
    assert!(started.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(500) + REAP_RESERVE + WALL_CLOCK_SLACK, "{:?}", started.elapsed());
    let pid: i32 = std::fs::read_to_string(&pid_file).unwrap().trim().parse().unwrap();
    // Our own child only, and only asked whether it exists (signal 0).
    assert_eq!(unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) }, -1, "the probe's child {pid} is still alive");
}

#[test]
fn a_missing_cli_is_missing() {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT;
    let result = version::cli_version("/nonexistent/anthrex-test/claude", deadline, &CancellationToken::new());
    assert_eq!(result.unwrap_err(), ProbeError::Missing);
}

#[test]
fn a_model_id_that_is_not_a_model_ref_is_dropped() {
    // Review focus 3: a CLI's id with a space or a control character never reaches argv.
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let reply = r#"{"type":"control_response","response":{"subtype":"success","request_id":"anthrex-models-1","response":{"models":[{"value":"bad id","displayName":"x","description":"","supportsEffort":false},{"value":"claude-ok","displayName":"\u001b[31mOK\u202e","description":"","supportsEffort":false}]}}}"#;
    let program = script(dir.path(), "claude", &format!("read -r line; echo '{reply}'; cat > /dev/null"));
    let (models, _) = claude_probe::probe(&program, Instant::now() + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT, &CancellationToken::new(), &[]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(models.iter().map(|m| m.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["claude-ok"]);
    // Labels are kept as given; the TUI cleans them when drawing (decision 38).
    assert_eq!(models[0].label, "\u{1b}[31mOK\u{202e}");
}

#[test]
fn an_endless_reply_line_ends_the_probe() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let program = script(dir.path(), "claude", "read -r line; yes x | tr -d '\\n'");
    let result = claude_probe::probe(&program, Instant::now() + DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT, &CancellationToken::new(), &[]);
    assert!(matches!(result, Err(ProbeError::Failed(ref m)) if m.contains("too long")), "{result:?}");
}
```

`daemon/src/models/tests_service.rs` (a `CountingProbes` implementing `Probes` with scripted results and call counters; `ModelService::new(tempdir, probes)`; `#[tokio::test]`):

- `a_cache_for_the_same_version_is_used_without_probing`: first `list(Some(Codex), false)` probes once and writes `models/codex-0.160.1.json`; a second `list` does not call `models` again and returns the same `fetched_at`, `source: Live`.
- `a_new_version_probes_again`: the version changes to `0.161.0`; `list` calls `models` and writes `codex-0.161.0.json`.
- `refresh_probes_even_with_a_matching_cache`.
- `a_failed_probe_serves_the_newest_cache_as_cached`: two cache files for older versions (`fetched_at` 10 and 20); the probe fails with `Timeout`; the catalog is the second's models, `source: Cached`, `problem: Some("codex did not answer within 10 s")`.
- `no_cache_and_a_failed_probe_serve_the_builtin_list`: `source: Builtin`, `models[0].id == "default"`, a `problem`.
- `a_missing_cli_is_builtin_and_says_not_found`: `problem == Some("claude not found")`.
- `two_concurrent_lists_probe_once` (review focus 5): two `list(None, true)` joined; each runtime's `models` counter is 1 for the first and 2 after both only if the second started after the first ended; assert the counter is at most 2 and that no two probes of one runtime overlapped (the counting probe records `(start, end)` and the test asserts the intervals are disjoint).
- `current_never_probes`: a fresh service's `current()` is empty and no counter moved; after `load_disk().await` with a cache on disk, it holds that catalog.

`cli/tests/models_discovery.rs` (real daemon, `fake-agent` as both CLIs through `RunHarness::new("")`, which pins them; `support/models.rs::list_models(socket, runtime, refresh) -> Vec<ModelCatalog>` connects as `run_harness::connect` does, sends `ClientMsg::ListModels`, and reads frames until `DaemonMsg::Models`, under `MODELS_WAIT`):

```rust
#[test]
fn list_models_reports_both_clis_from_their_handshakes_and_caches_them() {
    let h = RunHarness::new("");
    let catalogs = list_models(&h.socket(), None, false);
    assert_eq!(catalogs.len(), 2, "{catalogs:?}");
    let claude = catalogs.iter().find(|c| c.runtime == Runtime::Claude).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(claude.source, CatalogSource::Live);
    assert_eq!(claude.cli_version, "0.155.0");
    assert_eq!(claude.models.iter().map(|m| m.label.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["Haiku 4.5", "Sonnet 5", "Opus 5.5"]);
    let codex = catalogs.iter().find(|c| c.runtime == Runtime::Codex).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(codex.models.len(), 3, "both pages");
    assert!(codex.models.iter().any(|m| m.id == "gpt-6-sol" && m.is_default));
    assert_eq!(h.io_lines("discovery-claude", "stdin").len(), 1, "one initialize, no prompt");
    let again = list_models(&h.socket(), None, false);
    assert_eq!(again, catalogs, "the same version's cache, unchanged");
    assert_eq!(h.io_lines("discovery-claude", "stdin").len(), 1, "no second probe");
    list_models(&h.socket(), Some(Runtime::Claude), true);
    assert_eq!(h.io_lines("discovery-claude", "stdin").len(), 2, "r refreshes");
}
```

**Run.**
- `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 cargo test -p anthrex-daemon --lib models::` → FAIL before, PASS after.
- `cargo build -p anthrex-fake-agent && GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 cargo test -p anthrex --test models_discovery` → PASS.

**Acceptance.** The tests pass; `git diff <move commit>^ <move commit> -- crates/daemon/src` shows `ProbeChild`'s `Drop` body moved byte for byte; `grep -rnE "signal_group|libc::kill\(|SIGKILL|SIGTERM|SIGHUP" crates/daemon/src/models` prints only the test's `libc::kill(pid, 0)`; no probe runs under `crate::lock` (`grep -n "crate::lock" crates/daemon/src/models/*.rs` shows only the memory map's clone and replace); the timing rows are in `docs/timing-budgets.md`; the five AGENTS.md commands pass.

**Commit.** `refactor(daemon): move ProbeChild out of the codex version probe` (the move), then `feat(daemon): discover each CLI's models, cached per version`

### M9.8.7a Launchers I: the frozen table, workers, racer, test writer, reviewer

**Files.** First a **move-only commit**: `plan.rs::for_repo` (with its doc comment) to `run/plan_repo.rs` ("File sizes"), test count recorded. Then create `daemon/src/run/model_roles.rs`, `run/model_roles_tests.rs`, `run/driver/build_models.rs`; modify `run/model_limits.rs` (`RunLimits.models`, `RunLimits::models()`), `run/plan.rs` (`BuildContext.models`; `limits.models = Some(ctx.models.clone())` before the tasks resolve; the run-log lines from `validate`), `run/test_support.rs` (`build_full_tuned` passes `RunModels::resolve(&config.roles, None)`), `run/validate.rs` (`resolve_task_lenient`'s route: the task's row via `RunModels::task_role`, unless the plan's route names a model (until M9.8.9 clears plan routes): then that model on the route's runtime, at the plan's effort else the row's; the reviewer forecast at `:233` becomes `models.reviewer_route(&route).0`), `run/validate_graph.rs` (`planned_runtime(task) = task.route.runtime`, rule 9's new text, decision 33), `run/route_pick.rs` (`pick_all`/`pick_named` become no-ops left for M9.8.13; `review_route`'s callers move), `run/route_pick_step.rs` (`writer_route` and `racer_route` call the new rules; `Mover` moves to `model_roles.rs`), `run/engine/{race,pair,integration}.rs`, `run/edits_amend.rs:217` (the reviewer), `run/driver/build.rs` (one call into `build_models::freeze`), `run/driver/restore.rs` (decision 9's fill for a run without `models`, through `build_models::fill`), `run/orch/launch.rs::orchestrator_role` and the document reviewer only where they read `route_lists` for the reviewer.

**Interfaces.** Consumes `config::models::resolve`, `ModelService::current`. Produces `RunModels`, `ModelEfforts`, `runtime_open`, `Mover` (moved), `BuildContext.models`, `RunLimits.models`.

**Tests first.** `run/model_roles_tests.rs`:

```rust
use proto::{CatalogModel, CatalogSource, ModelCatalog, ModelRef, ModelTable, Role, RoleChoice, Runtime};
use super::*;
use crate::run::test_support::{build_with, plan_with, show, task_toml};

fn row(model: &str, effort: Option<&str>, fallback: Option<&str>) -> RoleChoice {
    RoleChoice { model: ModelRef::parse(model).unwrap(), effort: effort.map(Into::into), fallback: fallback.map(|f| ModelRef::parse(f).unwrap()) }
}

fn config_with(rows: &[(Role, RoleChoice)]) -> config::Orchestrator {
    let mut cfg = config::Orchestrator::default();
    cfg.roles = ModelTable { rows: rows.iter().cloned().collect(), brainstorm: None };
    cfg
}

const PROFILE: &str = "goal = \"g\"\n\n[profile]\ncheck = \"true\"\nhub = [\"hub/**\"]\n";
const CHECK: &str = "test_mode = \"check\"\ntest_mode_reason = \"r\"";

#[test]
fn workers_take_their_size_row() {
    let cfg = config_with(&[
        (Role::ImplementerSmall, row("codex:gpt-6-luna", Some("low"), None)),
        (Role::ImplementerMedium, row("claude:claude-sonnet-5", Some("medium"), None)),
        (Role::ImplementerHub, row("codex:gpt-6.1-sol", Some("xhigh"), None)),
    ]);
    let plan = plan_with(PROFILE, &[
        task_toml("s", "S", "[\"a/**\"]", CHECK),
        task_toml("m", "M", "[\"b/**\"]", CHECK),
        task_toml("h", "M", "[\"hub/x.rs\"]", ""),
    ]);
    let run = build_with(&plan, &cfg).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}", show(&e)));
    let got: Vec<(String, String, String)> = run.tasks.iter()
        .map(|t| (t.id().to_string(), format!("{}:{}", t.route.runtime.label(), t.route.model), t.route.effort.as_str().to_string()))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(got, [
        ("s".into(), "codex:gpt-6-luna".into(), "low".into()),
        ("m".into(), "claude:claude-sonnet-5".into(), "medium".into()),
        ("h".into(), "codex:gpt-6.1-sol".into(), "xhigh".into()),
    ]);
}

#[test]
fn the_reviewer_is_never_the_authors_model() {
    let author = RunModels::route_of(&ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-sonnet-5").unwrap(), Some("medium"));
    let with_fallback = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::Reviewer, row("claude:claude-sonnet-5", Some("high"), Some("codex:gpt-6.1-sol")))].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    let (route, warning) = with_fallback.reviewer_route(&author);
    assert_eq!((route.runtime, route.model.as_str(), route.effort.is_default()), (Runtime::Codex, "gpt-6.1-sol", true));
    assert_eq!(warning, None);
    let without = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::Reviewer, row("claude:claude-sonnet-5", Some("high"), None))].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    let (route, warning) = without.reviewer_route(&author);
    assert_eq!((route.runtime, route.model.as_str()), (Runtime::Claude, "claude-sonnet-5"));
    assert_eq!(warning.as_deref(), Some("reviewer: claude:claude-sonnet-5 reviews work by the same model; set an \"if it struggles\" model for the reviewer in C-b S"));
    // Same runtime, another model: allowed (D3).
    let other = RunModels::route_of(&ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-opus-5-5").unwrap(), None);
    assert_eq!(without.reviewer_route(&other), (RunModels::route_of(&ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-sonnet-5").unwrap(), Some("high")), None));
}

#[test]
fn the_racer_takes_the_rows_fallback_else_the_same_model() {
    let current = RunModels::route_of(&ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-sonnet-5").unwrap(), Some("medium"));
    let with = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::ImplementerMedium, row("claude:claude-sonnet-5", Some("medium"), Some("codex:gpt-6-sol")))].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    assert_eq!(with.racer_route(Role::ImplementerMedium, &current), RunModels::route_of(&ModelRef::parse("codex:gpt-6-sol").unwrap(), None));
    let without = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable::default(), None);
    assert_eq!(without.racer_route(Role::ImplementerMedium, &current), current);
}

fn catalog(models: &[(&str, &[&str], Option<&str>)], source: CatalogSource) -> ModelCatalog {
    ModelCatalog {
        runtime: Runtime::Codex, cli_version: "0.160.1".into(), fetched_at: 1, source, problem: None,
        models: models.iter().map(|(id, efforts, default)| CatalogModel {
            id: id.to_string(), label: id.to_string(), description: String::new(),
            efforts: efforts.iter().map(|e| e.to_string()).collect(),
            default_effort: default.map(Into::into), is_default: false,
        }).collect(),
    }
}

#[test]
fn an_effort_the_model_does_not_offer_is_replaced_and_logged() {
    let mut models = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::ImplementerSmall, row("codex:gpt-6-luna", Some("max"), None))].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    let live = catalog(&[("gpt-6-luna", &["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"], Some("medium"))], CatalogSource::Live);
    let lines = models.validate(&[live.clone()]);
    assert_eq!(lines, ["effort 'max' not offered by gpt-6-luna; using medium"]);
    assert_eq!(models.choice(Role::ImplementerSmall).effort.as_deref(), Some("medium"));
    assert_eq!(models.efforts[&ModelRef::parse("codex:gpt-6-luna").unwrap()].efforts, ["low", "medium", "high", "xhigh"]);
    // Validated once: a second pass changes nothing.
    assert!(models.validate(&[live]).is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn a_builtin_catalog_validates_nothing() {
    let mut models = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::ImplementerSmall, row("codex:gpt-6-luna", Some("max"), None))].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    assert!(models.validate(&[catalog(&[("gpt-6-luna", &["low"], None)], CatalogSource::Builtin)]).is_empty());
    assert_eq!(models.choice(Role::ImplementerSmall).effort.as_deref(), Some("max"));
}

#[test]
fn rule_9_names_the_sizes_and_the_role_table() {
    let cfg = config_with(&[
        (Role::ImplementerSmall, row("codex:default", None, None)),
        (Role::ImplementerMedium, row("claude:claude-sonnet-5", None, None)),
    ]);
    let plan = plan_with(PROFILE, &[task_toml("s", "S", "[\"src/a.rs\"]", CHECK), task_toml("m", "M", "[\"src/**\"]", CHECK)]);
    let errors = show(&build_with(&plan, &cfg).unwrap_err());
    assert!(errors.contains("the role table runs size S on codex and size M on claude; give them the same size or separate owns (rule 9)"), "{errors}");
}
```

plus `a_run_from_before_the_role_table_gets_the_current_one` (`build_models::fill` on `run/delivery/m9_1_run.json`, checked in by an earlier milestone, loaded through `restore`: `limits.models` becomes `Some`, the log's last line is decision 9's text, and every task's route is unchanged), `the_test_writer_takes_its_row_unless_its_runtime_is_held` (a paired hub task with an unfinished overlapping task on Codex and `test_writer = codex:default`: the writer takes the task's own route; without the overlap, `codex:default`), `a_users_route_is_kept` (an `amend_task` with `EditSource::User` naming `claude:claude-opus-5-5` keeps it over the row), and, for review focus 5, `a_broken_repository_file_falls_back_to_the_global_table` (`build_models::freeze` with `<repo_dir>/models.toml` holding `[models.reviewer` gives the global table's rows and returns the warning `<path>: not valid TOML …` for the run log; the run builds).

**Change.** `RunModels` per Interfaces; `route_of` sets `strength: Strength::Standard` (comment per Interfaces). `driver/build_models.rs::freeze(global, project, data_dir, catalogs) -> (RunModels, Vec<String>)` reads `profile::repo_dir(data_dir, project).join(REPO_FILE)` with `config::models::load_repo` inside the start's existing `spawn_blocking` (never under the engine lock), resolves, validates against `ModelService::current()`, and calls `refresh_in_background` when that is empty (decision 20); its warnings and log lines go to the run log at build. Rule 9's text gains the sizes: find each task's size class name (`S`, `M`, `hub`) by its row.

**Run.**
- `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 cargo test -p anthrex-daemon --lib model_roles` → FAIL before, PASS after.
- `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 cargo test -p anthrex-daemon --lib run::` (the touched modules' tests; tests asserting a roster or list choice for a task's worker, reviewer, racer or writer are rewritten to the row they now take, each listed under "Implementation notes" with its old and new expectation).

**Acceptance.** The tests pass; `plan.rs` and `driver/build.rs` stay under 600; the five AGENTS.md commands pass.

**Commit.** `refactor(daemon): move plan.rs's for_repo out` (the move), then `feat(daemon): runs freeze the role table; workers, racers, writers and reviewers take their rows`

**Files.** Modify `run/orch/launch.rs` (`resolve_orchestrator` becomes `orchestrator_route(choice: Option<&OrchestratorChoice>, models: &RunModels) -> Resolved` with `source` `explicit_choice` or `role_table` and `candidates` = `[chosen]`; `scout_route_of`, `frozen_scout_route`, `planner_route` read the `research` and `planner` rows), `run/orch/role_lists.rs` (`brainstorm_picks_with`: the brainstorm row's two models, with today's unsaved rule (ruling T8-4): a model whose runtime cannot run unsaved is replaced by the other row model, both unusable → `None` (`UNSAVED_BRAINSTORMER`); labels as today; `review_pick`: the reviewer row against the orchestrator's route (decision 27), unsaved rule as today), `run/orch/roles.rs` (routing records: source `role_table`, policy `m9.8-roles-v1`), the goal start's call sites of `resolve_orchestrator` (`driver/adapt_goal.rs`, `driver/build_name.rs`, `run promote`'s path), `scout/spec.rs` (`ScoutRouting` replaced by the run's `research` row; the onboarding scout, which runs without a run, resolves `research` from the live table and the repository file in the profile service's own blocking start), `decider/mod.rs` and `decider/call.rs` (`DeciderContext.route` and `Routing` replaced by `live: Arc<LiveSettings>` and `data_dir`; `fn route_for(&self, kind: DeciderKind, project: Option<&Path>) -> Route` resolves `Role::Helper(kind)` from the live table and, with a project, its repository file (read inside the call's existing `spawn_blocking`); the mode's `Off` still turns helpers off; `routed` keeps moving a call to the other runtime only when its runtime is not installed **and** the row's fallback is on the other runtime (D2: never an unchosen model)), `profile/service.rs:447` (`DeciderContext::new` gets the live settings), `run/driver/*` call sites that pass a project to a decider.

**Tests first.**
- `run/orch/launch_tests.rs::the_orchestrator_takes_its_row_unless_the_goal_form_chose`: with `orchestrator = codex:gpt-6.1-sol, high`, `orchestrator_route(None, &models)` is that at `high`, source `role_table`; with `OrchestratorChoice { runtime: Claude, model: Some("claude-opus-5-5"), effort: Some("max") }` it is Opus at `max`, source `explicit_choice`; with `effort: None` and the row's own model chosen, the row's effort; with another model and `effort: None`, `Effort::DEFAULT`.

### M9.8.8 Escalation along the effort list, then the fallback

**Files.** Create `run/role_step.rs`, `run/role_step_tests.rs`. Modify `run/route_pick_step.rs` (`rung2_route`, `writer_step`, `escalate_for`: their bodies call `role_step::escalate` for the task's row, the test writer's row or the culprit's row, with `failed_routes(task)`; with `None`, the current route, as today when nothing is left), `run/engine/ladder.rs:281`, `engine/requests.rs:425`, `engine/actions/effects.rs:173-174`, `engine/bisect.rs:568`, `engine/delivery/fix.rs:313` (only their imports if the signatures hold), `run/roster.rs` (delete `escalate`, `escalate_skipping`, `strength_one_up`; their tests), `proto/src/effort.rs` (delete `raised`).

**Interfaces.** Consumes `RunModels.efforts`. Produces `role_step::escalate(models, role, current, failed) -> Option<Route>`.

**Tests first.** `run/role_step_tests.rs`:

```rust
use proto::{Effort, ModelRef, ModelTable, Role, RoleChoice, Route};
use super::escalate;
use crate::run::model_roles::{ModelEfforts, RunModels};

fn m(s: &str) -> ModelRef { ModelRef::parse(s).unwrap() }

fn models(model: &str, effort: Option<&str>, fallback: Option<&str>, lists: &[(&str, &[&str], Option<&str>)]) -> RunModels {
    let row = RoleChoice { model: m(model), effort: effort.map(Into::into), fallback: fallback.map(m) };
    let mut r = RunModels::resolve(&ModelTable { rows: [(Role::ImplementerMedium, row)].into(), brainstorm: None }, None);
    for (name, efforts, default) in lists {
        r.efforts.insert(m(name), ModelEfforts { efforts: efforts.iter().map(|e| e.to_string()).collect(), default: default.map(Into::into) });
    }
    r
}

fn at(model: &str, effort: &str) -> Route { RunModels::route_of(&m(model), (!effort.is_empty()).then_some(effort)) }

const SOL: &str = "codex:gpt-6-sol";
const OPUS: &str = "claude:claude-opus-5-5";

#[test]
fn effort_climbs_the_models_list_first() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("medium"), Some(OPUS), &[(SOL, &["low", "medium", "high", "max"], Some("medium"))]);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "medium"), &[]), Some(at(SOL, "high")));
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "high"), &[]), Some(at(SOL, "max")));
}

#[test]
fn at_the_top_it_switches_to_the_fallback_at_its_default_effort() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("medium"), Some(OPUS), &[(SOL, &["low", "medium", "high", "max"], Some("medium"))]);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "max"), &[]), Some(at(OPUS, "")));
}

#[test]
fn on_the_fallback_it_climbs_the_fallbacks_list_then_stays() {
    let r = models(SOL, None, Some(OPUS), &[(SOL, &["low"], None), (OPUS, &["low", "medium", "high"], Some("high"))]);
    // DEFAULT counts as the model's default effort (`high`), the top: nothing left.
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(OPUS, ""), &[]), None);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(OPUS, "medium"), &[]), Some(at(OPUS, "high")));
}

#[test]
fn without_a_fallback_it_stays_at_the_top() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("high"), None, &[(SOL, &["low", "medium", "high"], Some("medium"))]);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "high"), &[]), None);
}

#[test]
fn a_model_with_no_reported_efforts_goes_straight_to_the_fallback() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("medium"), Some(OPUS), &[]);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "medium"), &[]), Some(at(OPUS, "")));
}

#[test]
fn a_default_effort_with_no_model_default_starts_below_the_first() {
    let r = models(SOL, None, None, &[(SOL, &["low", "high"], None)]);
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, ""), &[]), Some(at(SOL, "low")));
}

#[test]
fn a_route_that_failed_in_the_task_is_stepped_over() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("high"), Some(OPUS), &[(SOL, &["low", "high"], None)]);
    // The model failed for an environment reason: no effort step helps it (RL-1).
    assert_eq!(escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &at(SOL, "low"), &[at(SOL, "low")]), Some(at(OPUS, "")));
}

#[test]
fn escalation_never_names_a_model_outside_the_row() {
    let r = models(SOL, Some("low"), Some(OPUS), &[(SOL, &["low", "high"], None), (OPUS, &["low", "high"], None)]);
    let mut route = at(SOL, "low");
    let mut seen = vec![route.clone()];
    while let Some(next) = escalate(&r, Role::ImplementerMedium, &route, &[]) {
        assert!(next.model == "gpt-6-sol" || next.model == "claude-opus-5-5", "{next:?}");
        seen.push(next.clone());
        route = next;
    }
    // Opus reports no default effort, so its DEFAULT sits below `low` (decision 29).
    assert_eq!(seen, [at(SOL, "low"), at(SOL, "high"), at(OPUS, ""), at(OPUS, "low"), at(OPUS, "high")]);
}
```

And an engine-level test, `engine/tests/ladder_roles.rs::rung_two_raises_the_effort_then_takes_the_fallback`, through the existing ladder fixture used by `engine/tests/route_lists.rs` (the same `Fx` setup, with `limits.models` set from the rows above): a task stalls twice; its second session runs `gpt-6-sol` at `high`, its third `claude-opus-5-5` at the default; `Effort::raised` no longer exists (`cargo build` proves it).

### M9.8.9 The orchestrator sizes; it never routes

**Files.** Modify `run/edits.rs` and `run/edits_amend.rs` (an `add_task`, `split_task` or `amend_task` from `EditSource::{Orchestrator, Planner}` clears its `route` before resolution; a cleared route that named anything adds `EditConsequence::RouteIgnored` once per batch, which `engine/orch.rs::edit_plan` and the planner's edit path log with `requests::log` as `ROUTE_IGNORED`), `run/plan.rs::build_run` (clears every plan task's route; one `ROUTE_IGNORED` line in `run.log` when any named something), `run/orch/contract.rs` (rules 4, 21, 22; the sub-planner's rule 7; `ROUTE_IGNORED` lives here as `pub const ROUTE_IGNORED: &str = "route model ignored: models come from the role table";`), `run/orch/context.rs` (`roles` replaces `roster` and `limits.routes`, decision 34), `run/orch/tools_bounds.rs` (`Rule::Route`, decision 32), `mcp/src/tools_orch.rs::route` and `mcp/src/fixtures/orch_tools.json` (regenerated with the existing fixture test's record mode), and the test suite's plan routes (below).

**Contract text** (exact):

- Rule 4's "the models you can route to" becomes "the role table (which model each size runs on)".
- Rule 21: `21. Size every task; never route it. Each size runs on the model the user chose for it in the role table (get_context's roles): S tasks on implementer.small, M tasks on implementer.medium, hub tasks on implementer.hub. A route you send is ignored.`
- Rule 22: `22. Tasks whose sizes run on different runtimes (get_context's roles shows each size's runtime) must not have overlapping owns: the engine rejects it. A task on the critical path that is not a hub can set race to true: a second worker builds it at once on the size's "if it struggles" model, or the same model when none is set, and the first to pass every gate wins. It costs twice the tokens and twice the test slots, so use it only where finishing sooner matters.`
- The sub-planner's rule 7: its sentences "Set route on every task, and never give tasks on different runtimes overlapping owns. Model lists are set by the user; leave route empty to use them, or name one of the listed models." become "Never set route: each size's model comes from the user's role table, and tasks whose sizes run on different runtimes must not have overlapping owns."

**The test suite's plan routes.** A plan-file route no longer picks a runtime, so every test that relied on one moves to the role table. In `cli/tests/support/run_plans.rs`, delete `CODEX` (so every use fails to compile and is visited) and add:

```rust
/// Milestone 9.8: the harness config that runs M tasks on Codex (a plan's route is
/// ignored, MR §3.6). Use with `RunHarness::with_config("", CODEX_MEDIUM, &[])`.
pub const CODEX_MEDIUM: &str = "[models.implementer.medium]\nmodel = \"codex:default\"\n";

/// [`task`] at size M: with [`CODEX_MEDIUM`], a Codex task.
pub fn task_m(id: &str, owns: &[&str], extra: &str) -> String {
    task(id, owns, extra).replacen("size = \"S\"", "size = \"M\"", 1)
}
```

**Files.** Create `tui/src/app/models_table.rs`, `app/model_picker.rs` (filling out M9.8.4's `Catalogs`), `app/models_keys.rs`, and their tests `app/models_table_tests.rs`, `app/model_picker_tests.rs`. Modify `tui/src/app/settings_screen.rs` (`SettingsSection::{Models, Limits}`; `SettingsScreen.models: ModelsTable` replaces `claude`, `codex`, `runtime`, `model`; `doc()` puts `models.global` into `SettingsDoc.roles`; `dirty()` compares it), `app/settings_keys.rs` (the `Models` section's keys go to `models_keys.rs`), `app/settings_flow.rs` (opening sends `ListModels { runtime: None, refresh: false }` when `App.catalogs.list` is empty; the repository scope's `RepoModels`/`PutRepoModels` requests and their replies, by `request_id`, through `route_settings_reply`), `app/daemon.rs` (`DaemonMsg::Models` replaces `App.catalogs.list` per runtime and refreshes an open picker's entries, keeping its selection on the same model). **Interfaces.**

```rust
// app/model_picker.rs
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Catalogs { pub list: Vec<ModelCatalog> }
impl Catalogs {
    pub fn of(&self, runtime: Runtime) -> Option<&ModelCatalog>;
    /// `Claude · Opus 5.5`: the runtime name, padded to 6 when `pad`, then the
    /// catalog's label, else the id, else `default`; cleaned (decision 38).
    pub fn label(&self, model: &ModelRef, pad: bool) -> String;
    /// The model's efforts as the catalog reports them (empty when unknown).
    pub fn efforts(&self, model: &ModelRef) -> Vec<String>;
    /// MR §4.4: `not reported by codex 0.160.1` when the runtime's catalog is Live or
    /// Cached and does not list the model.
    pub fn unreported(&self, model: &ModelRef) -> Option<String>;
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PickerFor { Row(Role), Fallback(Role), Brainstorm(u8), Goal, TaskEdit }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PickerEntry {
    /// `CLAUDE  (claude 2.1.290)`, `(cached)`, `(built-in list)`, or `codex not found`.
    Header { runtime: Runtime, text: String, missing: bool },
    Model { model: ModelRef, label: String, description: String, efforts: String, current: bool },
    /// `f`'s first entry.
    NoFallback,
    /// The goal and task edit forms' first entry: no choice.
    RoleTable(String),
    Custom,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Picked { Model(ModelRef), NoFallback, RoleTable, Refresh, Cancel }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ModelPicker {
    pub target: PickerFor,
    pub entries: Vec<PickerEntry>,
    /// An index into `entries`; never a header or a missing runtime's row.
    pub selected: usize,
    /// `custom…`'s two steps: the runtime, then the name.
    pub custom: Option<CustomModel>,
}

impl ModelPicker {
    pub fn new(target: PickerFor, catalogs: &Catalogs, current: Option<&ModelRef>, first: Option<PickerEntry>) -> ModelPicker;
    pub fn on_key(&mut self, key: KeyEvent) -> Option<Picked>;
}

// app/models_table.rs
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Scope { Everywhere, Repo }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum RowKey { Role(Role), Brainstorm }

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct TableRow {
    pub key: RowKey,
    pub role: String,      // `implementer · medium`, `helpers ▸`, `  run name`
    pub model: String,     // `Codex  · gpt-6 sol`, or `same as helpers`
    pub effort: String,    // `medium`, `—`
    pub fallback: String,  // `Claude · Opus 5.5`, `—`
    /// `this repo` scope: the repository overrides this row (`●`).
    pub overridden: bool,
    /// `this repo` scope: inherited, drawn dimmed with `(everywhere)`.
    pub inherited: bool,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ModelsTable {
    pub scope: Scope,
    pub global: ModelTable,
    pub repo: Option<RepoTable>,
    pub project: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub selected: usize,
    pub helpers_open: bool,
    pub picker: Option<ModelPicker>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RepoTable { pub table: ModelTable, pub base: ModelTable, pub path: PathBuf, pub put_id: Option<u64> }

impl ModelsTable {
    pub fn rows(&self, catalogs: &Catalogs) -> Vec<TableRow>;
    /// The table the scope edits.
    pub fn editing(&self) -> &ModelTable;
}
```

**Keys** (`models_keys.rs`): `j`/`k` and `↓`/`↑` move; `←`/`→` switch the scope (`this repo` only with a project; entering it the first time sends `RepoModels`); `space` on `helpers ▸` opens or closes the six kinds; `⏎` opens the picker (`PickerFor::Row`, or `Brainstorm(0|1)`: `⏎` on the brainstorm row picks its first, then its second); `f` the picker with `NoFallback` first; `e` cycles the row model's catalog efforts then `default` (no-op with `—`); `x` removes the scope's row (`this repo`: the override; `everywhere`: back to the built-in); `w` saves the scope (M9.8.12); `tab` goes to `limits`; `esc` leaves (the existing discard confirm). In the picker: `j`/`k` skip headers and missing runtimes; `⏎` picks; `r` returns `Picked::Refresh` (the app sends `ListModels { runtime: None, refresh: true }` and keeps the picker open); `esc` cancels; `custom…` asks `runtime: ‹ claude › codex` then `name:` and accepts only what `ModelRef::parse` accepts, showing its error.

**Tests first.** `app/models_table_tests.rs` builds an `App` on the Settings screen as `ui/settings_tests.rs::opened` does, with a `SettingsDoc` whose `roles` is the spec's table (MR §5.1: `small = codex:gpt-6-luna low, fallback codex:gpt-6-sol`; `medium = codex:gpt-6-sol medium, fallback claude:claude-opus-5-5`; `hub = claude:claude-opus-5-5 high, fallback codex:gpt-6.1-sol`; `test_writer = codex:gpt-6-sol medium`; `reviewer = codex:gpt-6.1-sol high, fallback claude:claude-opus-5-5`; `research = claude:claude-sonnet-5 medium`; `brainstorm = claude:claude-opus-5-5 + codex:gpt-6.1-sol`; `helpers = claude:claude-haiku-4-5`) and `App.catalogs` from `fake-agent`'s fixture models (Claude 2.1.290, Codex 0.160.1, both `Live`):

- `rows_follow_the_spec_order_with_catalog_labels`: `rows()` gives, by `(role, model, effort, fallback)`: `orchestrator / Claude · Opus 5.5 / high / —`, `planner` the same, `implementer · small / Codex  · gpt-6 luna / low / Codex · gpt-6 sol`, `implementer · medium / Codex  · gpt-6 sol / medium / Claude · Opus 5.5`, `implementer · hub / Claude · Opus 5.5 / high / Codex · gpt-6.1 sol`, `test writer / Codex  · gpt-6 sol / medium / —`, `reviewer / Codex  · gpt-6.1 sol / high / Claude · Opus 5.5`, `research / Claude · Sonnet 5 / medium / —`, `brainstorm / Claude · Opus 5.5  +  Codex · gpt-6.1 sol / high / ` (empty), `helpers ▸ / Claude · Haiku 4.5 / — / —`.
- `helpers_expand_to_six_kinds_same_as_helpers_until_set`: `space` on `helpers ▸` → it reads `helpers ▾`, followed by `  run name`, `  triage`, `  size check`, `  check summary`, `  blocked reason`, `  ci summary`, each model `same as helpers`; after picking `claude:claude-sonnet-5` on `  run name`, that row shows `Claude · Sonnet 5` and the doc's `rows[Helper(RunName)]` is set.
- `this_repo_scope_marks_overrides_and_dims_inherited`: `→` sends one `SettingsRequest::RepoModels { project }` (`goal_project()`); the reply's table overrides `reviewer`; `rows()` has `overridden` only on `reviewer` and `inherited` on every other row; `x` on `reviewer` removes the override (`dirty()`); `x` in `everywhere` on `implementer · small` resets it to the built-in (`Claude · Sonnet 5 / low`).
- `e_cycles_the_models_efforts_and_default`: on `implementer · small` (`gpt-6 luna`: `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`), `e` × 5 gives `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`, `default` (shown `—`), `low`; on `helpers ▸` (`Haiku 4.5`, no efforts) `e` changes nothing.

### M9.8.11 TUI: drawing the table and the picker; the goal and task edit forms

**Files.** Create `tui/src/ui/models_table.rs`, `ui/model_picker.rs`, `ui/models_table_tests.rs`, `ui/model_picker_tests.rs`, `tui/src/run_edit_tests_models.rs`. Modify `tui/src/ui/settings.rs` (the `models` section draws through `models_table::render`; the `claude`, `codex`, `orchestrator` drawing goes), `ui/settings_tests.rs` (its section tests rewritten to `models`/`limits`), `tui/src/run_goal.rs`, `ui/run_goal.rs`, `app/goal.rs` (decision 39), `tui/src/run_edit.rs`, `run_edit_cycle.rs`, `ui/run_edit.rs` (decision 39), the help screen's Settings and form entries (`app/help.rs`).

**Drawing** (exact, MR §5.1 and §5.2):

- The table's header row: ` ROLE                  MODEL                   EFFORT   IF IT STRUGGLES`. Each row: one marker column (`▸` selected, `●` overridden in `this repo`, else a space), then the role padded to 22, the model padded to 24, the effort padded to 9, the fallback; cut at the interior's width with `…`. The model column pads the runtime name to 6 (`Codex  · gpt-6 sol`); the fallback column does not (`Codex · gpt-6 sol`). The brainstorm row's model is `<first>  +  <second>` (fallback column empty). Inherited rows in `this repo` are drawn in `Role::Muted` with ` (everywhere)` after the model.
- Above it: ` models · scope ‹ everywhere › / this repo (<basename>)`, the current scope between `‹ ›`, and `tab: limits` right-aligned; a blank row. Below it: a blank row, then every row's warnings as `⚠ <role>: <warning text without its ⚠>` in `Role::Attention`, then the footer ` ⏎ choose model   e effort   f if-it-struggles   x reset   w save   esc back`, entries dropped from the right until it fits.
- The picker, drawn as a kit dialog over the screen: title ` choose model · <role label>` and, right-aligned, `r refresh · updated <age> ago` (the age from the app's tick clock, the one toasts age by, in `s`/`m`/`h`); per runtime a header `  CLAUDE  (claude 2.1.290)`; each model `  ● ` or `    `, the label padded to 17, the description padded to 38, then its efforts (`effort —` with none); `  ─────`; `    custom…   type any model name`; the footer ` j/k move  ⏎ select  r refresh  esc cancel`. (MR §5.2's mock spaces its two blocks one apart; these widths are the rule.)
- The status line after a save: `saved · new runs use these models · runs in progress keep theirs` (replaces `SAVED`).

**Tests first.** `ui/models_table_tests.rs`, with M9.8.10's fixture App and the existing `rows`/`head` helpers:

```rust
#[test]
fn the_table_draws_as_the_spec_at_80x24_and_120x40() {
    let mut app = opened_models(spec_roles(), fixture_catalogs());
    select(&mut app, Role::ImplementerMedium);
    for (w, h) in SIZES {
        let interior = rows(&app, w, h);
        assert!(interior[0].starts_with(" models · scope ‹ everywhere › / this repo (repo)"), "{w}x{h}: {interior:?}");
        assert!(interior[0].ends_with("tab: limits"), "{w}x{h}");
        assert_eq!(
            interior[2..14],
            [
                " ROLE                  MODEL                   EFFORT   IF IT STRUGGLES",
                " orchestrator          Claude · Opus 5.5       high     —",
                " planner               Claude · Opus 5.5       high     —",
                " implementer · small   Codex  · gpt-6 luna     low      Codex · gpt-6 sol",
                "▸implementer · medium  Codex  · gpt-6 sol      medium   Claude · Opus 5.5",
                " implementer · hub     Claude · Opus 5.5       high     Codex · gpt-6.1 sol",
                " test writer           Codex  · gpt-6 sol      medium   —",
                " reviewer              Codex  · gpt-6.1 sol    high     Claude · Opus 5.5",
                " research              Claude · Sonnet 5       medium   —",
                " brainstorm            Claude · Opus 5.5  +  Codex · gpt-6.1 sol",
                " helpers ▸             Claude · Haiku 4.5      —        —",
                "",
            ],
            "{w}x{h}"
        );
        assert_eq!(interior.last().unwrap(), " ⏎ choose model   e effort   f if-it-struggles   x reset   w save   esc back");
    }
}
```

plus `this_repo_scope_draws_overrides_and_inherited_rows` (`●reviewer …`, inherited rows end with ` (everywhere)` and are drawn in `Role::Muted`, checked through the buffer's cell styles as `settings_tests.rs` does), `warnings_are_listed_under_the_table` (`⚠ implementer · small: effort 'max' not offered`, `⚠ research: not reported by claude 2.1.290`), and `ui/model_picker_tests.rs::the_picker_draws_as_the_spec` (the lines of MR §5.2 with this brief's widths, at 80×24 and 120×40; at 80 columns the description is cut with `…`), `a_hostile_label_draws_clean` (review focus 3: no cell holds `\u{1b}` or `\u{202e}`, and the 500-character description ends in `…` inside the dialog).

### M9.8.12 Saving: `[models]` written, old keys removed, the repository file

**Files.** Create `config/src/settings/write_models.rs`, `write_models_tests.rs`. Create `cli/tests/run_e2e_settings_models.rs`.

**Tests first.** `config/src/settings/write_models_tests.rs`:

```rust
use crate::models::{ModelRef, Role, RoleChoice};
use crate::settings::{doc_of, save};

const OLD: &str = r#"# my settings
prefix = "a"

[orchestrator]
default_runtime = "codex"
builtin_models = true
max_writers = 3 # keep this comment

[orchestrator.agent]
runtime = "claude"
model = "claude-opus-5-5"
effort = "high"

[orchestrator.planners]
strength = "frontier"
max_tool_calls = 300

[orchestrator.deciders]
mode = "claude"
effort = "low"

[[orchestrator.models]]
runtime = "codex"
model = "gpt-6-sol"
strength = "standard"

[orchestrator.routes.review]
candidates = [{ runtime = "codex", model = "gpt-6-sol" }]

[testing]
"#;

#[test]
fn a_save_writes_models_and_removes_every_old_key() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("config.toml");
    std::fs::write(&path, OLD).unwrap();
    let before = crate::parse(OLD).0.orchestrator;
    let doc = doc_of(&before);
    save(&path, &doc, &Default::default()).unwrap();
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    for gone in ["default_runtime", "builtin_models", "[orchestrator.agent]", "strength", "[[orchestrator.models]]", "[orchestrator.routes.review]", "mode = \"claude\"\neffort"] {
        assert!(!text.contains(gone), "{gone} is still there:\n{text}");
    }
    for kept in ["# my settings", "prefix = \"a\"", "max_writers = 3 # keep this comment", "max_tool_calls = 300", "[orchestrator.deciders]\nmode = \"claude\"\n", "[testing]"] {
        assert!(text.contains(kept), "{kept} was lost:\n{text}");
    }
    let after = crate::parse(&text).0.orchestrator;
    assert!(after.roles_notes.is_empty(), "{:?}", after.roles_notes);
    assert_eq!(after.roles, before.roles, "the saved table is the migrated one");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join("config.toml.bak")).unwrap(), OLD);
}

#[test]
fn saving_keeps_every_resolved_row() {
    // Review focus 1: old keys and an explicit [models] row for the same role.
    let text = format!("{OLD}\n[models.reviewer]\nmodel = \"claude:claude-opus-5-5\"\n");
    let before = crate::parse(&text).0.orchestrator;
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("config.toml");
    std::fs::write(&path, &text).unwrap();
    save(&path, &doc_of(&before), &Default::default()).unwrap();
    let after = crate::parse(&std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap()).0.orchestrator;
    for role in Role::all() {
        assert_eq!(crate::models::resolve(role, None, &after.roles), crate::models::resolve(role, None, &before.roles), "{role:?}");
    }
    assert_eq!(crate::models::resolve_brainstorm(None, &after.roles), crate::models::resolve_brainstorm(None, &before.roles));
}

#[test]
fn a_save_with_no_old_key_writes_no_bak() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("config.toml");
    std::fs::write(&path, "[models.planner]\nmodel = \"codex:default\"\n").unwrap();
    let mut o = crate::parse("[models.planner]\nmodel = \"codex:default\"\n").0.orchestrator;
    o.roles.rows.insert(Role::Research, RoleChoice { model: ModelRef::parse("claude:claude-sonnet-5").unwrap(), effort: Some("medium".into()), fallback: None });
    save(&path, &doc_of(&o), &Default::default()).unwrap();
    assert!(!dir.path().join("config.toml.bak").exists());
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
    assert!(text.contains("[models.research]\nmodel = \"claude:claude-sonnet-5\"\neffort = \"medium\"\n"), "{text}");
}

#[test]
fn a_models_key_the_writer_cannot_edit_refuses() {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let path = dir.path().join("config.toml");
    let text = "models = { planner = { model = \"codex:default\" } }\n";
    std::fs::write(&path, text).unwrap();
    let o = crate::parse(text).0.orchestrator;
    let problems = save(&path, &doc_of(&o), &Default::default()).unwrap_err();
    assert!(problems.iter().any(|p| p.starts_with("models is written in a form the settings screen does not edit")), "{problems:?}");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), text);
}
```

(`save`'s third argument is the existing `&AtomicBool` cancel flag; `&Default::default()` builds an unset one.) Daemon: `run/driver/settings_tests.rs::a_repository_table_is_saved_beside_the_profile` (`PutRepoModels` writes `profile::repo_dir(data_dir, project).join("models.toml")`; `RepoModels` reads it back; an empty table removes it; an invalid row is `Refused`). `cli/tests/run_e2e_settings_models.rs::saving_models_rewrites_the_config_and_new_runs_use_them`: a harness config with `[orchestrator.routes.s]` (one Claude Sonnet candidate) and `[[orchestrator.models]]`; a `Settings(Put)` whose `roles` sets `implementer.small = claude:claude-opus-5-5, high`; `config.toml` then has `[models.implementer.small]` and no `[orchestrator.routes.s]`, `config.toml.bak` holds the old text, and a plan run's `S` worker argv has `--model claude-opus-5-5 --effort high`.

**Run.**
- `cargo test -p anthrex-config settings::` → FAIL before, PASS after.
- `cargo build -p anthrex-fake-agent && GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 cargo test -p anthrex --test run_e2e_settings_models --test run_e2e_settings --test run_e2e_settings_live` → PASS.
