```bash
grep -nE "std::(fs|process|net|thread)|tokio::|Command::new|SystemTime" crates/proto/src/models.rs crates/proto/src/effort.rs crates/config/src/models/mod.rs crates/config/src/models/parse.rs crates/config/src/models/migrate.rs crates/daemon/src/run/model_roles.rs crates/daemon/src/run/role_step.rs crates/daemon/src/models/builtin.rs
grep -nE "std::(fs|process|net)|tokio::" crates/tui/src/app/models_table.rs crates/tui/src/app/model_picker.rs crates/tui/src/app/models_keys.rs crates/tui/src/ui/models_table.rs crates/tui/src/ui/model_picker.rs
# No new kill or signal code: only the moved ProbeChild and the test's signal-0 check.
git diff origin/main...HEAD -- crates/daemon/src | grep -E "^\+.*(signal_group|libc::kill|SIGKILL|SIGTERM|SIGHUP)"
# No probe under a lock.
grep -n "crate::lock" crates/daemon/src/models/*.rs   # only the memory map's clone and replace
# Nothing picks a model but the role table.
grep -rnE "first_at|lowest_at_or_above|strongest_of|pick_reviewer|peer_route|route_lists|class_routes|\bStrength\b" crates --include='*.rs' | grep -v "config/src/models/migrate.rs\|config/src/orchestrator/roster.rs"
# No test reaches a real agent.
grep -rnE "ANTHREX_(CLAUDE|CODEX|DECIDER)_BIN\"?, *\"(claude|codex)\"" crates/*/tests scripts
```

And `wc -l` of every file in "File sizes" and every new file, recorded under "Implementation notes"; none above 600. `pgrep -fl "anthrex daemon"` shows nothing of the implementer's.

## Manual check

**Done by the user, not the implementer** (MR §8: it runs the real CLIs, so it needs the user's approval). It sends no prompt: the probes write only `initialize` and `model/list`. On macOS and on nexus1 (`ssh nexus1`, commands through `bash -lc`), with `ANTHREX_SOCKET` and `ANTHREX_DATA_DIR` under `/tmp` and the real `claude` and `codex` on `PATH` (no `ANTHREX_*_BIN` override):

1. `anthrex` (TUI), `C-b S`: the table shows; `⏎` on `implementer · medium` opens the picker; both CLIs are listed with their versions, their own model names, descriptions and effort ranges; `r` refreshes and `updated` resets.
2. Note the models and efforts each CLI reported, and copy `<ANTHREX_DATA_DIR>/models/*.raw.jsonl` (decision 26). Compare their field names and shapes with `crates/fake-agent/fixtures/`; where they differ, open a follow-up to change the fixtures and the probes' parsing (MR §4.1's last bullet), with the raw files attached.
3. Pick a model, cycle its effort, `w` in `everywhere` and in `this repo`; check `config.toml`, `config.toml.bak` (when old keys existed) and `<data_dir>/repos/<repo>-<hash8>/models.toml`.
4. Start no run. End with `anthrex daemon stop` under the same variables; `pgrep -fl "anthrex daemon"` shows nothing; `pgrep -fl "claude -p\|codex app-server"` shows nothing anthrex started.

Recorded under "Implementation notes" by whoever runs it, with each CLI's version and the raw files' location.

## Review focus

The inputs and conditions MR implies that the tasks' main tests do not exercise, most likely to bite first. Each has its test in the owning task.

1. **A config with both old keys and an explicit `[models]` row for the same role**: saving must not change which model any role resolves to (M9.8.12 `saving_keeps_every_resolved_row`).
2. **A CLI that prints banners, warnings or non-JSON before its reply, or one endless line**: the probe skips non-JSON lines, caps a line at 1 MiB and the reply at 4 MiB, and never hangs past its deadline (M9.8.6 `claude_probe_reads_the_initialize_reply_and_sends_no_prompt`, `an_endless_reply_line_ends_the_probe`, `a_probe_past_its_deadline_is_killed_reaped_and_reported`).
3. **Hostile text from a CLI** (control characters, bidi overrides, an id with spaces, a 500-character description): ids that are not a `ModelRef` are dropped and never reach argv; labels and descriptions are drawn clean and cut (M9.8.6 `a_model_id_that_is_not_a_model_ref_is_dropped`, M9.8.10 `a_hostile_catalog_is_kept_out_of_the_entries_text`, M9.8.11 `a_hostile_label_draws_clean`).
4. **Two `ListModels` at once** (`r` pressed twice, two TUIs): one probe per runtime at a time, no extra CLI processes (M9.8.6 `two_concurrent_lists_probe_once`).
5. **A broken `models.toml` in a repository**: the run starts on the global table with a warning in its log (M9.8.7a `a_broken_repository_file_falls_back_to_the_global_table`).

Also for the reviewer: the golden file is a constructed input (the review-failure pattern "defects come from constructed inputs"): check that every cell changed after its first run has its code path recorded; check `ProbeChild`'s move commit byte for byte; check every move-only commit's test count.

## Risks and gotchas

- **The real handshakes may differ from the documented fields.** The fixtures follow the public SDK and app-server docs; the manual check records the real replies (decision 26) and any difference becomes a follow-up. The probes parse only the named fields and skip what they do not know.
- **`claude -p` with stream-json input may wait for a first user message before answering `initialize` on some versions.** The probe never sends one; such a CLI times out, and the catalog falls back to `Cached` or `Builtin` with `claude did not answer within 10 s`. The manual check is where this would show.
- **`codex app-server` may print to stderr or start slowly on first run**; stderr is null and the deadline is 10 s.
- **A large test-suite migration in M9.8.9.** Deleting `CODEX` forces every user to be visited; the review compares the per-test list in "Implementation notes" with the grep.
- **Runs in flight across the upgrade** keep their tasks' routes and get today's table at restore (decision 9); their racers, writers and reviewers from then on follow the table.
- **Hub reviews change model** (decision 12) and **the racer may run the same model** (decision 13): both by MR's design; CHANGELOG says so.

## Spec and brief defects

Each is resolved by the decision named, with the smallest change; none changes an MR decision (D1–D9).

1. **MR §6.1's `research` built-in differs from today** (Sonnet/medium vs today's scouts' Haiku/low). MR asks for the correction: decision 8.
2. **MR §8's golden test cannot compare one reviewer row with today's three review levels**: decision 12 (the medium level).
3. **MR §3.1 changes the racer on purpose**, so it cannot be in a "same models" test: decision 13.
4. **MR §6.2 does not derive `test_writer` and `reviewer`**, but MR §1 and §8 need them to stay the same under `default_runtime = "codex"`: decision 15.
5. **`default_runtime` with no model at a class's strength** fails validation today; MR is silent: decision 16.
6. **`[orchestrator.deciders] mode`** is both an on/off switch and today's helper runtime; MR §6.2 lists "deciders runtime", which does not exist: decision 17.
7. **MR §3.2's effort string vs `proto::Effort`'s three-value enum** in every route on the wire and in `run.json`: decision 5 (a string newtype that decodes the old values).
8. **MR §3.5 does not say what happens once on the fallback**: decision 29 (its own effort list, then stay).
9. **MR §4.2's `ModelCatalog` cannot say "codex not found"**, which MR §5.2 draws: decision 24 (`problem`).
10. **MR names no wire message for the repository scope's read and save**: decision 36 (`RepoModels`, `PutRepoModels`).
11. **MR does not say when probes run**: decision 20 (lazily, never at daemon start, never blocking a start).
12. **Rule 9 and contract rule 22 assume the orchestrator picks runtimes**: decision 33 keeps rule 9 on the rows' runtimes and rewrites rule 22.
13. **MR §6.2's "keeps the previous file as config.toml.bak"** does not say when: decision 40 (each save that removes an old key).
14. **MR §3.2 places `RoleChoice` nowhere**; it must cross the wire: decision 1 (`proto::models`).
15. **The old `[orchestrator.routes]` lists' `pick = "spread"`** has no equivalent in a row (MR D1): migration takes the first candidate as the model and the next as the fallback, `spread` included (decision 14); its round-robin is gone.
16. **MR §6.3 removes `routes.rs` and the roster's strength, yet keeps the old keys parseable for migration**: their readers stay (`routes.rs::read_routes`, `roster.rs::read_models` with a config-private `LegacyStrength`), feeding only `migrate.rs`; everything that resolved a model from them goes (M9.8.13, M9.8.14).

## Implementation notes

Each task records here what it found: the fact check, every golden cell changed and why, every test rewritten or deleted with its old and new expectation, test counts around move-only commits, file sizes, and the manual check when it is run. Notes a later task superseded are marked so in place, not deleted.

### Summary of deviations

One line per real deviation from this brief, tasks M9.8.1 to M9.8.15; the detail is under each task below.

- **M9.8.1**: the golden test builds through `build_tuned(.., refit::tuned(..))`, not `build_with`, since a run's lists come only from `Tuned` (M9.8.1, "Build helper").
- **M9.8.2**: `proto::models::Role` is not re-exported at the proto root (it would clash with the conversation's `Role`); `[models.helpers]` and `[models.implementer]` are containers unless they set a row key (M9.8.2).
- **M9.8.3**: an old key present with an invalid value still feeds its row; notes follow the parsed (alphabetical) key order (M9.8.3).
- **M9.8.4**: `variant_names` is `delivery::tests::variant_names`; the settings-roles round trips live in `design_tests_agents.rs`; three `clippy::large_enum_variant` allows (M9.8.4).
- **M9.8.6**: `ProbeChild`'s fields are `pub(crate)` (F23, the user's approval); `ModelService::list` is a plain `fn` returning a future (F27); new `EXIT_GRACE`; the probe applies a headless session's whole environment (F28, F29); the read loop is duplicated (F30) (M9.8.6).
- **M9.8.7a**: `runtime_open` holds a lane off for a task on *another* runtime (the code's reading, not decision 28's wording); an effort-only route is kept; rule 9 reads `planned_runtime`; the start's repository read has its own bounded `spawn_blocking` and `models_log`; reviewers launch from `engine/review.rs` and `reach.rs` counts rows (files the brief left out); a user's route with no model is refused (M9.8.7a and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.7b**: a missing orchestrator runtime refuses the start instead of stepping to the peer; an explicit orchestrator model outside the catalog is accepted; `DeciderContext.route` stays as the per-call route; a runtime-only orchestrator choice takes the row, else the runtime's built-in (I2); `below_frontier` removed; MR §7's role-naming error is not applied to the orchestrator's PTY window (M9.8.7b and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.8**: a route outside the row climbs its own list, then the row's fallback; escalation keeps the overlap rule and the installed skip; built-in effort lists fill a runtime with no catalog; the decider's S→M raise takes the medium row; fix tasks' route specs name no strength (M9.8.8 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.9**: `ROUTE_IGNORED` is logged in `apply_batch` for every source; decision 32's "values unchecked" keeps the model length bound; ignored routes are made readable before serde (`ignored_route.rs`) (M9.8.9 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.10**: `Catalogs.received_at` (F10) and `CustomModel.naming` (F36); a current model no catalog lists is its own picker entry (M9.8.10 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.11**: ` (everywhere)` closes the row and is cut at 80 columns; the picker is `min(74, max(64, width − 16))` wide; a user's model with no effort runs at `DEFAULT`, the row's own model at the row's effort, and `TaskInfo.row` was added within protocol 19 (M9.8.11 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.12**: no `toml_edit` (a line-based writer); a list none of whose models is known is kept on save with a note; `RepoModels` carries unreadable rows' problems and `PutRepoModels` refuses while they exist; an existing `config.toml.bak` is never replaced (supersedes decision 40); the `OLD` fixture's review candidate is `claude-sonnet-5` (M9.8.12 and its fix rounds).
- **M9.8.13**: `repicks` deleted, not moved; old `run.json` fields removed outright (serde skips them); `ClassTuning.route` and `TuningReport.orchestrator_list` left the wire; a user's model and the orchestrator choice must pass the name rule, which now refuses a leading `-` (M9.8.13 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.14**: `Orchestrator.models` stays `pub` over a private `LegacyModel` (no `note`); a stage fix's route is the approved route with the highest effort; the acceptance grep also hits the config crate's own tests (M9.8.14 and its fix round 1).
- **M9.8.15**: the end-to-end table adds `[models.implementer.hub]` and a sub-planner (F21); the two acceptance tests passed on their first run (F39); Task 11's two parked minors fixed (M9.8.15).

### M9.8.1 (golden test)

- **Fact check** at `abb9ee4c` (PR #49 merged): `PROTO_VERSION = 18` (`proto/src/lib.rs:132`), `HISTORY_VERSION = 5`, `recorded_version` as named, every "File sizes" count as written; the "Starting point" line drift equals preflight S9–S11 (F44) plus `SettingsDoc` at `:80`. Names all unchanged.
- **Build helper.** The brief's `build_with` passes `Tuned::default()`, whose `lists` is empty, while a real start freezes the config's lists through `refit::tuned`; with it 9 of the `routes` config's 11 cells came out as the default's. `one_task` builds with `test_support::build_tuned(&plan, cfg, refit::tuned(&TuningFile::default(), cfg))`, what a start with no history freezes. The code was wrong, not the JSON: **no golden cell changed**. `FrozenList` is imported from `crate::run::model` (the brief's `model_tuning` path does not compile).

### M9.8.2 (role types, resolution and the repository file)

- **`proto::models::Role` is not re-exported at the crate root**: `proto::Role` is the conversation's. Later tasks spell it `proto::models::Role` (or `config::models::Role`).
- `settings/save.rs`'s `temp_path` and `write_temp` became `pub(crate)` for `save_repo` (visibility only). One extra test, `an_empty_config_has_an_empty_role_table`.
- A repository file's top-level key other than `models` is an unknown-key warning. `[models.helpers]` is a row only when it sets `model`, `effort` or `fallback`; `[models.implementer]` is only a container. A bad effort or fallback drops the whole row; an unknown key inside a row is reported and the row kept.

### M9.8.3 (migrating the old keys)

- `orchestrator_tests_adapt::adapt_wrong_types_are_problems` amended: an invalid `scouts.strength`/`scouts.effort` is present, so it feeds the research row (at its defaults) with two notes (decisions 14 and 18: "present in the file").
- The `default_runtime` note names the three implementer rows; decision 16's notes follow it. Notes follow the parsed table's key order, which is alphabetical (the workspace's `toml` has no `preserve_order`).
- With the medium class unresolved, no test writer or reviewer row is derived (both keep the built-in). A `review` list's qualifying candidate is the model, the next listed one the fallback. An `agent.model` outside the roster is kept as named.

### M9.8.4 (protocol 19)

- The brief's `crate::delivery_tests_wire::variant_names` is `crate::delivery::tests::variant_names`. The settings document's `roles` tests (decode without, round trip, F26) live in `design_tests_agents.rs`, where `a_settings_doc` is.
- `#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]` (commented) on the TUI's `Effect`, `GoalOutcome` and `IterateOutcome`: `ClientMsg`/`RunRequest` grew past clippy's threshold; boxing would have changed the Interfaces' types.
- The CLI client swallows `DaemonMsg::Models` (the daemon never pushes it unsolicited). `Effort`'s `Debug` prints `Effort("medium")`.

### M9.8.5 (fake-agent: both discovery handshakes)

- Extra tests beyond the brief's (unknown method, empty mode, hang, version default). An unknown Codex cursor answers JSON-RPC error `-32602`; pages follow the previous page's `nextCursor`. `FAKE_AGENT_VERSION` replaces the whole `--version` line (F8).

### M9.8.6 (discovery)

- **`ProbeChild` move (preflight F23, the user's approval).** Its own commit, `refactor(daemon): move ProbeChild to a shared module`: the struct and its `Drop` moved byte for byte to `daemon/src/probe_child.rs` (`mod probe_child;` in `lib.rs`); the only change is `pub(crate)` on the struct and its four fields, as the user approved; no line of `Drop` and no kill or signal code changed, and none was written. `codex_version.rs` imports it. Daemon test count (`cargo test -p anthrex-daemon -- --list`): 4389 before, 4389 after; `cargo test -p anthrex --test codex_version`: 8 passed.
- **The Claude version is `0.160.1` in the end-to-end test** (ruling F8): `fake-agent --version` prints `codex-cli 0.160.1` for both runtimes; the brief's `0.155.0` is replaced.
- **Single flight (ruling F27).** `ModelService::list` is a plain `fn` returning a `Send + 'static` future rather than an `async fn`: it notes each runtime's count of finished refreshes and spawns that runtime's task when it is called, so "entered" is the call itself. A task that gets the runtime's gate after a refresh it did not start has finished returns memory without probing. Callers still write `.list(..).await`. `CountingProbes` holds `models` until both runtimes' probes have started (both `list` calls are made before), and the test asserts each counter is exactly 1 and the spans disjoint; with the count check removed the test fails (`left: 2, right: 1`).
- **The scrub list (rulings F28, F29).** `CliProbes` gains `claude_auth` (`[orchestrator.claude] auth` from the live settings when `RunService::new` builds it) and passes `headless::credential_scrub_for(Claude, claude_auth)` (Codex: `ClaudeAuth::Login`) as `env_remove`. Fix round 1: `probe_command` applies a headless session's whole environment through `headless::session::session_env` (made `pub(crate)`, visibility only): the scrubbed prefixes (`CLAUDE_CODE_`, `BASH_FUNC_`, `MCP_`, `OTEL_`), names (`CLAUDECODE`, `BASH_ENV`, …), the session identity (`ANTHREX_WINDOW_ID`, `ANTHREX_SOCKET`) and the git location variables, less `env_remove`, with `headless::session_vars(runtime, &[])` on top. Pinned by `daemon/tests/models_env.rs` (its own binary, as `git_env_scrub.rs`, since it sets variables). Unit tests pass `&[]`.
- **Where the service is built.** `RunService::new` (not only `for_manager`) builds the `ModelService`, since the daemon builds its `RunService` with `new`; it also keeps the `CancellationToken` of the `CliProbes`, which `RunService::stop` cancels so a probe in flight ends (and `ProbeChild` kills its CLI) when the daemon stops, instead of holding the runtime's shutdown for up to 10 s.
- **`EXIT_GRACE` (500 ms) is new.** Ruling D10: after its answer a model probe closes the CLI's stdin and waits up to `EXIT_GRACE` (inside the read deadline) for the CLI to exit; only when it reaped the child itself does it mark `ProbeChild` completed. A CLI still running is killed and reaped by `ProbeChild::drop`, as the brief says.
- **`load_disk` (ruling F31)** keeps each runtime's newest `<runtime>-*.json` by `fetched_at`, marked `Cached` with `problem: None`; `*.raw.jsonl` is never read. A version that does not parse (`unknown`) never reads a cache by version; its live catalog is still written (as `<runtime>-unknown.json`), so a later failure can fall back to it.
- **A failed version probe** (other than `Missing`) is treated like a failed model probe: the newest cache as `Cached`, else the built-in list, with that error as the `problem`.
- **The timeout test warms its stand-in** (`warm` argument, exits at once) before the 500 ms starts, so macOS's first exec of a fresh file cannot stop the stand-in from writing its pid before it is killed. The bound and values are the brief's.
- **The read loop is duplicated** in `models/lines.rs` (ruling F30); the follow-up is in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-17-anthrex-foundation-followups.md` ("From M9.8.6").
- **Fix round 1 notes.** (a) When a probed CLI's leader exits within `EXIT_GRACE`, the probe marks `ProbeChild` completed, so descendants left in its process group are not killed (the same semantics as `codex_version::probe`); check for stragglers during the manual real-CLI check. (b) `CliProbes.claude_auth` is a snapshot taken at `RunService::new`: an `[orchestrator.claude] auth` change applies to discovery after a daemon restart. (c) Codex's version comes from the startup probe's recorded version when there is one, so a Codex upgraded while the daemon runs keeps being served the old version's cache as `Live` until `r` (refresh) or a daemon restart. (d) `a_garbage_cache_file_is_treated_as_absent` pins existing behaviour (a cache file that does not parse is no cache; the probe runs and rewrites it); it passed before the fix round too.

### M9.8.7a (launchers I)

- **Two move-only commits.** `refactor(daemon): move plan.rs's for_repo out` (the brief's; `plan.rs` re-exports it) and `refactor(daemon): move route_pick's survivors to run/model_roles.rs` (ruling F13): `Installed`, `missing`, `NOT_INSTALLED`, `FAILED_IN_TASK`, `failed_routes`, `failed_in`, `installed_roster`, `overlap`, `Mover`, `declared_dependents`, `alongside`, unchanged but for visibility, every caller importing them from `model_roles`. Daemon test count (`cargo test -p anthrex-daemon -- --list`): 4405 before and after each. The feature commit then moves `OVERLAPPING_OWNS` too, replaces `writer_route` and `racer_route` with `model_roles::{writer_route, racer_route}` (callers repointed, the old ones deleted), and drops `review_route`'s every caller outside `route_pick.rs` (now `RunModels::reviewer_route`). Left in `route_pick*.rs` for M9.8.8 (their bodies are the roster's escalation, which 8 replaces): `rung2_route`, `writer_step`, `escalate_for`, `every_route_failed`, `writer_route_failed`; for M9.8.13: `repicks` (reads `list_pick`), `review_route`/`forecast` (only `pick` uses them now).
- **`runtime_open` (ruling F40)** is `open_roster`'s predicate, and `open_roster` calls it. Its semantics are the code's (a lane on `runtime` is held off by an unfinished overlapping task on *another runtime than `runtime`*), not decision 28's wording ("a task on that runtime"): rule 9 keeps every task overlapping a task on the task's own runtime, so the code's reading is the one that can hold a lane off. `the_test_writer_takes_its_row_unless_its_runtime_is_held` therefore holds the Codex writer off with an overlapping task on Claude (the hub task's runtime), not on Codex as the brief words it.
- **Route resolution (decision 10).** A route that names no model takes the row's model; an effort it names is kept (the task edit form's effort over the row's model, and `run edit`'s effort-only amend, which `amend_route_on_a_working_task_is_refused` exercises), else the row's. A runtime alone is ignored. The brief words only the model-named case; keeping an effort-only route is the extension. `resolve_task`/`resolve_task_lenient` and `validate_tasks`/`validate_tasks_with` lose their `default_runtime` parameter (unused); `validate_tasks*` gain `models: &RunModels`.
- **Rule 9 (decision 33)** reads `planned_runtime(task, models)`: the row's runtime, or the route's runtime when the route names a model. Not `task.route.runtime` as the brief writes: that counts a runtime the engine escalated to (rung 2), and `an_engine_escalation_to_the_peer_runtime_does_not_block_later_edits` (final review A-I1) fails with it. The refusal is decision 33's text, also when a named route (not a row) gave the runtime.
- **The start (decision 9).** `BuildContext` gains `models` and `models_log` (the start's lines: a broken repository file, a replaced effort), logged after the tuning lines; the brief names only `models`. `driver/build_models.rs::freeze` reads the repository file on its own `spawn_blocking`, bounded by `MODELS_READ_BOUND` (5 s; past it, or on a panic, the global table and a log line), not inside an existing one (hard rule 2's timeout); `build.rs` gains the one call. At restore the read runs inside the existing per-run reconcile `spawn_blocking`, and `build_models::fill` applies it (a run whose reconcile panicked gets the global table without a read). Repository-file problems go to the log through `safe_text::one_line`.
- **Reviewer (decision 27)** at launch is `model_roles::reviewer_at_launch` (RL-1: past a route that failed in this task, the row's other model, never the author's); `engine/review.rs`, which the brief's file list leaves out, is where reviewers launch. The same-model line goes to the run log once per run. `reach.rs` (not in the list either) counts the reviewer row's model and fallback for every reviewed route, a racing task's row fallback and a paired task's `test_writer` row.
- **Racer (decision 28).** `racer_route` always gives a route (the row's fallback, else the task's own), so a race no longer falls back to one worker for want of a peer model; the "race skipped: no installed … / would overlap …" lines are gone.
- **Routing records (ruling F16).** First worker, racer lane, test writer and reviewer decisions: source `role_table` (`explicit_task` for a model-named route), policy `m9.8-roles-v1`, candidates the row's model (at the route's effort when it is that model) then its fallback; skip reasons: `the task names the model …`, `not installed`/`overlapping owns` for a held writer row, `the author's own model`, `failed in this task`. `HISTORY_VERSION` stays 5. The route refit's samples (class-default records only) see none of these; it goes in M9.8.13.
- **Ruling F24**: `amend_task` refuses a route effort that is not `DEFAULT` and not `valid_effort`, field `route.effort` (`a_users_route_with_a_bad_effort_is_refused`).
- **Golden test.** `todays_resolution_matches_the_golden_file` now expects the built-in where a cell is `unresolved` (only `default_runtime_codex`'s `implementer.hub`: the plan builds now, on the built-in Opus row); the JSON is unchanged, every other cell matches.
- **Tests rewritten** (old → new expectation):
  - `edits_tests_state::an_escalated_route_survives_a_test_mode_amend`: `pick_reviewer` (Claude Sonnet, medium) → the reviewer row against the kept Codex route (its fallback, Claude Opus); `an_engine_escalation_…`: the rule-9 amend names `codex` *and* model `""`.
  - `design_snapshot::a_non_design_run_has_no_design_state_and_an_unchanged_snapshot`: 9.5's bytes with three changes: `review_route.effort` `medium` → `high`; racer b's route effort (round and lane) `medium` → `""`.

- **Tests ignored** (*superseded: deleted in M9.8.13*) (31, `#[ignore = "M9.8.7a: … deleted with route_pick.rs in M9.8.13"]`): every remaining test whose setup is a model list picking a worker or reviewer at build or edit, which no longer happens (`pick_all`/`pick_named` are no-ops): `route_pick_tests{,_fixes,_frozen,_roles}.rs` (23) and `engine/tests/route_lists{,_failed,_substitute}.rs` (8), listed by name in `.superpowers/sdd/M9.8-model-roles/task-7a-report.md`. `targets` is `#[cfg(test)]` for its one test.
- **Not changed** (noted for later tasks; *superseded: the raise takes the medium row since M9.8.8, and the frozen lists went in M9.8.13*): a decider's size raise still keeps the task's model and takes the new row's effort (M8b decision 19); rung 3 keeps the route. `RunLimits.route_lists` and `class_routes` are still frozen (M9.8.13).
- **Fix round 1 (review of M9.8.7a).**
  - *Same-runtime race cap.* A racer whose peer runs on the task's own runtime needs two free writer slots under that runtime's cap (`cap - writers_busy_on >= 2`), not just `has_room` twice. Before, the pair could exceed the per-runtime writer cap by one. Test `race::a_same_runtime_race_needs_two_slots_under_its_runtimes_cap` (cap = busy + 1: Wait, then single).
  - *The global fallback is validated.* When the repository's `models.toml` cannot be read (error, or past the 5 s bound), the start freezes the global table through `build_models::global_only`, which validates efforts against the cloned catalogs like `freeze` does. The reconcile-panic path in `restore.rs` uses it too. Test `the_global_fallback_is_validated`.
  - *`load_disk` before `restore()`* in `lifecycle.rs`. It spawns nothing and holds no lock across an await (its read is on `spawn_blocking`), so a run filled at restore (decision 9) is validated against the cached catalogs.
  - *Controller ruling: a user's route without a model is refused.* `amend_task` refuses a user (`EditSource::User`) route that names a runtime or a strength but no model, field `route.model`: "choose a model; runtime alone no longer selects one" ("strength alone" for a strength). The row would otherwise replace it silently. Orchestrator and planner routes are unaffected, and so is rule 9's `planned_runtime`. Test `a_users_route_without_a_model_is_refused`.
  - *`orchestrator_window` `broken_role`* writes `role_launch.effort = 42` (a number), which really is undecodable. `"extreme"` became a valid effort string in task 4.
  - *Known gaps, deliberately unchanged:*
    - `reach` counts the reviewer row's runtime unconditionally, so a Claude-only user on the built-in `codex:default` reviewer has Codex in reach.
    - The once-per-run same-model reviewer line dedups by scanning a trimmed `run.log`, so it can repeat once the line has been trimmed away.
    - `fill` rewrites a finished run's `run.json` once at restore.
    - Rule 9's text can say "size research" (the row name of a research task).
    - `gates_review.rs` is at 604 lines. (*Superseded: 552 since M9.8.9's fix round, its fixtures in `gates_support.rs`.*)
    - Controller ruling: a decider's S→M size raise keeping the task's model is deferred to M9.8.8, to decide against decision 10. (*Done in M9.8.8.*)

### M9.8.7b (launchers II)

- **The orchestrator.** `orch/launch.rs::resolve_orchestrator` is replaced by `orchestrator_route(choice, models) -> Resolved` (source `role_table` or `explicit_choice`, the chosen route the one candidate). The real callers are ruling F32's: `orch/installed.rs::{resolve_installed, resolve_planned, resolve_promoted}` (via `driver/build.rs`, `engine/promote.rs`); `driver/adapt_goal.rs` and `driver/build_name.rs` never called it. `resolve_installed` no longer steps to the peer runtime when the row's runtime is not installed (D2, MR §7): the start is refused with today's text (`... install it, or choose another runtime with --orchestrator`). `resolve_planned` and `resolve_promoted` lose their `window`/`installed` maps (only the `orchestrator` list read them); `build.rs` changes signatures only (net −3 lines). `engine/promote.rs` still clears `run.orch.promote_window`. A choice naming a model with no effort takes the row's effort only when it is the row's own model, else `Effort::DEFAULT` (so a continued chain, whose choice carries no effort, gets `DEFAULT` unless it names the row's model).
- **An explicit orchestrator model outside the catalog is accepted.** `resolve_orchestrator` refused `--orchestrator <runtime>:<model>` naming a model not in the roster (`claude:test-model is not in the roster`). `orchestrator_route` takes the user's model as given (MR §5.2's `custom…`, D2: the user's choice); `cli/tests/run_cli_orch.rs::orchestrator_flag_parses_and_refuses_bad_values` now starts on `claude:test-model` and asserts the orchestrator runs it. Flag syntax refusals are unchanged.
- **Planners and scouts.** `planner_route` is the `planner` row (once the run has an orchestrator); `scout_routing`, `scout_route_of` and `frozen_scout_route` become `launch::scout_route(run)`, the `research` row. The planner refusal's hint is now `choose another model for the planner in C-b S`. `RunLimits.orch.scouts` (`ScoutRouting`) is still written and loaded (ruling F15) but read by nothing; `scout/spec.rs` keeps the struct and `from_config` only (`route`, `route_within`, `run_scout_route`, `ScoutRouting::of` are deleted: no caller is left; M9.8.13 has the struct to delete). The onboarding scout's route is the `research` row of the repository's `models.toml` over the live table, read in the same bounded `spawn_blocking` as the start's installed probe (`build_installed.rs::installed_with`, generic over the read; `installed_now`/`found_within` are `#[cfg(test)]` now); `ProfileService.onboarding_rotation` (the `scout` list's rotation) is gone. `ScoutService::start` (no route given) uses `scout::spec::scout_route(ctx)`, the live `research` row without a repository file (`Roster::roles`).
- **D2 for roles with no run (rows' fallback).** `model_roles::row_route_over(choice, installed)`: the row's model, unless its runtime is recorded missing and the row's fallback is on the other runtime and not missing; then the fallback at its default effort. Deciders (`call::route_over`) and the onboarding scout use it; the run roles (orchestrator, planner, scouts) never move (their start checks refuse instead).
- **Brainstormers and the document reviewer (F11).** `brainstorm_picks_with` is the `brainstorm` row's two models at its effort; a model whose runtime is missing or cannot run unsaved is replaced by the other row model; neither → `None`. Labels as before. `unsaved_missing` names only the row's runtimes. `review_pick` is `RunModels::reviewer_route(orchestrator's route)`; when that pick's runtime is missing or cannot run unsaved, the orchestrator's own route with today's reason (the "roster has no … model" reason is gone). The same-model line of decision 27 is not logged for the document reviewer (only for task reviews). `default_pair` is deleted.
- **Helpers (decisions 17, 42; F14, F25).** `DeciderContext` loses `routing` (`call::Routing` and `ladder_route` are deleted, so M9.8.13 has nothing left there) and gains `live`, `data_dir`, `bins`, `decider_bin`; `new(live, manager, data_dir)`. `route` stays as the field the call's argv reads: `call::routed(ctx, kind, project)` sets it (and `program`) per call from `route_for`/`choice_for`; `new` sets the live `helpers` row's, which a direct `decide` (the CLI decider tests) runs on. This is a deviation from "`route` replaced": keeping it as the per-call route avoided rewriting the argv builders and every decider fixture. `decide()` takes its runtime from `ctx.route.runtime`; `mode` says only off or on. The repository file is read inside `routed`'s installed-probe `spawn_blocking` (bounded by `INSTALLED_PROBE_TIMEOUT`); past the bound, the global row. `decide_within` takes `(request, project)`. Run-bound deciders (`adapt.rs`) pass the run's project, triage the goal's, the run name the project being named. `config::settings::apply_owned` copies `roles` (F14). The record (`roles::decider_routed`) lists the row's model then its fallback, the row's model `not installed` when the call moved; `decider_candidates`, `decider_listed`, `ladder_candidates` and the `adaptation` parameters of `record_triage`/`record_run_name` are gone.
- **Routing records (F16 for roles).** Orchestrator, planner, scout, decider, brainstormer and document-reviewer records: source `role_table` (`explicit_choice`/`continued_chain` for an orchestrator, `same_runtime` for a document reviewer on the orchestrator's route), policy `m9.8-roles-v1`, candidates the row's model (at the row's effort) then its fallback, `not installed` marked; the chosen candidate never carries a reason (`roles::record`'s rule), even on a runtime the start found missing. The old policy constants stay (unused).
- **Role lists.** `role_lists.rs` loses `scout_pick`, `planner_pick`, `orchestrator`, `candidates`, `mark`, `LIST_SOURCE` (M9.8.13's list; deleted here because nothing called them). `reach` no longer counts the `scout`/`planner`/`brainstorm` lists or `peer_route` for the document reviewer; it counts the rows (and `review_pick`).
- **MR §7, missing CLI.** `driver/start_error.rs` (new): a session start whose spawn error chain holds `NotFound` records `<Role::label>: <runtime> not found; choose another model in C-b S` (`reviewer: codex not found; …`). Applied to `CreateWindow` (workers, reviewers, test writers, racers, research tasks; the role from the `RunRef`, a worker's from its task's row), run scouts (`research`), sub-planners (`planner`) and design agents (`brainstorm`, `reviewer`). Not applied: the orchestrator's PTY window and the onboarding scout (`profile/service_run.rs`). `ops.rs` is at 601 lines.
- **Report.** `report.rs::below_frontier` ("orchestrator below the frontier tier") is removed: every row route has `strength: Standard`, so the line would print for every planned run; tiers are gone (D8). Its two tests (`orch_edit::{an_orchestrator_below_the_frontier_tier_is_reported, a_fast_orchestrator_is_reported_below_the_frontier}`) are deleted.
- **Golden test.** `today` now calls the new launchers (`orchestrator_route`, `scout_route`, `brainstorm_picks_with` at `DECIDER_CAPS`, `DeciderContext::route_for`); every cell still matches the JSON.
- **Acceptance grep** (`grep -rn "Route {" crates/daemon/src --include='*.rs' | grep -v _tests | grep -v tests/`), the struct literals left: `model_roles.rs:133` (`route_of`, the row conversion); `validate.rs:345, 374` (a task's row, or a user's explicit route, M9.8.7a); `orch/mod.rs:394, 456` (an `EpicRecord`'s placeholder route, replaced by `planner_route` when the epic is queued, and a test constructor); `routing.rs:47, 156, 253`, `roster.rs:87, 123, 137`, `route_pick_step.rs:154`, `model_tuning.rs:224` (`FrozenList`), `validate_patterns.rs:93` (`peer_route`): the roster/list escalation and old selectors M9.8.8 and M9.8.13 remove. Every other hit is a signature, an enum variant (`TuningChange::Route`, `OrchEvent::RoleRoute`, `TriageRoute`) or `ClassRoute`.
- **Tests rewritten or deleted** (old → new): `engine/tests/orch.rs::{resolve_orchestrator_order, resolve_orchestrator_keeps_the_candidate_snapshot}` deleted (→ `launch_tests::the_orchestrator_takes_its_row_unless_the_goal_form_chose`); the peer-step and planner-step tests deleted); `role_lists_tests` rewritten (the list tests deleted; `a_rows_runtime_that_cannot_run_unsaved_is_named`, `a_brainstormers_record_is_the_rows` added); `roles_installed_tests` rewritten (`a_planners_record_is_its_row`, `a_run_scouts_record_is_the_research_row`); `roles_tests::{decider_candidates_are_the_modes_runtime_ladder_only, planner_and_scout_ladders_include_the_peer_runtime}` deleted, others' source/policy updated; `decider/tests_context.rs` and `tests_route.rs` rewritten for rows; `scout/tests.rs::route_picks_the_lowest_strength_at_or_above` deleted, `ctx(runtime)` puts the `research` row on the runtime; `profile/tests_installed.rs` → `onboarding_scouts_and_deciders_use_their_rows_over_what_is_installed`; `adapt_goal_installed_tests::a_codex_only_user_starts_planned_runs` → `…_on_codex_rows` (the built-in rows are refused for a Codex-only user; Codex rows start), `a_missing_planner_runtime_is_still_refused` (planner row on Claude; new hint), promote tests set the planner row, `a_codex_run_scout_is_covered_…` parses its configs (migration); `adapt_goal_list_tests` → `a_planned_start_takes_the_orchestrator_row_below_an_explicit_choice`; Targeted CLI e2e binaries (models_migration, profile_cli, run_cli_design, run_cli_orch, run_design_fixtures, run_e2e_{adapt,adapt_engine,adapt_profile,chain,design,design_rounds,orch,orch_ops,orch_wake,run_name,settings_live,model_roles}): 96 passed; decider_call* 21, scout_service* 10, fake-agent adapt_modes 12.

This replaces 7b's first reading (the runtime's CLI default), which in turn replaced the roster's frontier entry.

### M9.8.8 (escalation)

- **`role_step::escalate(models, role, current, failed)`** (decision 29) walks one ladder: on the row's fallback, the fallback's efforts above `current`'s; on any other model, that model's efforts above `current`'s, then the row's fallback at `DEFAULT`, then the fallback's efforts. `DEFAULT` sits at the model's reported default effort, else below the first; an effort the model's list does not hold (only a user's route can carry one, decision 21 replaces a row's) counts as the top. A model with no list in `RunModels.efforts` has no effort step. The first route of that ladder not `failed_in(failed)` is the step. `role_step::steps` returns the whole ladder (the routing record's pool and `reach`).
- **A route outside the row** (a user's, decision 10; a fix task's culprit route from a since-raised size) **(ruling)**: decision 29 words only the row's model and its fallback. Read as "any other model climbs its own list, then takes the row's fallback", so escalation never names a model that neither the route nor the row names. Test `a_route_outside_the_row_climbs_its_own_list_then_takes_the_rows_fallback`.
- **The overlap rule and the installed skip are kept (ruling T10a-6)**: `rung2_route`, `writer_step` and `escalate_for` pass `role_step::escalate`, as `failed`, the task's `failed_routes` plus every step on a runtime `model_roles::runtime_open` refuses for the mover (`Worker` for rung 2 and fix tasks, `Transient` for the writer). The brief names only `failed_routes(task)`; without this a rung 2 could move a worker onto a fallback runtime that an overlapping task holds (rule 9) or that the start found missing. `every_route_failed`/`writer_route_failed` keep their two texts: "no route … keeps the overlap rule" when a step past the installed skip alone is left.
- **Every route-changing rung goes through `role_step::escalate`**: rung 2 (`engine/ladder.rs::rung2` → `route_pick::rung2_route`), `run retry` (`engine/requests.rs::rung2` → `rung2_route`, or `pair::escalate_writer` → `writer_step` while the test is written), the actions menu's retry preview (`engine/actions/effects.rs` → `writer_step` / `rung2_route`), the test writer's step (`pair::escalate_writer` → `writer_step`), the bisect's fix task (`engine/bisect.rs` → `escalate_for`) and the delivery CI fix task (`engine/delivery/fix.rs` → `escalate_for`). Their signatures held; only their docs changed. `rung2_route` still returns `(Route, Option<ListPick>)`, always `None`: `next_candidate` (the list step) is no longer called on any rung and is left, with its ignored tests, for M9.8.13. `open_roster` and `roster_substitute` are deleted (their callers went).
- **Deleted**: `roster::{escalate, strength_one_up}`, `roster_skipping::escalate_skipping`, `model_roles::installed_roster` (its one caller was `reach`'s roster walk), `Effort::raised` and its asserts in `proto/src/run_tests.rs` and `models_tests.rs`. Acceptance grep: `role_step.rs:20 escalate`, `proto/src/run.rs:561 Size::raised`, `run/model.rs:86 ReviewLevel::raised`, `process.rs:25 escalate` (kill code, untouched, ruling F33).
- **Routing records (ruling F16)**: `routing::escalation_pool(models, role, from)`: `role_step::steps` from `from`, then `from` unchanged, then the row's route and its fallback where escalation never goes (`not an escalation step from <runtime>:<model>`). An escalation record's policy is `m9.8-roles-v1` (was `m8a-escalate-v1`; source still `escalation_policy`). Shape unchanged, `HISTORY_VERSION` stays 5.
- **`reach`** walks `role_step::steps` along the task's row at each size from its own up (rung 3 changes the row), and the `test_writer` row for the writer; past a runtime the start found missing. A larger size's row route is counted for a task that names no model (the decider's raise, below). The roster walk is gone.
- **Controller ruling: the decider's S→M raise** (`engine/deciders_size.rs::apply_raise`) now takes the route `ladder::reresolve` returns for the raised size (decision 10, through `validate::resolve_route` and `RunModels::task_role`): `implementer.medium`'s model and effort; a route that names its model keeps it, and an effort the route names stays. Before, only the effort changed. Test `deciders_size::a_raise_to_m_takes_the_medium_row_unless_the_route_names_a_model` (until M9.8.9 clears plan routes, a plan's named model counts as a user's, as in `validate::resolve_route`). Rung 3 still keeps the route (7a's note).
- **Fix tasks' route specs** (`bisect.rs`, `delivery/fix.rs::route_spec`) name `strength: None` instead of the route's: a row's route says `standard`, and `validate::resolve_route` refused `claude-opus-5-5` (frontier in the roster) at `standard`, so every fix task escalated to a frontier model fell back to the culprit's route. The spec's strength is now the roster's. `delivery_review_fix::route_spec_of` and `fix_routes`' FW-10 assertion follow.
- **Fixtures**: `test_support::{with_efforts, escalated}` and `Fixture::{with_efforts, escalated, escalation_pool}`. `with_efforts` reports `low`, `medium`, `high` (no default) for every row model, as a live catalog would; without it a built-in row (no fallback, no catalog in a unit test) has nothing to escalate to, which is decision 29's rule.
- **Tests rewritten** (old → new expectation):
  - `turns::stall_interrupts_then_nudges_then_goes_to_rung_2`: `effort.raised()` → with efforts, `low` → `medium`.

### M9.8.9 (the orchestrator sizes; it never routes)

- **Where the route is cleared (decision 31).** `Batch::unrouted` (`run/edits.rs`) clears the route of an `add_task` task and of each `split_task` child from `EditSource::{Orchestrator, Planner}` before resolution; `amend_task` (`edits_amend.rs`) treats such a source's `route` as absent, so it neither changes the task nor draws the started-task refusal. An amend from them that named *only* a route is a no-op (no error; "nothing to amend" would reject an older orchestrator's batch). `Batch::route_ignored` adds `EditConsequence::RouteIgnored` once per batch when a cleared route named a runtime, model, strength or effort (a `RouteSpec` not equal to its default). `build_run` (`run/plan.rs`) clears every plan task's route and appends `contract::ROUTE_IGNORED` to the start's log, after the tuning and role-table lines, when any named something.
- **Deviation: where `ROUTE_IGNORED` is logged.** The brief names `engine/orch.rs::edit_plan` and the planner's edit path; both apply their batch through `engine/batch.rs::apply_batch`, whose consequence match logs it with `requests::log` for every source. One arm there instead of two keeps `engine/orch.rs` at 564 lines (D15).
- **Deviation: decision 32's "values unchecked".** `tools_bounds`' `Rule::Route` was already an object of the four keys with `effort` and `strength` left to serde (and `Effort` is a string since task 4), so `{"runtime": "codex", "model": "x", "strength": "frontier", "effort": "xhigh"}` passed before (`a_route_with_any_values_is_accepted` is a pinning test, green on its first run). The `model` length bound (0 to 100) is kept: M9.11 review finding 3's protection against a forged oversized string (`nested_strings_past_their_bound_are_refused`), not a check of the value. The MCP schema's route is now `{type: object, additionalProperties: false, description, properties: {runtime, model, strength, effort: {type: string}}}` (no enums, no length), and `orch_tools.json`'s six route objects were edited to match (ruling F35; no record mode).
- **Deviation: the e2e asserts `run.json`'s log.** `run status` does not print the run log, so `run_e2e_orch/gate.rs::an_orchestrator_route_is_ignored_and_the_run_says_so` reads the line with `support::run_pr::log_lines`.
- **F24** was already done by M9.8.7a (`model_roles_tests::a_users_route_with_a_bad_effort_is_refused`); nothing added.
- **The decider's S→M raise (carried from M9.8.8)**, re-checked against decision 31: with the orchestrator's, planners' and plan files' routes cleared, a spec that names a model is a user's (or an engine fix task's), so `apply_raise` keeping a model-named route now equals the controller ruling. `deciders_size::a_raise_to_m_takes_the_medium_row_unless_the_route_names_a_model` sets `t2`'s route by a user `amend_task` (`apply_edits` with `EditSource::User` before the start) instead of in the plan; it passes unchanged otherwise.
- **The grep (ruling F34)**, `grep -rlnE 'route = \{|\[task\.route\]|CODEX\b' crates` on af0057f6, 70 files. Real plan-route users visited and migrated: `cli/tests/run_e2e_{basic,codex_config,detail,gates,merge,pair,review,sessions,settings,tuning,worker_pins}.rs`, `cli/tests/support/run_plans.rs`; daemon `decider`: none; `run/{edits_tests,history_tests_fixtures,plan_tests_build,plan_tests_routes,plan_tests_tuning,reach_tests,test_support,validate_patterns_tests,validate_tests}.rs`, `run/driver/adapt_goal_tests.rs`, `run/engine/tests/{actions_effects,concurrency,control_retry,deciders_size,dispatch,dispatch_edits,gates_fixes,gates_review,gates_rounds,pair_fixes,race,race_pair_plan,race_slots,turns,turns_fixes,turns_holds,turns_ops,turns_stale,view_fields}.rs` (plus `control_resume.rs`, which imported `CODEX_AUTHOR`). Hits that are not plan routes, left alone: `config/src/{models/mod.rs,orchestrator/tuning_tests.rs,settings/{mod,shipped,tests}.rs}` (`SHIPPED_CODEX`, the built-in `CODEX` model ref), `daemon/src/decider/tests_route.rs` (`NO_CODEX`), `daemon/src/transcript/mod.rs` (the Codex parser), `daemon/src/run/role_launch.rs` and `daemon/tests/{run_confine_protected,run_exec_env,run_git}.rs` (`.CODEX` paths, env names, a settings list), `daemon/src/run/design/report_tests.rs` (`[CODEX]` text), `run/engine/tests/{design_*,fix_routes}.rs` (a window id, a model ref), `fake-agent/src/discovery.rs`, `proto/src/{run,run_tests,types}.rs` (the plan format still parses `[task.route]`), `tui/src/app{,_tests}/settings_screen.rs`, and `run/route_pick_tests*.rs` (ignored since 7a, deleted in M9.8.13; `route_pick_tests_frozen::get_context_shows_the_lists` deleted here, its context check superseded by `get_context_shows_the_roles`). Acceptance grep `grep -rn 'route = {' crates/cli/tests crates/daemon/tests | grep -v '"route"'` prints nothing.

A Codex task in a unit test is an M task under `codex_medium` or an S task under `codex_small`, whichever keeps the test's other tasks on Claude at their sizes.

### M9.8.10 (TUI: the models table and the picker, as state)

- **Files.** New `tui/src/app/models_table.rs` (423), `models_keys.rs` (88), `models_table_tests.rs` (~520), `model_picker_tests.rs` (~370); `model_picker.rs` modified per F6 (474). `settings_screen.rs` 342, `settings_flow.rs` 378, `settings_keys.rs` 128, `app/mod.rs` 592 (only `mod models_keys; pub(crate) mod models_table;`).
- **RED** (`cargo test -p anthrex-tui --lib model`): 13 compile errors, every new type and method missing (`ModelPicker`, `PickerEntry`, `PickerFor`, `Picked`, `CustomModel`, `ModelsTable`, `RowKey`, `Scope`, `TableRow`, `Catalogs::{label, efforts, unreported, received}`, `SettingsScreen.models`). GREEN: 6 picker tests and 10 table tests. `w_saves_the_scope` was written after the scope's save existed and passed on its first run (pinning).
- **Deviation (F10): `Catalogs.received_at: Vec<(Runtime, Instant)>`** beside `list`; `absorb(incoming, now)` stamps it, and `on_daemon` passes `Instant::now()`. `Catalogs::{received, age(runtime, now)}` are for M9.8.11's `updated <age> ago`.
- **Deviation (F36): `CustomModel.naming: bool`** besides `runtime`, `name: TextArea` (the TUI's text-input idiom) and `error`: the two steps need to know which one is shown. The custom step starts on the current model's runtime (fix round 1; `claude` with none); `←`/`→`/`space` switch, `⏎` moves to the name, `esc` returns to the list. The typed name is trimmed and checked with `ModelRef::parse("<runtime>:<name>")`; a paste reaches it as one line.
- **Rules the brief leaves open, decided here.**
  - A catalog string is `one_line(..).trim()` (F19); an effort that is not `valid_effort` is dropped from the list and the cycle. A picker entry's description gets ` (<Runtime> default)` for the catalog's `is_default` model.
  - `Catalogs::label` of `<runtime>:default` reads `default` (not the CLI default's label: the row means whatever the CLI is set to use). `unreported` never warns `default`.
  - A row's warnings follow the daemon's launch rule (`RunModels::validate`): `effort '<e>' not offered` only when a Live/Cached catalog lists the model **with** efforts; `not reported by …` only for a Live/Cached catalog. Brainstorm checks both models. A helper kind showing `same as helpers` has no warnings, effort or fallback text (empty).
  - Header for a runtime with no catalog yet: `CLAUDE  (no list yet)`. A runtime is `missing` when its catalog's `problem` is exactly `<runtime> not found` (decision 24); its models are still listed (greyed) but never selectable, and `j`/`k` skip them.
  - The picker opens on the current model when it is a selectable entry, else on the first selectable entry (`none` for `f` with no fallback). A current model no catalog entry is (an unknown id, `<runtime>:default`) gets its own entry under its runtime, described `not reported` (fix round 1).
  - Picking a new model for a row keeps its effort when the new model's catalog offers it; otherwise the effort becomes the model's catalog default (none when it offers no effort). An unknown model keeps the effort. Brainstorm checks its first model.
  - `space` on `helpers` **or one of its kinds** toggles them; closing puts the selection on `helpers`. `f` on brainstorm does nothing. Edits in `this repo` wait for its `RepoModels` reply (nothing to edit before it).
  - `e` on a helper kind that shows `same as helpers` creates the kind's row from the helpers row (as any edit of an inherited row does).
  - Test `an_effort_not_offered_and_an_unreported_model_warn`: the brief's "with Claude's catalog `Builtin`, no warning for either" cannot hold for `implementer · small` (a Codex model). The test checks both catalogs `Builtin` → no warnings, and Claude's alone `Builtin` → research clear, small still warned.
- **Repository scope (decision 36).** `PendingWhat::RepoModels { project, put }` (one variant for both requests); `route_settings_reply` hands those replies to `settings_flow.rs::settings_repo_reply` by `request_id`. `→` asks once (an awaited ask is not doubled; a loaded table is kept when the scope is left and re-entered). A refused read toasts and returns to `everywhere`. `w` in `this repo` sends `PutRepoModels` (`no changes to save` when equal to its base); `RepoSaved` sets the base and shows `Saved`, `Refused` lists the problems. `w` in `everywhere` sends the screen's `Put`, whose `roles` is `models.global`. `dirty()` is the document or the repository table (for `esc`'s discard), `doc_dirty()` the document alone (what `w` in `everywhere` checks).
- **F18** (*superseded: the strength warnings left the `models` section in M9.8.11's fix round and every section in M9.8.12*). `built()` takes `models` and `orchestrator` from `base` unchanged; `roles` is the `everywhere` table. `config::settings::warnings` (the strength warnings) therefore still shows the roster's warnings until M9.8.13/14 remove strength; left as is.
- **Interim drawing (M9.8.11 replaces it).** `ui/settings.rs` lost the `claude`/`codex`/`orchestrator` sections and the custom dialog; the `models` section draws a plain interim list (scope line, one padded line per `TableRow` with `●`/muted for the scope, warnings, or the picker's entries while it is open), with ASCII glyphs in ASCII mode, so the screen stays usable on the branch. Status-bar hints: `⏎ choose model`, `e effort`, `f if-it-struggles`, `x reset`, `←/→ scope`; in the picker `⏎ select`, `r refresh`, `j/k move`, `esc cancel`.
- **Opening** sends `ListModels { runtime: None, refresh: false }` only while `App.catalogs.list` is empty; `r` sends `refresh: true` and keeps the picker open; a `Models` reply refreshes an open picker's entries, the selection kept on the same model (or the same first entry / `custom…`).
- **Deleted with their sections** (decision 35): `app_tests/settings_screen.rs::{rows_are_the_shipped_lists_then_custom_models, space_toggles_and_the_doc_follows, saving_with_no_model_is_refused_inline, the_orchestrator_picker_offers_only_enabled_models, custom_adds_a_model_with_its_strength}`; `ui/settings_tests.rs::{codex_section_renders_at_80x24_and_120x40, orchestrator_section_renders_at_80x24_and_120x40, the_custom_dialog_names_a_shipped_model}`; `ui/settings_tuning_tests.rs::settings_shows_the_orchestrator_list_override` (the orchestrator section and `SettingsScreen::orchestrator_overridden` are gone). `saving_with_no_model…`'s refusal is still covered by `problems_warnings_and_outcomes_render` (a loaded doc with no models).
- **Rewritten:** `ui/settings_tests.rs::claude_section_renders…` → `models_section_renders_at_80x24_and_120x40` (interim lines); `limits_section_renders…` (section row `models  limits`); `ascii_mode_draws_only_ascii` (models rows in ASCII, no custom dialog); `the_pages_render_as_kit_dialogs` (discard only); `problems_warnings_and_outcomes_render` (`load` of a doc with no models instead of unchecking rows); `one_accented_border` (states 0, 2, 3; the custom dialog is gone); `no_panic_at_tiny_sizes` (picker open instead of the custom dialog); `settings_screen_text_is_sanitised` (hostile catalog labels, descriptions, efforts and CLI version, table and picker). `app_tests/settings_screen.rs::only_the_tuning_ask` leaves `ListModels` aside; `defaults_are_marked` drops the `enabled models`/orchestrator text. `app_tests/settings_screen_save.rs`: edits by a limit instead of a row toggle (`esc_with_changes_asks_first`, `the_screen_has_the_keys_while_it_is_open`, `a_saved_reply_keeps_edits_made_while_saving`), `a_paste_reaches_only_the_custom_name` through the picker's `custom…`, `the_saved_doc_is_adopted…` reads `built().models`. `ui/settings_tuning_tests.rs::opening_settings_sends_a_read_only_stats` holds the fixture catalogs (so no `ListModels`).

### M9.8.12 (saving: `[models]` written, old keys removed, the repository file)

- **Files.** New `config/src/settings/{scan.rs (213), write_models.rs (382), write_old.rs (102), write_models_tests.rs (416)}`, `config/src/models/old_keys.rs (177)`, `cli/tests/run_e2e_settings_models.rs`. `write.rs` 230 (limits only), `save.rs` 288, `validate.rs`, `origin.rs`, `mod.rs`; `proto/src/settings.rs`; daemon `run/driver/settings.rs`; TUI `settings_screen.rs`, `screens.rs`, `action_forms.rs`, `ui/settings.rs`.
- **RED.** `cargo test -p anthrex-config settings::write_models`: 6 of 7 failed before the writer (`saving_keeps_every_resolved_row` passed, nothing being written); daemon `a_repository_table_is_saved_beside_the_profile` failed on the Refused placeholder (F7); `run_e2e_settings_models` failed with the models writer stubbed out (no `[models.implementer.small]`). GREEN: all pass.
- **Deviation: no `toml_edit`.** It is not in the tree (`Cargo.lock`), and decision 30's line-based writer already keeps comments and formatting. `write.rs`'s scanner moved to `scan.rs` (shared); `write_models.rs` edits `[models.*]` tables in place (an unchanged row is left byte for byte, a changed key keeps its line's comment, a dropped row loses its keys and then its header, a new row follows the last `[models.*]` table or ends the file); `write_old.rs` removes the old keys. A blank line a deletion leaves doubled, leading or trailing goes (`Scan::emit`).
- **Kept keys (Task 3's review, MUST).** `models::old_keys::kept`: an old key whose every row has no row in the migrated table nor in the table being saved could not be migrated; a save keeps it, plus the roster keys and `default_runtime` it is read against (so it reads the same after the save). Its decision 18 note is replaced (`kept_notes`, applied at parse) by `config: [orchestrator.routes.hub] could not be migrated: no model it lists resolves, so [models.implementer.hub] keeps claude:claude-opus-5-5; a save keeps the list until you choose that row in C-b S` (`default_runtime`: `… the roster has no model of that runtime, so … keep their built-ins; a save keeps the key until you choose those rows`; a key kept with it: `config: <key> is kept with <list>, which is read against it`). Choosing the row and saving removes them all. Test: `an_old_list_that_migrated_to_no_row_is_kept_and_says_why`.
- **Precedence tests (Task 3's review, MUST):** `explicit_helper_kinds_and_brainstorm_win_over_migrated_lists` (an explicit `[models.helpers.run_name]` over the migrated `decider` list, other kinds on the list's row; an explicit `[models.brainstorm]` over the migrated `brainstorm` list), before and after a save.
- **Read back (decision 40), widened.** The writer proves: every row of the saved table reads back as it is, every role and brainstorm resolves as the table resolves it, no note is left unless a key was kept, and nothing but the limits, `[models]` and the old keys changed. Equality of the whole table is not required: a live key that is not removed (`deciders.mode = "codex"`) still migrates a `helpers` row; when the screen reset that row, the writer writes the row as the table resolves it (the built-in) so what runs is what the screen showed (`a_reset_row_goes_and_a_live_key_cannot_bring_it_back`).
- **Other rules decided here.** An old key or `models` key in another form (an inline table, a dotted key, `[[models.*]]`) refuses with `UNSUPPORTED_FORM` (`{table}` the row table or the old key's table). A `[models.*]` row the parser skipped (a bad value) is left as written unless the saved table has that row. `[models.*]` rows are appended after the file's last table when the file has none (in `OLD`, after `[testing]`). `config.toml.bak` is written to a temporary file before the cancel claim and renamed just before the file, so a cancelled or refused save leaves neither a `.bak` nor a temporary file. `cleaned` is now the identity (the document holds no free text: a `ModelRef` cannot hold a hidden character); `clean_entry` and `warnings` are gone.
- **Protocol (still 19).** `SettingsDoc.{models, orchestrator}`, `OrchestratorDefault`, `key::{MODELS, AGENT_RUNTIME, AGENT_MODEL}` removed; `key::ROLES = "models"` added; `SETTINGS_KEYS` has eleven keys; `origin_of` reports `models` as `File` for a `[models]` table or any old key. No CLI or mcp code referenced the removed keys.
- **Daemon (F7).** `RepoModels` reads `<repo_dir>/models.toml` with `load_repo` on `spawn_blocking` within `SettingsIo.timeout` (missing: the empty table; broken: `Refused` with its problems); `PutRepoModels` validates, takes `settings_write`, writes with `save_repo` on `spawn_blocking` (the guard dropped inside, so a write that outlives the timeout still serialises), replies `RepoSaved`; the timeout's text is `REPO_WRITE_TIMEOUT_TEXT`. `live_config.rs`: doc comment only.
- **TUI.** `built()` sends the limits and `roles`; the roster's strength warnings are gone from every section (carried from Task 11's review; asserted in `no_strength_warning_and_a_limit_saves` and `problems_warnings_and_outcomes_render`). The Promote form's picker, which listed the cache's roster, now lists `App.catalogs`' models (`configured` alone without a catalog).

A list none of whose models is known is kept by a save (with the roster and `default_runtime` it is read against) while the table still holds what the migration made of its row; choosing another model for the row lets the next save remove it. Its note: `config: [orchestrator.routes.<name>]: none of its models are known; [models.<row>] uses <model> until you choose one in C-b S` (brainstorm: `uses <first> and <second>`); this replaces the first round's `could not be migrated` text for lists. The TUI keeps them in `RepoTable.problems` and draws them as `⚠` warnings in `this repo`. Tests: daemon `unreadable_repository_rows_are_reported_and_never_erased`, TUI `unreadable_repository_rows_show_as_warnings`.
