### M9.8.14 (removal II: strength)

- **Protocol** stays 19 (unreleased). `proto::Strength` and `proto::ModelEntry` are deleted; `Route` has no `strength` (serde ignores an old one in JSON and MessagePack); `RouteSpec.strength` is `#[serde(default, skip_serializing)] Option<String>`, so an old plan's, edit file's or peer's value is accepted, ignored and never written. `deny_unknown_fields` still refuses every other route key. `HISTORY_VERSION` stays 5.
- **Brief tests.** `models_tests::a_protocol_18_route_with_strength_decodes` (RED: `missing field strength in initializer of run::Route`; GREEN), `plan_tests::an_old_plan_with_route_strength_still_loads` (RED at `e913b732` with the test copied in: the written route spec still named `strength`; GREEN; it also asserts `tier = "x"` is refused), `history_tests::an_old_routing_line_still_decodes` (both lines of `proto/src/m9_history_v2.jsonl`; RED: does not compile at `e913b732`, `route` takes a strength; GREEN). Placed in the daemon's `run/history_tests.rs`, the file the brief's run command names.
- **Ruling F5.** `Orchestrator.models` is `Vec<config::orchestrator::LegacyModel>` (`runtime`, `model`, `strength: LegacyStrength`, all `pub(crate)`), defined with `LegacyStrength` in `config/src/orchestrator/roster.rs`. The field stays `pub` (only its type's fields are private) because `config::Orchestrator { .., ..Default::default() }` literals in the daemon and the CLI need every field visible. `default_roster` is `pub(crate)` and no longer re-exported. `PlannerConfig.strength`, `Deciders.strength` and `Scouts.strength` are `pub(crate) LegacyStrength`, read only by `migrate.rs`. The roster entry's `note` is still validated (length) but no longer kept: `LegacyModel` has the three fields the ruling names; `orchestrator_tests_roster::user_models_extend_and_replace_builtins` lost its `note` assertion, the only config test change beyond type renames. Config lib 170, unchanged.
- **Acceptance grep deviation.** `LegacyStrength`/`LegacyModel` also appear in the config crate's own tests, `config/src/orchestrator_tests{,_adapt,_agent,_roster}.rs` (type renames of `proto::Strength`/`proto::ModelEntry`), which sit beside `orchestrator/`, not in it.
- **Removed with strength.** `daemon::run::orch::PlannerLimits.strength` (an old `run.json`'s is ignored), `report_task::strength_label` (a report's route line is `<runtime> <model> (<effort>)`, was `(<strength>/<effort>)`), `tui::inspector::run_format::strength_text`, replaced by `route_label`: `<runtime> · <model> · <effort>`, a blank model left out, used by the plan review's route lines, the round and task inspectors (`route` field, the reviewer's route), and the round names. The reviewer round's `strength` field (`frontier vs author standard`) is now `route` (`<reviewer route> vs author <author route>`); `judging` names the judged round's route.
- **`engine/delivery/fix.rs::strongest`** (decision 26, a stage CI/review fix's route): superseded by fix round 1 below (highest effort, first on a tie).
- **Kept on purpose.** `edits_amend.rs` still refuses a user's route naming only a strength (`choose a model; strength alone no longer selects one`), now any string; `Batch::route_ignored` and `build_run` still note a cleared route that named only a strength. `tools_bounds`' `("strength", Rule::Serde)` and the MCP schema's `strength` (one of the four ignored keys) are unchanged; `orch_tools.json` did not change.
- **`scout::spec::Roster`** holds a `ModelTable` (`Fixed(ModelTable)`, `From<ModelTable>`), not a roster; `current()` is gone (no production caller). `driver/settings_tests::{the_scout_roster_is_live, the_wired_scouts_read_the_live_roster}` assert the live `roles()` follow a save (a `research` row on Luna) instead of the roster.
- **Tests rewritten** (the roster they set chose nothing since M9.8.13): `reach_tests` (`config(review_small)`, no roster), `dispatch_edits::an_edit_that_reaches_an_unchecked_runtime_is_refused`, `race_pair_plan::a_racing_task_that_widens_the_reach_is_refused_by_the_trust_check`, `pair` (the test-writer row case), `validate_patterns_tests::race_needs_code_and_no_peer` (default config), `model_roles_tests_user` (no roster push or check), `fixes::a_fix_task_the_plan_rules_refuse_changes_nothing` and `gates_review::the_reviewer_prompt_never_names_the_author` (the old built-in roster's models, literal). `plan_tests_tuning`'s `of` compares efforts only. Old-data tests drop every `strength` key before comparing (`test_support::without_strength`, called by `without_pre_9_8_keys`; `proto delivery_tests::old_snapshot_and_profile_still_decode`; `design_snapshot`'s M9.5 text). The four generated history fixtures (`daemon/tests/fixtures/history/{refit,too-few,outlier-run,quality}.jsonl`) were regenerated: only their `"strength":…` pairs went. The checked-in old data (`proto/src/m9*_history*.jsonl`, `*_run_info.json`, `daemon/tests/fixtures/run/*.json`) is untouched and still loads.
- **Stale before this task:** `daemon/tests/server_runs::pre_run_triage_writes_a_record_even_when_no_run_is_created` expected source `decider_config` and a roster ladder; its record has been the `role_table` helper row and its fallback since M9.8.7b (ruling F16). Rewritten to that (it no longer compiled); passes.
- **TUI expectations** changed for the route text only (23 tests: `run_round_tests`, `run_task_tests`, `run_patterns{,_review}_tests`, `panel/{rows,outcome}_tests`, `app_tests/overview/run_inspector`, `ui/plan_review{,_frame}_tests`). The TUI's fixture model type is a test-local `FixtureModel`.
- **Counts.** proto 193 (+1); config 170; tui lib 1704; mcp 25 + 3 + 13; daemon lib 3740 (3738 + 2); `daemon --test {history_io 12, run_journal 45, server_runs 7}`; `anthrex-fake-agent --test adapt_modes` 12; `anthrex --test {scout_service_planner 1, run_e2e_adapt 10}`. `migrate.rs` 612 lines (611 before; longer type names rewrapped), `model_roles_tests.rs` 624 (unchanged).
- **Fix round 1** (review `task-14-review.md`).
  - *I1 (controller ruling):* `strongest` is the approved route with the highest effort (`Effort: Ord`), the first task in plan order on a tie, by a strict `>` fold. `delivery_ci_repro::reproduced_without_a_culprit_adds_a_stage_fix_with_the_strongest_route` (name restored): t1 Haiku `low`, t2 Opus `high`, t2's wins (RED: t1's route; GREEN); its bisect half has equal `medium` efforts and t1's wins (pins the tie; passes under both the old and new rule).
  - *M2:* the triage test asserts each candidate's skip reason again (`not installed` before the selected one, `an earlier candidate was taken` after).
  - *M3:* `amend_task` stores a user's route with `strength: None`, so the spec in memory equals the one `run.json` reloads.
  - *M6:* `ignored_route.rs`' module doc rewrapped.
  - *M4:* the brief's `--lib plan_tests history_tests` filter selects none of the new tests; the working filter is `run::plan::tests run::history::tests`.
  - *M5:* `Orchestrator.models` stays `pub` (functional record update outside the crate needs it); an opaque newtype is the route if tighter encapsulation is ever wanted.
  - *M7:* clippy ran on the host only (no Linux target installed here); run it for Linux (or on the Linux box) before the merge.
  - Counts: daemon lib 3740; `--lib delivery` 280; `server_runs pre_run_triage` 1; fmt and host clippy clean.

### M9.8.15 (end to end, docs and the roadmap)

- **The acceptance tests** (ruling F39, pinning): `run_e2e_model_roles::each_role_launches_its_configured_model` and `the_design_flow_launches_the_brainstorm_and_reviewer_rows` passed on their first run, as the brief expects of the milestone's acceptance test. A discrimination check: with the `planner` and `implementer.hub` rows removed from the table, the first fails (`--model fake-planner`: the sub-planner ran the built-in `claude-opus-5-5 --effort high`); restored.
- **Ruling F21.** The table adds `[models.implementer.hub] model = "claude:fake-hub"`; the scripted orchestrator spawns one sub-planner (epic `a`, area `a.txt`), which submits the `S` task, so the planner row and the epic's integration reviewer (`reviewer-a-int1-1`) are asserted too. The hub task is a hub by the stored profile's `hub = ["tests/**"]` (as `run_e2e_pair.rs`), paired, `tdd`.
- **What each launch is asserted to carry**, as tasks M9.8.7a and M9.8.11 defined it: the racer's lane b runs the row's fallback at its default effort (`-m fake-medium-fallback`, no `model_reasoning_effort`, decision 28); rows that name no effort launch with no effort flag; the orchestrator `--effort high`, the small worker `model_reasoning_effort="low"`, lane a `--effort medium`. Lane b's script hangs until it loses (as `run_e2e_patterns.rs`). The user-route cases (a picked model with no effort at its default, the row's own model at the row's effort) are the unit tests of M9.8.11's fix round, not this end-to-end test.
- **Harness.** `RunHarness::{adapt_with_config, orch_with_config}`: `adapt` and `orch` with extra TOML after the harness's tables (`adapt` and `orch` call them with none).
- **Task 11's parked minors**, RED then GREEN in `run_edit_tests_models.rs`: (1) `a_pinned_effort_is_never_contradicted_by_the_rows`: with `TaskInfo.row` set and an effort pinned, the role table text named the row's effort (`… · max`) while the task runs at the pinned one; it now names the model alone and the effort row the pinned effort. (2) `a_size_change_says_the_row_is_resolved_on_save`: a size change that can move the task to another row (not a hub, research or review task; S against M or L) reads `role table (resolved on save)`, with no stale resolved effort and no stale model's efforts to cycle; back at the opened size the row shows again. The row's resolution moved to `tui/src/run_edit_row.rs` (89 lines; `run_edit.rs` 623 → 548).
- **Docs.** README: the `[models]` table, `codex:default`, the repository `models.toml`, migration and `config.toml.bak`, kept keys, Settings' keys; `[orchestrator] default_runtime` left the example and `routes`/`models` the tables list. CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]`: the feature and the behaviour changes. `docs/timing-budgets.md`: M9.8.6's rows checked against the code (unchanged), M9.8.5's, M9.8.7a's and this task's bounds recorded. ROADMAP: 9.8 `done`; its protocol line already said 19 (F42), unchanged. Follow-ups: "From M9.8 (collected at M9.8.15)".
- **Outstanding for the PR**: the manual check (above), the full suite, the smoke run, and clippy for `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` (M9.8.14's M7).

#### Final review fix wave

- **I1 (a save never reverts a hand edit).** `config::settings::changed_since_loaded` parses the file and compares its `roles` and `roles_notes` with the live settings; `put_settings` runs it inside the `spawn_blocking` save closure and refuses with `CHANGED_SINCE_LOADED` (restart the daemon or reopen `C-b S`). `apply_owned` now also copies `roles_notes`, so the live notes follow the file after a save. No protocol change. It catches edits that change a row or the migration's notes; an edit of an old key that migrates to the same rows and notes is not detected. Tests: `a_put_over_a_hand_edited_models_row_is_refused`, `a_file_edited_after_it_was_read_is_noticed`.
- **I2 (a backup is always kept).** A save that removes old keys writes `config.toml.bak`, or the next free `config.toml.bak.N` (1, 2, ...) when that name is taken; an existing backup is never replaced. This supersedes M9.8.12's M1 ("an existing `.bak` is never replaced; no backup then"). README updated. The status line still does not name the file written: the `Saved` reply carries no path, so naming it needs a protocol change, left for later. Test: `an_existing_bak_does_not_stop_the_backup_of_the_old_keys` (and `an_existing_bak_is_never_overwritten` now expects `.bak.1` beside a `.bak` directory).
- **M1.** `list_models` moved above `restart`; `restart` has its doc comment again.
- **M2.** MR §4.4 says an effort the model does not offer is replaced by the model's default. A model the catalog lists with no efforts offers none, so `RunModels::validate` now replaces the row's effort (here, none) and logs it. Builtin catalogs still validate nothing. Test: `a_model_that_reports_no_efforts_loses_the_rows_effort`.
- **M3.** `OrchestratorChoice::effort_problem` (the effort-name rule) is checked beside `model_problem` in `adapt_goal` and `promote` (`orchestrator.effort: ...`), and `orchestrator_route` ignores an invalid effort. Test: `a_bad_effort_name_is_a_problem`.
- **M4.** CHANGELOG `[Unreleased]` says hub tasks' reviewers change model.
- **M5.** `e` on brainstorm cycles only the efforts both models offer; `validate` drops a brainstorm effort either model does not offer (logged). Tests: `e_on_brainstorm_cycles_only_what_both_models_offer`, `a_brainstorm_effort_one_model_does_not_offer_is_dropped`.
- M6, M7 and M8 are in the follow-ups under "From milestone 9.8's final review".

#### Real-CLI manual check fix (2026-10-09)

Discovery run against the real CLIs (claude 2.1.280, codex 0.160.1; evidence and defects D1-D6 in the manual check notes) found that Claude lists its models by alias (`default`, `opus[1m]`, `claude-fable-5-1[1m]`, `sonnet`, `haiku`), each with a `resolvedModel` (`claude-opus-5-5[1m]`, `claude-sonnet-5`, `claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`) and `xhigh` among its efforts, Haiku with none; Codex offers `ultra` ("automatic task delegation") past `max`. Literal id matching left 8 of 10 built-in rows "not reported", their effort ladders empty, and the reviewer-never-the-author rule comparing spellings. The controller's decisions, as implemented:

- **Decision 1 (matching).** `CatalogModel.resolved` (wire, optional, protocol 19 unchanged, round-trip test `a_catalog_entry_keeps_its_resolved_model_on_the_wire`) is parsed from `resolvedModel` by the Claude probe. `ModelCatalog::find` takes the entry of that exact id, else a non-default entry that answers to it (`CatalogModel::answers_to`: the id or the resolved model, both sides past a trailing `[...]` tag and a `-YYYYMMDD` date, `proto::models::base_model_id`), else the default entry. Every lookup (the picker's warnings and efforts, `retarget`, `RunModels::validate`) goes through it.
- **Decision 2 (identity).** `proto::models::canonical_id` (the found entry's canonical id, else the id stripped) and, for a run, `RunModels.canonical` (filled by `validate` from the catalogs it used, serde-default so an older run decodes) with `RunModels::{canonical_of, same_model_ref, same_model, efforts_of}`. The reviewer rule (`reviewer_route`, `reviewer_at_launch`, `orch::role_lists::review_pick`), the routing record's "the author's own model" (`routing::record_reviewer`) and escalation's fallback test (`role_step::ladder`) use it; the two literal helpers (`is_model`, `is_model_route`, `role_step::same`) are gone. `RunModels::same_model` is now a method. (Superseded by the gate fix's B2 below: `failed_in` and the "row's own model keeps the row's effort" rule now use the same identity too.)
- **Decision 3 (picker).** Entries keep the CLI's own id (`claude:opus[1m]`); an entry whose resolved model differs from its id shows it first in the description (`claude-opus-5-5[1m] · Opus 5.5 with 1M context · …`). The table's label is the catalog's only for the entry's exact id or exact resolved model (`claude-sonnet-5` reads `Sonnet`); an id matched past a tag or a date reads as itself (`claude-opus-5-5`, not `Opus (1M context)`, which is another context window). A current model not literally listed but found by decision 1 shows `as <label>` and that entry's efforts instead of `not reported`.
- **Decision 4 (ladder).** `Effort::LADDER` (`minimal < low < medium < high < xhigh < max`) and `Effort::ladder_rank`; `Effort`'s order follows it (`xhigh` between `high` and `max`; before, `xhigh` sorted after `max` by name), any other name after `max`. `role_step::above` climbs only ladder efforts; a default or current effort off the ladder (`ultra`) counts as the top. `ultra` stays choosable in the picker and with `e`.
- **Decision 5 (fixtures).** `fake-agent/fixtures/claude-initialize.json` holds the five real model entries (no account, org, email or token fields; the reply's other keys are not kept); `codex-model-list.json` the seven real entries, split over two pages (`p2`) so the cursor is still exercised (the real reply was one page). The TUI's `claude_catalog()` mirrors the Claude fixture; `codex_catalog()` stays a hand-made catalog in the real shape. Tests updated to the real shapes: the probe test parses the fixture itself; the TUI picker, table, goal form and task edit tests now expect alias entries, `xhigh` and ids for alias-matched rows (two tests that pick "the row's own model" add a full-id Opus entry, `with_opus_full_id`, since the real list has none).
- **Decision 6 (no default effort).** Unchanged behaviour: a Claude row with no effort escalates from the bottom of the ladder (`low`), since Claude reports no default; documented on `above`.
- **New tests** (RED before, GREEN after): `proto` `a_built_in_id_finds_the_alias_entry_by_its_resolved_model`, `the_canonical_id_strips_the_context_tag_and_the_date`, `xhigh_orders_between_high_and_max_and_ultra_is_off_the_ladder`; `daemon` `run/model_roles_tests_real.rs` (the stock table against both real catalogs, alias vs resolved as one model for the reviewer, no `ultra` in escalation, a route on the fallback's alias); `tui` `the_stock_table_against_the_real_claude_catalog_warns_about_nothing`.
- **Seen, not changed.** The built-in research row (`claude:claude-haiku-4-5`, `low`) now finds Haiku, which reports no effort, so every run on the stock table logs `effort 'low' not offered by haiku; using the model's default` (final review M2); the TUI table does not warn about it (`not_offered` ignores a model with no efforts). Long real labels (`Claude · Default (recommended)`) and `Claude · claude-opus-5-5` are wider than the table's 24-column model cell, which grows by design.

### Gate fix (2026-10-09)

The first macOS full-suite gate (at `279e868c`) failed two e2e tests and smoke stage 11t; the controller added rulings B1–B3 and the alias-fix review's C1–C2.

- **`run_e2e_patterns::a_racing_tasks_snapshot_names_its_lanes_and_winner`** (stale test, since M9.8.7a): it expected lane b on Codex, 9.5's peer runtime. Decision 28 / MR §3.1 give lane b the medium row's fallback, else the task's own model, and the built-in medium row has none, so lane b ran on Claude. The test now configures the medium row (Sonnet, `medium`) with a `codex:default` fallback and still asserts lane b on Codex.
- **`run_e2e_large::kinds::e2e_review_goal_reviews_a_range_without_merging`** (stale test, since M9.8.7a): a review task's reviewer takes the `reviewer` row, built in on Codex, so its prompt is the Codex turn message (argv), not Claude's stdin, which was empty. The test reads `codex_messages("reviewer-r1-1")`; the assertions are unchanged.
- **Smoke stage 11t** (stale stage): it toggled `gpt-6-sol` in the old Codex model list and checked `builtin_models = false`, both removed by M9.8.13. It now waits for the role table and the Claude catalog, cycles the orchestrator row's effort to `xhigh` with `e`, saves with `w` (`MODELS_SAVED`), and checks `[models.orchestrator]` with `effort = "xhigh"` in the written file.
- **B1.** The built-in `research` row is Haiku with no effort (Haiku reports none), so a stock install logs no "effort 'low' not offered by haiku" line. Migrated scout keys still give Haiku at `low` (`orchestrator_tests_adapt::adapt_wrong_types_are_problems` now names that row); `scout::tests::area_scout_spec_is_read_only` expects `Effort::DEFAULT`.
- **B2.** "The row's own model keeps the row's effort" (`validate::resolve_route`, `orch::launch::orchestrator_route`) and `model_roles::failed_in` (now taking `&RunModels`) use `RunModels::same_model`. The TUI's goal and task edit forms carry `row_aliases` (`Catalogs::aliases`, set by the app with the row and before each key) and decide with `model_picker::is_row_model` over the new `proto::models::same_model`, so both sides agree that `opus[1m]` is a `claude-opus-5-5` row's model.
- **B3.** The C-b S table cuts a model label wider than its 24-column cell at 23 columns with `…` (`...` in ASCII); the picker is unchanged. The table tests' expected rows changed accordingly.
- **C1.** `proto::models::base_model_id` checks `is_char_boundary` before splitting (a multibyte id panicked) and strips only a real `-YYYYMMDD` date (month 01–12, day 01–31).
- **C2.** `routing::record_worker`'s initial record compares the route with the row by `RunModels::same_model`.
- New tests: `proto` `base_model_id_handles_multibyte_ids_and_strips_only_real_dates`; daemon `model_roles::tests_canon::{picking_the_rows_alias_keeps_the_rows_effort, the_orchestrator_choice_of_the_rows_alias_keeps_the_rows_effort, failed_in_matches_an_alias_and_its_resolved_model, the_initial_record_counts_the_rows_alias_as_the_rows_model}`; TUI `run_edit::tests_models::picking_the_rows_alias_shows_the_rows_effort`, `run_goal::tests::picking_the_rows_alias_shows_the_rows_effort`, and the alignment check in `ui::models_table::tests::the_table_draws_as_the_spec_at_80x24_and_120x40`. Each failed before its fix.
